================================================
FILE: README.md
================================================
# N4 Kanji — Flashcard, Kuis ABC & Seri Buku Irodori

Aplikasi belajar bahasa Jepang berbasis React + TypeScript + Tailwind CSS + Firebase yang menggabungkan **500 kosakata kanji JLPT N4** dan **3 buku lengkap JF Irodori** (*Pemula*, *Dasar 1*, dan *Dasar 2*) dengan sinkronisasi cloud real-time.

## Fitur Utama

- **JLPT N4 (500 Kanji)**
  - Pembagian level *Easy* (210), *Medium* (200), dan *Hard* (90).
  - Flashcard interaktif (pintasan keyboard `Spasi`, `←/→`, `H`, `S`, `A`), mode kartu sulit, acak, dan sembunyikan hafal.
  - Daftar kosakata lengkap dengan pencarian, filter level/status, dan pelafalan audio Jepang (Web Speech API).
  - Kuis ABC 5 model (hingga 2.500 variasi soal dengan analisis morfologi, anti-ambiguitas sinonim, statistik performa, & riwayat jawaban per sesi).
- **Seri Buku JF Irodori (100% Utuh, Bab 1–18)**
  - **Irodori Pemula (A1)**: 1.136 kosakata (`いろどり 入門`).
  - **Irodori Dasar 1 (A2)**: 1.067 kosakata (`いろどり 初級1`).
  - **Irodori Dasar 2 (A2)**: 1.288 kosakata (`いろどり 初級2`).
  - Pemilih bab fleksibel di menu kuis (Semua Bab, 1 bab, atau pilih beberapa bab sekaligus).
  - Tautan langsung ke daftar kosakata resmi PDF masing-masing buku.
- **Sinkronisasi Cloud & Backup Lokal**
  - Autentikasi Firebase (Google, GitHub, Email/Password dengan verifikasi email).
  - Sinkronisasi cloud otomatis & real-time (`onSnapshot`) untuk **JLPT N4** (`/users/{uid}`) dan **seluruh 3 buku Irodori** (`/users/{uid}/irodoriProgress/{bookId}`).
  - Penggabungan progres lokal → cloud secara otomatis saat masuk, serta ekspor/impor backup `.json`.

## Menjalankan Secara Lokal

```bash
npm install
npm run dev
```

Aplikasi berjalan di `http://localhost:3000`.

## Skrip Pengujian & Validasi

```bash
# Type-check TypeScript
npm run lint

# Lint keamanan Firestore Security Rules
npm run lint:rules

# Jalankan seluruh unit test (morfologi, mesin kuis, Irodori, storage, aturan keamanan)
npm test

# Jalankan pengujian Firestore Security Rules langsung di Firebase Emulator (butuh Java 11+ & Firebase CLI)
npm run test:rules:emulator

# Build produksi
npm run build
```

## Deploy ke GitHub Pages

Workflow `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` otomatis menjalankan `npm run lint`, `npm run lint:rules`, `npm test`, lalu membangun bundle produksi dengan `VITE_BASE_PATH=/kanji-n4-plus-plus/` setiap kali push ke branch `main`.

> **Catatan Firebase Auth untuk GitHub Pages:**
> Pastikan domain GitHub Pages Anda (mis. `<username>.github.io`) sudah ditambahkan ke daftar **Authorized domains** di Firebase Console (`Authentication → Settings → Authorized domains`) agar login Google/GitHub berjalan lancar di URL produksi.

================================================
FILE: firestore.emulator.test.ts
================================================
import test from 'node:test';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import {
  assertFails,
  assertSucceeds,
  initializeTestEnvironment,
  type RulesTestEnvironment,
} from '@firebase/rules-unit-testing';
import {
  collection,
  deleteDoc,
  doc,
  getDoc,
  getDocs,
  serverTimestamp,
  setDoc,
  updateDoc,
} from 'firebase/firestore';

const RULES_PATH = path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'firestore.rules');
const EMULATOR_HOST = process.env.FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST;

function parseEmulatorHost(hostSpec: string): { host: string; port: number } {
  const [host, portStr] = hostSpec.split(':');
  return {
    host: host || '127.0.0.1',
    port: Number(portStr) || 8080,
  };
}

/**
 * Real Firestore Security Rules integration suite powered by @firebase/rules-unit-testing.
 *
 * Run locally or in CI with Java 11+ installed:
 *   npm run test:rules:emulator
 *   (executes: firebase emulators:exec --only firestore "npx tsx --test firestore.emulator.test.ts")
 */
test(
  'Firestore Emulator executes real firestore.rules against full-array, XSS, and Dirty Dozen payloads',
  {
    skip: !EMULATOR_HOST
      ? 'FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST is not set; run via `npm run test:rules:emulator` (requires Java 11+ & Firebase Emulator)'
      : false,
  },
  async () => {
    const { host, port } = parseEmulatorHost(EMULATOR_HOST!);
    const rules = fs.readFileSync(RULES_PATH, 'utf8');

    const testEnv: RulesTestEnvironment = await initializeTestEnvironment({
      projectId: 'demo-n4-kanji-rules-test',
      firestore: { host, port, rules },
    });

    try {
      const unauthDb = testEnv.unauthenticatedContext().firestore();
      const unverifiedDb = testEnv
        .authenticatedContext('userA_123', { email_verified: false })
        .firestore();
      const userADb = testEnv
        .authenticatedContext('userA_123', { email_verified: true })
        .firestore();
      const userBDb = testEnv
        .authenticatedContext('userB_456', { email_verified: true })
        .firestore();

      const validCreatePayload = () => ({
        uid: 'userA_123',
        mastered: ['n1', 'n2', 'n500'],
        hard: ['n3', 'n4'],
        createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
        updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
      });

      // 1. Unauthenticated / unverified / cross-user / list attacks MUST fail
      await assertFails(getDoc(doc(unauthDb, 'users', 'userA_123')));
      await assertFails(setDoc(doc(unauthDb, 'users', 'userA_123'), validCreatePayload()));
      await assertFails(setDoc(doc(unverifiedDb, 'users', 'userA_123'), validCreatePayload()));
      await assertFails(getDocs(collection(userADb, 'users')));

      // 2. Full-array validation attacks (index > 0) MUST fail
      await assertFails(
        setDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123'), {
          ...validCreatePayload(),
          mastered: ['n1', 'n2', '<script>alert(1)</script>'],
        })
      );
      await assertFails(
        setDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123'), {
          ...validCreatePayload(),
          mastered: ['n1', 'n2,n3'], // comma smuggling
        })
      );
      await assertFails(
        setDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123'), {
          ...validCreatePayload(),
          mastered: ['n1', 'n2|n3'], // pipe smuggling
        })
      );
      await assertFails(
        setDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123'), {
          ...validCreatePayload(),
          mastered: ['n1', 42], // non-string element
        })
      );
      await assertFails(
        setDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123'), {
          ...validCreatePayload(),
          mastered: ['n1', 'n501'], // out-of-range ID
        })
      );
      await assertFails(
        setDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123'), {
          ...validCreatePayload(),
          mastered: ['n1', 'n1'], // duplicate ID
        })
      );
      await assertFails(
        setDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123'), {
          ...validCreatePayload(),
          mastered: ['n1'],
          hard: ['n1'], // overlap between mastered & hard
        })
      );

      // 3. Shadow field & timestamp spoofing MUST fail
      await assertFails(
        setDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123'), {
          ...validCreatePayload(),
          isAdmin: true,
        })
      );
      await assertFails(
        setDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123'), {
          ...validCreatePayload(),
          createdAt: new Date('2020-01-01T00:00:00Z'),
        })
      );

      // 4. Master Gate: creating Irodori subcollection doc BEFORE parent /users/{uid} exists MUST fail
      const validIrodoriPayload = (bookId: 'pemula' | 'dasar1' | 'dasar2', mastered: string[], hard: string[]) => ({
        uid: 'userA_123',
        bookId,
        mastered,
        hard,
        createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
        updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
      });

      await assertFails(
        setDoc(
          doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123', 'irodoriProgress', 'pemula'),
          validIrodoriPayload('pemula', ['irp1'], ['irp2'])
        )
      );

      // 5. Legitimate create, get, progress update, profile update on /users/{uid} MUST succeed
      await assertSucceeds(setDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123'), validCreatePayload()));
      await assertSucceeds(getDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123')));
      await assertFails(getDoc(doc(userBDb, 'users', 'userA_123')));

      await assertSucceeds(
        updateDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123'), {
          mastered: ['n1', 'n2', 'n3'],
          hard: ['n4'],
          updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
        })
      );

      await assertFails(
        updateDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123'), {
          mastered: ['n1', 'n2', '<script>alert(1)</script>'],
          updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
        })
      );

      await assertFails(
        updateDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123'), {
          avatarUrl: 'javascript:alert(1)',
          updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
        })
      );

      await assertSucceeds(
        updateDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123'), {
          displayName: 'Rasen',
          avatarUrl: 'https://example.com/avatar.png',
          updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
        })
      );

      // 6. Irodori subcollection /users/{uid}/irodoriProgress/{bookId} rules
      await assertFails(getDocs(collection(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123', 'irodoriProgress')));
      await assertFails(
        setDoc(
          doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123', 'irodoriProgress', 'dasar3'),
          {
            uid: 'userA_123',
            bookId: 'dasar3',
            mastered: ['irp1'],
            hard: [],
            createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
            updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
          }
        )
      );
      await assertFails(
        setDoc(
          doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123', 'irodoriProgress', 'pemula'),
          validIrodoriPayload('pemula', ['irp1', 'irp2', '<script>alert(1)</script>'], [])
        )
      );
      await assertFails(
        setDoc(
          doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123', 'irodoriProgress', 'pemula'),
          validIrodoriPayload('pemula', ['irp1', 'ir1'], []) // wrong book prefix
        )
      );

      await assertSucceeds(
        setDoc(
          doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123', 'irodoriProgress', 'pemula'),
          validIrodoriPayload('pemula', ['irp1', 'irp1136'], ['irp2'])
        )
      );
      await assertSucceeds(
        setDoc(
          doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123', 'irodoriProgress', 'dasar1'),
          validIrodoriPayload('dasar1', ['ir1', 'ir1067'], ['ir2'])
        )
      );
      await assertSucceeds(
        setDoc(
          doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123', 'irodoriProgress', 'dasar2'),
          validIrodoriPayload('dasar2', ['ir2_1', 'ir2_1288'], ['ir2_2'])
        )
      );

      await assertSucceeds(getDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123', 'irodoriProgress', 'dasar2')));
      await assertFails(getDoc(doc(userBDb, 'users', 'userA_123', 'irodoriProgress', 'dasar2')));

      await assertSucceeds(
        updateDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123', 'irodoriProgress', 'dasar2'), {
          mastered: ['ir2_1', 'ir2_2', 'ir2_1288'],
          hard: [],
          updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
        })
      );
      await assertSucceeds(
        deleteDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123', 'irodoriProgress', 'dasar2'))
      );

      // 7. QuizHistory subcollection /users/{uid}/quizHistory/{datasetId} rules
      const validQuizToken1 = 'q-1|2026-11-18T12:00:00.000Z|all|random|8|10|80||1:5:n1;2:1:n2:n3';
      const validQuizToken2 = 'q-2|2026-11-18T13:00:00.000Z|all|k2m|9|10|90|1.2.5|1:6:ir1;2:1:ir2:ir3';

      await assertFails(getDocs(collection(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123', 'quizHistory')));
      await assertFails(
        setDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123', 'quizHistory', 'n4'), {
          uid: 'userA_123',
          datasetId: 'n4',
          sessions: [validQuizToken1, '<script>alert(1)</script>'],
          createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
          updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
        })
      );
      await assertFails(
        setDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123', 'quizHistory', 'n4'), {
          uid: 'userA_123',
          datasetId: 'n4',
          sessions: [validQuizToken1, `${validQuizToken2},${validQuizToken2}`],
          createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
          updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
        })
      );
      await assertSucceeds(
        setDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123', 'quizHistory', 'n4'), {
          uid: 'userA_123',
          datasetId: 'n4',
          sessions: [validQuizToken1],
          createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
          updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
        })
      );
      await assertSucceeds(getDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123', 'quizHistory', 'n4')));
      await assertFails(getDoc(doc(userBDb, 'users', 'userA_123', 'quizHistory', 'n4')));
      await assertSucceeds(
        updateDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123', 'quizHistory', 'n4'), {
          sessions: [validQuizToken2, validQuizToken1],
          updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
        })
      );
      await assertSucceeds(
        updateDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123', 'quizHistory', 'n4'), {
          sessions: [],
          updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
        })
      );
      await assertSucceeds(deleteDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123', 'quizHistory', 'n4')));

      await assertFails(deleteDoc(doc(userBDb, 'users', 'userA_123')));
      await assertSucceeds(deleteDoc(doc(userADb, 'users', 'userA_123')));
    } finally {
      await testEnv.cleanup();
    }
  }
);



================================================
FILE: firestore.rules

rules_version = '2';

service cloud.firestore {
  match /databases/{database}/documents {
    // Global Safety Net: Default-deny catch-all
    match /{document=**} {
      allow read, write: if false;
    }

    // --- Reusable Security Primitives ---
    function incoming() {
      return request.resource.data;
    }

    function existing() {
      return resource.data;
    }

    function isSignedIn() {
      return request.auth != null;
    }

    function isVerifiedUser() {
      return isSignedIn() && request.auth.token.email_verified == true;
    }

    function isValidId(id) {
      return id is string && id.size() >= 1 && id.size() <= 128 && id.matches('^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+$');
    }

    function isOwner(userId) {
      return isSignedIn() && request.auth.uid == userId;
    }

    function isValidKanjiId(val) {
      return val is string
        && val.size() >= 2
        && val.size() <= 4
        && val.matches('^n([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-4][0-9]{2}|500)$');
    }

    // Full-array validation without loops:
    // 1. Enforces list type, max size (<= 500), and unique elements via toSet().
    // 2. Validates EVERY element via dual-separator join (',' and '|'):
    //    - Any non-string element fails join() with a runtime deny.
    //    - Because valid IDs contain only [n0-9], checking BOTH ',' and '|'
    //      mathematically prevents separator injection (e.g. ["n1", "n2,n3"] or ["n1", "n2|n3"])
    //      and rejects any malicious/invalid element at ANY index (e.g. index 2 or 499).
    function isValidKanjiList(arr) {
      return arr is list
        && arr.size() <= 500
        && arr.toSet().size() == arr.size()
        && (
          arr.size() == 0
          || (
            isValidKanjiId(arr[0])
            && arr.join(',').size() <= 2500
            && arr.join(',').matches('^n([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-4][0-9]{2}|500)(,n([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-4][0-9]{2}|500))*$')
            && arr.join('|').matches('^n([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-4][0-9]{2}|500)(\\|n([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-4][0-9]{2}|500))*$')
          )
        );
    }

    function isValidIrodoriBookId(bookId) {
      return bookId is string && bookId in ['pemula', 'dasar1', 'dasar2'];
    }

    function isValidQuizDatasetId(datasetId) {
      return datasetId is string && datasetId in ['n4', 'pemula', 'dasar1', 'dasar2'];
    }

    function isValidIrodoriPemulaList(arr) {
      return arr is list
        && arr.size() <= 1136
        && arr.toSet().size() == arr.size()
        && (
          arr.size() == 0
          || (
            arr[0] is string
            && arr[0].matches('^irp([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-9][0-9]{2}|10[0-9]{2}|11[0-2][0-9]|113[0-6])$')
            && arr.join(',').size() <= 9500
            && arr.join(',').matches('^irp([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-9][0-9]{2}|10[0-9]{2}|11[0-2][0-9]|113[0-6])(,irp([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-9][0-9]{2}|10[0-9]{2}|11[0-2][0-9]|113[0-6]))*$')
            && arr.join('|').matches('^irp([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-9][0-9]{2}|10[0-9]{2}|11[0-2][0-9]|113[0-6])(\\|irp([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-9][0-9]{2}|10[0-9]{2}|11[0-2][0-9]|113[0-6]))*$')
          )
        );
    }

    function isValidIrodoriDasar1List(arr) {
      return arr is list
        && arr.size() <= 1067
        && arr.toSet().size() == arr.size()
        && (
          arr.size() == 0
          || (
            arr[0] is string
            && arr[0].matches('^ir([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-9][0-9]{2}|10[0-5][0-9]|106[0-7])$')
            && arr.join(',').size() <= 8000
            && arr.join(',').matches('^ir([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-9][0-9]{2}|10[0-5][0-9]|106[0-7])(,ir([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-9][0-9]{2}|10[0-5][0-9]|106[0-7]))*$')
            && arr.join('|').matches('^ir([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-9][0-9]{2}|10[0-5][0-9]|106[0-7])(\\|ir([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-9][0-9]{2}|10[0-5][0-9]|106[0-7]))*$')
          )
        );
    }

    function isValidIrodoriDasar2List(arr) {
      return arr is list
        && arr.size() <= 1288
        && arr.toSet().size() == arr.size()
        && (
          arr.size() == 0
          || (
            arr[0] is string
            && arr[0].matches('^ir2_([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-9][0-9]{2}|1[0-1][0-9]{2}|12[0-7][0-9]|128[0-8])$')
            && arr.join(',').size() <= 12000
            && arr.join(',').matches('^ir2_([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-9][0-9]{2}|1[0-1][0-9]{2}|12[0-7][0-9]|128[0-8])(,ir2_([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-9][0-9]{2}|1[0-1][0-9]{2}|12[0-7][0-9]|128[0-8]))*$')
            && arr.join('|').matches('^ir2_([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-9][0-9]{2}|1[0-1][0-9]{2}|12[0-7][0-9]|128[0-8])(\\|ir2_([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-9][0-9]{2}|1[0-1][0-9]{2}|12[0-7][0-9]|128[0-8]))*$')
          )
        );
    }

    function isValidIrodoriList(arr, bookId) {
      return (bookId == 'pemula' && isValidIrodoriPemulaList(arr))
        || (bookId == 'dasar1' && isValidIrodoriDasar1List(arr))
        || (bookId == 'dasar2' && isValidIrodoriDasar2List(arr));
    }

    // Full-array validation for compact ID-reference quiz session tokens (<= 15 sessions, <= 160 KB joined):
    // Format: id|completedAt|level|mode|correct|total|score|scopeCode|answersPacked
    // Uses dual-separator join (',' and '~') so every element (indices 0..14) is strictly validated
    // against pure ASCII token syntax with zero free-form text or HTML/XSS risk.
    function isValidQuizSessionToken(val) {
      return val is string
        && val.size() >= 25
        && val.size() <= 12000
        && val.matches('^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+\\|[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,3})?Z\\|(all|easy|medium|hard)\\|(random|random5|k2r|k2m|m2k|r2k|ex2k)\\|[0-9]{1,3}\\|[1-9][0-9]{0,2}\\|(100|[0-9]{1,2})\\|(|all|[1-9][0-9]?(\\.[1-9][0-9]?)*)\\|(|[1-9][0-9]{0,2}:[0-9]:(n|irp|ir|ir2_)[1-9][0-9]{0,3}(:(n|irp|ir|ir2_)[1-9][0-9]{0,3})?(;[1-9][0-9]{0,2}:[0-9]:(n|irp|ir|ir2_)[1-9][0-9]{0,3}(:(n|irp|ir|ir2_)[1-9][0-9]{0,3})?)*)$');
    }

    function isValidQuizHistorySessions(arr) {
      return arr is list
        && arr.size() <= 15
        && arr.toSet().size() == arr.size()
        && (
          arr.size() == 0
          || (
            isValidQuizSessionToken(arr[0])
            && arr.join(',').size() <= 160000
            && arr.join(',').matches('^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+\\|[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,3})?Z\\|(all|easy|medium|hard)\\|(random|random5|k2r|k2m|m2k|r2k|ex2k)\\|[0-9]{1,3}\\|[1-9][0-9]{0,2}\\|(100|[0-9]{1,2})\\|(|all|[1-9][0-9]?(\\.[1-9][0-9]?)*)\\|(|[1-9][0-9]{0,2}:[0-9]:(n|irp|ir|ir2_)[1-9][0-9]{0,3}(:(n|irp|ir|ir2_)[1-9][0-9]{0,3})?(;[1-9][0-9]{0,2}:[0-9]:(n|irp|ir|ir2_)[1-9][0-9]{0,3}(:(n|irp|ir|ir2_)[1-9][0-9]{0,3})?)*)(,[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+\\|[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,3})?Z\\|(all|easy|medium|hard)\\|(random|random5|k2r|k2m|m2k|r2k|ex2k)\\|[0-9]{1,3}\\|[1-9][0-9]{0,2}\\|(100|[0-9]{1,2})\\|(|all|[1-9][0-9]?(\\.[1-9][0-9]?)*)\\|(|[1-9][0-9]{0,2}:[0-9]:(n|irp|ir|ir2_)[1-9][0-9]{0,3}(:(n|irp|ir|ir2_)[1-9][0-9]{0,3})?(;[1-9][0-9]{0,2}:[0-9]:(n|irp|ir|ir2_)[1-9][0-9]{0,3}(:(n|irp|ir|ir2_)[1-9][0-9]{0,3})?)*))*$')
            && arr.join('~').matches('^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+\\|[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,3})?Z\\|(all|easy|medium|hard)\\|(random|random5|k2r|k2m|m2k|r2k|ex2k)\\|[0-9]{1,3}\\|[1-9][0-9]{0,2}\\|(100|[0-9]{1,2})\\|(|all|[1-9][0-9]?(\\.[1-9][0-9]?)*)\\|(|[1-9][0-9]{0,2}:[0-9]:(n|irp|ir|ir2_)[1-9][0-9]{0,3}(:(n|irp|ir|ir2_)[1-9][0-9]{0,3})?(;[1-9][0-9]{0,2}:[0-9]:(n|irp|ir|ir2_)[1-9][0-9]{0,3}(:(n|irp|ir|ir2_)[1-9][0-9]{0,3})?)*)(~[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+\\|[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,3})?Z\\|(all|easy|medium|hard)\\|(random|random5|k2r|k2m|m2k|r2k|ex2k)\\|[0-9]{1,3}\\|[1-9][0-9]{0,2}\\|(100|[0-9]{1,2})\\|(|all|[1-9][0-9]?(\\.[1-9][0-9]?)*)\\|(|[1-9][0-9]{0,2}:[0-9]:(n|irp|ir|ir2_)[1-9][0-9]{0,3}(:(n|irp|ir|ir2_)[1-9][0-9]{0,3})?(;[1-9][0-9]{0,2}:[0-9]:(n|irp|ir|ir2_)[1-9][0-9]{0,3}(:(n|irp|ir|ir2_)[1-9][0-9]{0,3})?)*))*$')
          )
        );
    }

    function isValidDisplayName(data) {
      return !('displayName' in data)
        || (data.displayName is string && data.displayName.size() <= 60);
    }

    function isValidAvatarUrl(data) {
      return !('avatarUrl' in data)
        || (data.avatarUrl is string
          && data.avatarUrl.size() <= 20000
          && (data.avatarUrl.size() == 0
            || data.avatarUrl.matches('^https://.*')
            || data.avatarUrl.matches('^data:image/(jpeg|png|webp);base64,[A-Za-z0-9+/=]+$')));
    }

    // Standalone Validation Blueprint for UserProgress (safe for both create and update)
    function isValidUserProgress(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['uid', 'mastered', 'hard', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly(['uid', 'mastered', 'hard', 'displayName', 'avatarUrl', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.uid is string
        && isValidId(data.uid)
        && data.uid == request.auth.uid
        && isValidKanjiList(data.mastered)
        && isValidKanjiList(data.hard)
        && data.mastered.toSet().intersection(data.hard.toSet()).size() == 0
        && isValidDisplayName(data)
        && isValidAvatarUrl(data)
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp;
    }

    // Standalone Validation Blueprint for IrodoriBookProgress (safe for both create and update)
    function isValidIrodoriProgress(data, bookId) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['uid', 'bookId', 'mastered', 'hard', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly(['uid', 'bookId', 'mastered', 'hard', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.uid is string
        && isValidId(data.uid)
        && data.uid == request.auth.uid
        && isValidIrodoriBookId(data.bookId)
        && data.bookId == bookId
        && isValidIrodoriList(data.mastered, bookId)
        && isValidIrodoriList(data.hard, bookId)
        && data.mastered.toSet().intersection(data.hard.toSet()).size() == 0
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp;
    }

    // Standalone Validation Blueprint for QuizHistoryBucket (safe for both create and update)
    function isValidQuizHistoryBucket(data, datasetId) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['uid', 'datasetId', 'sessions', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly(['uid', 'datasetId', 'sessions', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.uid is string
        && isValidId(data.uid)
        && data.uid == request.auth.uid
        && isValidQuizDatasetId(data.datasetId)
        && data.datasetId == datasetId
        && isValidQuizHistorySessions(data.sessions)
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp;
    }

    // --- /users/{userId} ---
    match /users/{userId} {
      allow get: if isSignedIn()
        && isValidId(userId)
        && isOwner(userId);

      allow list: if false;

      allow create: if isSignedIn()
        && isVerifiedUser()
        && isValidId(userId)
        && isOwner(userId)
        && isValidUserProgress(incoming())
        && incoming().uid == userId
        && incoming().createdAt == request.time
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;

      allow update: if isSignedIn()
        && isVerifiedUser()
        && isValidId(userId)
        && isOwner(userId)
        && isValidUserProgress(incoming())
        && incoming().uid == existing().uid
        && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
        && (
          // Action 1: Progress-only sync
          incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['mastered', 'hard', 'updatedAt']) ||
          // Action 2: Profile-only update
          incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['displayName', 'avatarUrl', 'updatedAt']) ||
          // Action 3: Combined progress + profile update
          incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['mastered', 'hard', 'displayName', 'avatarUrl', 'updatedAt'])
        );

      allow delete: if isSignedIn()
        && isVerifiedUser()
        && isValidId(userId)
        && isOwner(userId);

      // --- /users/{userId}/irodoriProgress/{bookId} ---
      match /irodoriProgress/{bookId} {
        allow get: if isSignedIn()
          && isValidId(userId)
          && isOwner(userId)
          && isValidIrodoriBookId(bookId)
          && exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(userId));

        allow list: if false;

        allow create: if isSignedIn()
          && isVerifiedUser()
          && isValidId(userId)
          && isOwner(userId)
          && isValidIrodoriBookId(bookId)
          && isValidIrodoriProgress(incoming(), bookId)
          && incoming().uid == userId
          && incoming().createdAt == request.time
          && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
          && exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(userId));

        allow update: if isSignedIn()
          && isVerifiedUser()
          && isValidId(userId)
          && isOwner(userId)
          && isValidIrodoriBookId(bookId)
          && isValidIrodoriProgress(incoming(), bookId)
          && incoming().uid == existing().uid
          && incoming().bookId == existing().bookId
          && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
          && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
          && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['mastered', 'hard', 'updatedAt'])
          && exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(userId));

        allow delete: if isSignedIn()
          && isVerifiedUser()
          && isValidId(userId)
          && isOwner(userId)
          && isValidIrodoriBookId(bookId)
          && exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(userId));
      }

      // --- /users/{userId}/quizHistory/{datasetId} ---
      match /quizHistory/{datasetId} {
        allow get: if isSignedIn()
          && isValidId(userId)
          && isOwner(userId)
          && isValidQuizDatasetId(datasetId)
          && exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(userId));

        allow list: if false;

        allow create: if isSignedIn()
          && isVerifiedUser()
          && isValidId(userId)
          && isOwner(userId)
          && isValidQuizDatasetId(datasetId)
          && isValidQuizHistoryBucket(incoming(), datasetId)
          && incoming().uid == userId
          && incoming().createdAt == request.time
          && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
          && exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(userId));

        allow update: if isSignedIn()
          && isVerifiedUser()
          && isValidId(userId)
          && isOwner(userId)
          && isValidQuizDatasetId(datasetId)
          && isValidQuizHistoryBucket(incoming(), datasetId)
          && incoming().uid == existing().uid
          && incoming().datasetId == existing().datasetId
          && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
          && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
          && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['sessions', 'updatedAt'])
          && exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(userId));

        allow delete: if isSignedIn()
          && isVerifiedUser()
          && isValidId(userId)
          && isOwner(userId)
          && isValidQuizDatasetId(datasetId)
          && exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(userId));
      }
    }
  }
}

FILE: firestore.rules.test.ts

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import {
  AVATAR_URL_MAX_LENGTH,
  AVATAR_URL_PATTERN,
  DISPLAY_NAME_MAX_LENGTH,
  IRODORI_BOOK_SYNC_RULES,
  KANJI_ID_PATTERN,
  MAX_KANJI_LIST_SIZE,
  UID_MAX_LENGTH,
  UID_PATTERN,
  buildIrodoriProgressWriteData,
  buildUserProfileWriteData,
  buildUserProgressWriteData,
  isValidAvatarUrl,
  isValidFirebaseUid,
  isValidIrodoriBookId,
  markKanjiHard,
  markKanjiMastered,
  mergeIrodoriProgressSets,
  mergeProgressSets,
  sanitizeDisplayName,
  sanitizeIrodoriProgressLists,
  sanitizeProgressLists,
} from './src/utils/progressSync';
import {
  MAX_CLOUD_QUIZ_JOINED_LENGTH,
  MAX_CLOUD_QUIZ_SESSIONS,
  MAX_CLOUD_QUIZ_SESSION_TOKEN_LENGTH,
  QUIZ_SESSION_COMMA_JOINED_PATTERN,
  QUIZ_SESSION_TILDE_JOINED_PATTERN,
  QUIZ_SESSION_TOKEN_PATTERN,
  isValidQuizDatasetId,
} from './src/utils/quizHistorySync';
import {
  IRODORI_DASAR2_QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY,
  IRODORI_PEMULA_QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY,
  IRODORI_QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY,
  QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY,
} from './src/utils/quizStats';
import {
  HARD_KEY,
  IRODORI_DASAR2_HARD_KEY,
  IRODORI_DASAR2_LS_KEY,
  IRODORI_HARD_KEY,
  IRODORI_LS_KEY,
  IRODORI_PEMULA_HARD_KEY,
  IRODORI_PEMULA_LS_KEY,
  LS_KEY,
  OWNER_UID_KEY,
  clearStoredProgress,
  type StorageLike,
} from './src/utils/storage';

const RULES_PATH = path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'firestore.rules');
const BLUEPRINT_PATH = path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'firebase-blueprint.json');

interface MockAuth {
  uid: string;
  token: { email_verified: boolean };
}

interface MockProgressDoc {
  uid?: unknown;
  mastered?: unknown;
  hard?: unknown;
  displayName?: unknown;
  avatarUrl?: unknown;
  createdAt?: unknown;
  updatedAt?: unknown;
  [extra: string]: unknown;
}

const SERVER_TIME = Symbol('request.time');

function simulateRulesDecision(
  op: 'get' | 'list' | 'create' | 'update' | 'delete',
  auth: MockAuth | null,
  pathUserId: string,
  incoming?: MockProgressDoc,
  existing?: MockProgressDoc
): 'ALLOW' | 'PERMISSION_DENIED' {
  const isSignedIn = auth !== null;
  const isVerifiedUser = isSignedIn && auth.token.email_verified === true;
  const isValidId = (id: unknown): id is string =>
    typeof id === 'string' && id.length >= 1 && id.length <= UID_MAX_LENGTH && UID_PATTERN.test(id);
  const isOwner = isSignedIn && auth.uid === pathUserId;

  if (op === 'list') return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  if (op === 'get') {
    return isSignedIn && isValidId(pathUserId) && isOwner ? 'ALLOW' : 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }
  if (op === 'delete') {
    return isVerifiedUser && isValidId(pathUserId) && isOwner ? 'ALLOW' : 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }

  if (!incoming || !isVerifiedUser || !isValidId(pathUserId) || !isOwner) {
    return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }

  const requiredKeys = new Set(['uid', 'mastered', 'hard', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt']);
  const allowedKeys = new Set([
    'uid',
    'mastered',
    'hard',
    'displayName',
    'avatarUrl',
    'createdAt',
    'updatedAt',
  ]);
  const keys = Object.keys(incoming);
  if (![...requiredKeys].every(k => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(incoming, k))) {
    return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }
  if (!keys.every(k => allowedKeys.has(k))) {
    return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }
  if (!isValidId(incoming.uid) || incoming.uid !== auth.uid) {
    return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }
  if (
    incoming.displayName !== undefined &&
    (typeof incoming.displayName !== 'string' ||
      incoming.displayName.length > DISPLAY_NAME_MAX_LENGTH)
  ) {
    return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }
  if (incoming.avatarUrl !== undefined && !isValidAvatarUrl(incoming.avatarUrl)) {
    return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }

  const commaJoinedPattern =
    /^n([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-4][0-9]{2}|500)(,n([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-4][0-9]{2}|500))*$/;
  const pipeJoinedPattern =
    /^n([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-4][0-9]{2}|500)(\|n([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-4][0-9]{2}|500))*$/;

  // Mirrors firestore.rules isValidKanjiList() verbatim: validates EVERY element via
  // string type enforcement, uniqueness (toSet), and dual-separator join (',' and '|').
  const checkList = (arr: unknown): arr is string[] => {
    if (!Array.isArray(arr) || arr.length > MAX_KANJI_LIST_SIZE) return false;
    if (new Set(arr).size !== arr.length) return false;
    if (arr.length === 0) return true;
    if (!arr.every(el => typeof el === 'string')) return false;
    const first = arr[0];
    if (first.length < 2 || first.length > 4 || !KANJI_ID_PATTERN.test(first)) return false;
    const commaJoined = arr.join(',');
    const pipeJoined = arr.join('|');
    return (
      commaJoined.length <= 2500 &&
      commaJoinedPattern.test(commaJoined) &&
      pipeJoinedPattern.test(pipeJoined)
    );
  };

  if (!checkList(incoming.mastered) || !checkList(incoming.hard)) {
    return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }

  // Mutual exclusivity between mastered and hard (mirrors toSet().intersection(...).size() == 0)
  const hardSet = new Set(incoming.hard);
  if (incoming.mastered.some(id => hardSet.has(id))) {
    return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }

  if (op === 'create') {
    if (
      incoming.uid === pathUserId &&
      incoming.createdAt === SERVER_TIME &&
      incoming.updatedAt === SERVER_TIME
    ) {
      return 'ALLOW';
    }
    return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }

  if (!existing) return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  const changedKeys = keys.filter(k => incoming[k] !== existing[k]);
  const progressKeys = new Set(['mastered', 'hard', 'updatedAt']);
  const profileKeys = new Set(['displayName', 'avatarUrl', 'updatedAt']);
  const combinedKeys = new Set(['mastered', 'hard', 'displayName', 'avatarUrl', 'updatedAt']);
  const validAction =
    changedKeys.every(k => progressKeys.has(k)) ||
    changedKeys.every(k => profileKeys.has(k)) ||
    changedKeys.every(k => combinedKeys.has(k));

  if (!validAction) {
    return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }
  if (
    incoming.uid === existing.uid &&
    incoming.createdAt === existing.createdAt &&
    incoming.updatedAt === SERVER_TIME
  ) {
    return 'ALLOW';
  }
  return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
}

test('firestore.rules file implements all Eight Pillars gates and matches firebase-blueprint.json', () => {
  const rulesText = fs.readFileSync(RULES_PATH, 'utf8');
  const blueprint = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(BLUEPRINT_PATH, 'utf8')) as {
    entities: {
      UserProgress: {
        properties: {
          uid: { maxLength: number };
          displayName: { maxLength: number };
          avatarUrl: { maxLength: number };
        };
      };
    };
    firestore: Record<string, unknown>;
  };

  assert.ok(rulesText.includes("rules_version = '2';"));
  assert.ok(rulesText.includes('match /{document=**}'));
  assert.ok(rulesText.includes('allow read, write: if false;'));
  assert.ok(rulesText.includes('allow list: if false;'));
  assert.ok(rulesText.includes('request.auth.token.email_verified == true'));
  assert.ok(rulesText.includes('isValidUserProgress(incoming())'));
  assert.ok(rulesText.includes(`id.size() <= ${blueprint.entities.UserProgress.properties.uid.maxLength}`));
  assert.ok(
    rulesText.includes(
      `data.displayName.size() <= ${blueprint.entities.UserProgress.properties.displayName.maxLength}`
    )
  );
  assert.ok(
    rulesText.includes(
      `data.avatarUrl.size() <= ${blueprint.entities.UserProgress.properties.avatarUrl.maxLength}`
    )
  );
  assert.ok(rulesText.includes('arr.toSet().size() == arr.size()'));
  assert.ok(rulesText.includes("arr.join(',').size() <= 2500"));
  assert.ok(rulesText.includes("arr.join(',').matches("));
  assert.ok(rulesText.includes("arr.join('|').matches("));
  assert.ok(rulesText.includes('data.mastered.toSet().intersection(data.hard.toSet()).size() == 0'));
  assert.ok(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(blueprint.firestore, '/users/{userId}'));
  assert.ok(
    Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(
      blueprint.firestore,
      '/users/{userId}/irodoriProgress/{bookId}'
    )
  );
  assert.ok(rulesText.includes('match /irodoriProgress/{bookId}'));
  assert.ok(rulesText.includes('isValidIrodoriBookId(bookId)'));
  assert.ok(rulesText.includes('isValidIrodoriPemulaList(arr)'));
  assert.ok(rulesText.includes('isValidIrodoriDasar1List(arr)'));
  assert.ok(rulesText.includes('isValidIrodoriDasar2List(arr)'));
  assert.ok(rulesText.includes('isValidIrodoriProgress(incoming(), bookId)'));
  assert.ok(
    Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(
      blueprint.firestore,
      '/users/{userId}/quizHistory/{datasetId}'
    )
  );
  assert.ok(rulesText.includes('match /quizHistory/{datasetId}'));
  assert.ok(rulesText.includes('isValidQuizDatasetId(datasetId)'));
  assert.ok(rulesText.includes('isValidQuizHistorySessions(data.sessions)'));
  assert.ok(rulesText.includes('isValidQuizHistoryBucket(incoming(), datasetId)'));
  assert.ok(rulesText.includes("arr.join('~').matches("));
  assert.ok(rulesText.includes('exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(userId))'));
});

test('Dirty Dozen adversarial payloads and unsafe profile inputs result in PERMISSION_DENIED', () => {
  const userA: MockAuth = { uid: 'userA_123', token: { email_verified: true } };
  const userB: MockAuth = { uid: 'userB_456', token: { email_verified: true } };
  const unverifiedA: MockAuth = { uid: 'userA_123', token: { email_verified: false } };

  const validExisting: MockProgressDoc = {
    uid: 'userA_123',
    mastered: ['n1'],
    hard: ['n2'],
    createdAt: SERVER_TIME,
    updatedAt: SERVER_TIME,
  };

  // 1-12 Dirty Dozen
  assert.equal(simulateRulesDecision('get', null, 'userA_123'), 'PERMISSION_DENIED');
  assert.equal(
    simulateRulesDecision('create', null, 'userA_123', { ...validExisting }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateRulesDecision('create', unverifiedA, 'userA_123', { ...validExisting }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(simulateRulesDecision('get', userB, 'userA_123'), 'PERMISSION_DENIED');
  assert.equal(simulateRulesDecision('list', userA, 'userA_123'), 'PERMISSION_DENIED');
  assert.equal(
    simulateRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', { ...validExisting, uid: 'userB_456' }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateRulesDecision(
      'create',
      { uid: 'a'.repeat(200), token: { email_verified: true } },
      'a'.repeat(200),
      { ...validExisting, uid: 'a'.repeat(200) }
    ),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', { ...validExisting, isAdmin: true }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateRulesDecision(
      'update',
      userA,
      'userA_123',
      { ...validExisting, role: 'admin' },
      validExisting
    ),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', {
      uid: 'userA_123',
      mastered: ['n1'],
      createdAt: SERVER_TIME,
      updatedAt: SERVER_TIME,
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateRulesDecision(
      'update',
      userA,
      'userA_123',
      { ...validExisting, createdAt: '1999-01-01' },
      validExisting
    ),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', {
      ...validExisting,
      updatedAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z',
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  const oversized = Array.from({ length: 501 }, (_, i) => `n${i + 1}`);
  assert.equal(
    simulateRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', { ...validExisting, mastered: oversized }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', {
      ...validExisting,
      mastered: ['<script>alert(1)</script>'],
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );

  // Full-array hardening: malicious or invalid elements at index > 0 MUST be rejected
  assert.equal(
    simulateRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', {
      ...validExisting,
      mastered: ['n1', 'n2', '<script>alert(1)</script>'],
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateRulesDecision('update', userA, 'userA_123', {
      ...validExisting,
      hard: ['n2', 'n3', '<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>'],
    }, validExisting),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', {
      ...validExisting,
      mastered: ['n1', 'n2,n3'], // comma smuggling attempt
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', {
      ...validExisting,
      mastered: ['n1', 'n2|n3'], // pipe smuggling attempt
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', {
      ...validExisting,
      mastered: ['n1', 123], // non-string element at index 1
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', {
      ...validExisting,
      mastered: ['n1', 'n501'], // out-of-range kanji ID
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', {
      ...validExisting,
      mastered: ['n1', 'n1'], // duplicate kanji ID
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', {
      ...validExisting,
      mastered: ['n1', 'n2'],
      hard: ['n2'], // overlap between mastered & hard
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );

  // Unsafe profile XSS / SVG / oversized payload rejection
  assert.equal(
    simulateRulesDecision(
      'update',
      userA,
      'userA_123',
      { ...validExisting, avatarUrl: 'javascript:alert(1)' },
      validExisting
    ),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateRulesDecision(
      'update',
      userA,
      'userA_123',
      { ...validExisting, avatarUrl: 'data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2Zz4=' },
      validExisting
    ),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateRulesDecision(
      'update',
      userA,
      'userA_123',
      { ...validExisting, displayName: 'x'.repeat(61) },
      validExisting
    ),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );

  // Valid profile & progress updates pass
  assert.equal(
    simulateRulesDecision(
      'update',
      userA,
      'userA_123',
      {
        ...validExisting,
        displayName: 'Rasen',
        avatarUrl: 'data:image/jpeg;base64,/9j/4AAQSkZJRg==',
      },
      validExisting
    ),
    'ALLOW'
  );
});

test('progressSync enforces blueprint bounds, sanitization, profile checks, and merging', () => {
  assert.equal(isValidFirebaseUid('abc_XYZ-123'), true);
  assert.equal(isValidFirebaseUid('bad uid!'), false);
  assert.equal(isValidFirebaseUid('a'.repeat(129)), false);

  assert.equal(sanitizeDisplayName('  Rasen  '), 'Rasen');
  assert.equal(sanitizeDisplayName('a'.repeat(100)).length, 60);
  assert.equal(AVATAR_URL_PATTERN.test('https://example.com/a.png'), true);
  assert.equal(AVATAR_URL_PATTERN.test('data:image/webp;base64,UklGRh4='), true);
  assert.equal(AVATAR_URL_PATTERN.test('javascript:alert(1)'), false);
  assert.equal(isValidAvatarUrl('https://' + 'a'.repeat(AVATAR_URL_MAX_LENGTH)), false);
  assert.throws(() => buildUserProfileWriteData('Rasen', 'http://insecure.example/a.png'));
  assert.deepEqual(buildUserProfileWriteData('  Rasen ', 'https://example.com/a.png'), {
    displayName: 'Rasen',
    avatarUrl: 'https://example.com/a.png',
  });

  const clean = sanitizeProgressLists(
    ['n1', 'n1', 'invalid_id', 42, 'n2'],
    ['n2', 'n3', 'n9999', 'n4']
  );
  assert.deepEqual(clean.mastered, ['n1', 'n2']);
  assert.deepEqual(clean.hard, ['n3', 'n4']);

  const payload = buildUserProgressWriteData('user_01', ['n5'], ['n6', 'n5']);
  assert.deepEqual(payload, { uid: 'user_01', mastered: ['n5'], hard: ['n6'] });

  const merged = mergeProgressSets(
    new Set(['n1', 'n2']),
    new Set(['n3']),
    ['n3', 'n4'],
    ['n1', 'n5']
  );
  assert.deepEqual([...merged.mastered].sort(), ['n1', 'n2', 'n3', 'n4']);
  assert.deepEqual([...merged.hard], ['n5']);
  assert.equal(merged.addedFromSecondary, 3);

  // Mutual exclusivity between Hafal (mastered) and Sulit (hard)
  const afterHard = markKanjiHard(new Set(['n1', 'n2']), new Set(['n3']), 'n1');
  assert.equal(afterHard.nextMastered.has('n1'), false);
  assert.equal(afterHard.nextHard.has('n1'), true);
  assert.equal(afterHard.nextMastered.has('n2'), true);

  const afterMastered = markKanjiMastered(afterHard.nextMastered, afterHard.nextHard, 'n1');
  assert.equal(afterMastered.nextMastered.has('n1'), true);
  assert.equal(afterMastered.nextHard.has('n1'), false);
  assert.equal(afterMastered.isNewMastered, true);
});

interface MockIrodoriDoc {
  uid?: unknown;
  bookId?: unknown;
  mastered?: unknown;
  hard?: unknown;
  createdAt?: unknown;
  updatedAt?: unknown;
  [extra: string]: unknown;
}

function simulateIrodoriRulesDecision(
  op: 'get' | 'list' | 'create' | 'update' | 'delete',
  auth: MockAuth | null,
  pathUserId: string,
  pathBookId: string,
  parentUserDocExists: boolean,
  incoming?: MockIrodoriDoc,
  existing?: MockIrodoriDoc
): 'ALLOW' | 'PERMISSION_DENIED' {
  const isSignedIn = auth !== null;
  const isVerifiedUser = isSignedIn && auth.token.email_verified === true;
  const isValidId = (id: unknown): id is string =>
    typeof id === 'string' && id.length >= 1 && id.length <= UID_MAX_LENGTH && UID_PATTERN.test(id);
  const isOwner = isSignedIn && auth.uid === pathUserId;

  if (op === 'list') return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  if (!isValidId(pathUserId) || !isValidIrodoriBookId(pathBookId)) {
    return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }
  if (op === 'get') {
    return isSignedIn && isOwner && parentUserDocExists ? 'ALLOW' : 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }
  if (op === 'delete') {
    return isVerifiedUser && isOwner && parentUserDocExists ? 'ALLOW' : 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }

  if (!incoming || !isVerifiedUser || !isOwner || !parentUserDocExists) {
    return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }

  const requiredKeys = new Set(['uid', 'bookId', 'mastered', 'hard', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt']);
  const keys = Object.keys(incoming);
  if (
    keys.length !== requiredKeys.size ||
    !keys.every(k => requiredKeys.has(k))
  ) {
    return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }
  if (!isValidId(incoming.uid) || incoming.uid !== auth.uid || incoming.bookId !== pathBookId) {
    return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }

  const rule = IRODORI_BOOK_SYNC_RULES[pathBookId];
  const rawBody = rule.pattern.source.slice(1, -1);
  const commaJoinedPattern = new RegExp(`^${rawBody}(,${rawBody})*$`);
  const pipeJoinedPattern = new RegExp(`^${rawBody}(\\|${rawBody})*$`);

  const checkIrodoriList = (arr: unknown): arr is string[] => {
    if (!Array.isArray(arr) || arr.length > rule.maxSize) return false;
    if (new Set(arr).size !== arr.length) return false;
    if (arr.length === 0) return true;
    if (!arr.every(el => typeof el === 'string')) return false;
    if (!rule.pattern.test(arr[0])) return false;
    const commaJoined = arr.join(',');
    const pipeJoined = arr.join('|');
    return (
      commaJoined.length <= rule.maxJoinedLength &&
      commaJoinedPattern.test(commaJoined) &&
      pipeJoinedPattern.test(pipeJoined)
    );
  };

  if (!checkIrodoriList(incoming.mastered) || !checkIrodoriList(incoming.hard)) {
    return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }

  const hardSet = new Set(incoming.hard);
  if (incoming.mastered.some(id => hardSet.has(id))) {
    return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }

  if (op === 'create') {
    return incoming.uid === pathUserId &&
      incoming.createdAt === SERVER_TIME &&
      incoming.updatedAt === SERVER_TIME
      ? 'ALLOW'
      : 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }

  if (!existing) return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  const changedKeys = keys.filter(k => incoming[k] !== existing[k]);
  const allowedUpdateKeys = new Set(['mastered', 'hard', 'updatedAt']);
  if (
    !changedKeys.every(k => allowedUpdateKeys.has(k)) ||
    incoming.uid !== existing.uid ||
    incoming.bookId !== existing.bookId ||
    incoming.createdAt !== existing.createdAt ||
    incoming.updatedAt !== SERVER_TIME
  ) {
    return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }
  return 'ALLOW';
}

test('Irodori subcollection rules enforce Master Gate, per-book full-array validation, and isolation', () => {
  const userA: MockAuth = { uid: 'userA_123', token: { email_verified: true } };
  const userB: MockAuth = { uid: 'userB_456', token: { email_verified: true } };
  const unverifiedA: MockAuth = { uid: 'userA_123', token: { email_verified: false } };

  const validPemula: MockIrodoriDoc = {
    uid: 'userA_123',
    bookId: 'pemula',
    mastered: ['irp1', 'irp1136'],
    hard: ['irp2'],
    createdAt: SERVER_TIME,
    updatedAt: SERVER_TIME,
  };
  const validDasar1: MockIrodoriDoc = {
    uid: 'userA_123',
    bookId: 'dasar1',
    mastered: ['ir1', 'ir1067'],
    hard: ['ir2'],
    createdAt: SERVER_TIME,
    updatedAt: SERVER_TIME,
  };
  const validDasar2: MockIrodoriDoc = {
    uid: 'userA_123',
    bookId: 'dasar2',
    mastered: ['ir2_1', 'ir2_1288'],
    hard: ['ir2_2'],
    createdAt: SERVER_TIME,
    updatedAt: SERVER_TIME,
  };

  // Master Gate: missing parent /users/{userId} MUST deny
  assert.equal(
    simulateIrodoriRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', 'pemula', false, validPemula),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateIrodoriRulesDecision('get', userA, 'userA_123', 'pemula', false),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );

  // Unauth / unverified / cross-user / list / invalid bookId MUST deny
  assert.equal(
    simulateIrodoriRulesDecision('get', null, 'userA_123', 'pemula', true),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateIrodoriRulesDecision('create', unverifiedA, 'userA_123', 'pemula', true, validPemula),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateIrodoriRulesDecision('get', userB, 'userA_123', 'pemula', true),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateIrodoriRulesDecision('list', userA, 'userA_123', 'pemula', true),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateIrodoriRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', 'dasar3', true, {
      ...validPemula,
      bookId: 'dasar3',
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );

  // Full-array XSS, separator smuggling, cross-book prefix spoofing, and out-of-range IDs MUST deny
  assert.equal(
    simulateIrodoriRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', 'pemula', true, {
      ...validPemula,
      mastered: ['irp1', 'irp2', '<script>alert(1)</script>'],
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateIrodoriRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', 'pemula', true, {
      ...validPemula,
      mastered: ['irp1', 'irp2,irp3'],
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateIrodoriRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', 'pemula', true, {
      ...validPemula,
      mastered: ['irp1', 'ir1'], // dasar1 ID inside pemula
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateIrodoriRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', 'pemula', true, {
      ...validPemula,
      mastered: ['irp1', 'irp1137'], // out of range (> 1136)
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateIrodoriRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', 'dasar1', true, {
      ...validDasar1,
      mastered: ['ir1', 'ir1068'], // out of range (> 1067)
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateIrodoriRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', 'dasar2', true, {
      ...validDasar2,
      mastered: ['ir2_1', 'ir2_1289'], // out of range (> 1288)
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateIrodoriRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', 'dasar2', true, {
      ...validDasar2,
      mastered: ['ir2_1', 'ir2_2'],
      hard: ['ir2_2'], // overlap
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );

  // Legitimate operations across all 3 books MUST succeed
  assert.equal(
    simulateIrodoriRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', 'pemula', true, validPemula),
    'ALLOW'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateIrodoriRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', 'dasar1', true, validDasar1),
    'ALLOW'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateIrodoriRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', 'dasar2', true, validDasar2),
    'ALLOW'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateIrodoriRulesDecision(
      'update',
      userA,
      'userA_123',
      'dasar2',
      true,
      { ...validDasar2, mastered: ['ir2_1', 'ir2_2', 'ir2_1288'], hard: [] },
      validDasar2
    ),
    'ALLOW'
  );
  // Attempting to mutate immutable bookId MUST deny
  assert.equal(
    simulateIrodoriRulesDecision(
      'update',
      userA,
      'userA_123',
      'dasar2',
      true,
      { ...validDasar2, bookId: 'pemula' },
      validDasar2
    ),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
});

test('Irodori progressSync helpers sanitize, build payloads, merge, and clear storage cleanly', () => {
  const cleanPemula = sanitizeIrodoriProgressLists(
    'pemula',
    ['irp1', 'irp1', 'n1', 'ir1', 'irp1137', '<script>', 'irp1136'],
    ['irp1', 'irp2', 'irp9999', 'ir2_1']
  );
  assert.deepEqual(cleanPemula.mastered, ['irp1', 'irp1136']);
  assert.deepEqual(cleanPemula.hard, ['irp2']);

  const payload = buildIrodoriProgressWriteData(
    'user_01',
    'dasar2',
    ['ir2_1', 'ir2_1288'],
    ['ir2_1', 'ir2_5']
  );
  assert.deepEqual(payload, {
    uid: 'user_01',
    bookId: 'dasar2',
    mastered: ['ir2_1', 'ir2_1288'],
    hard: ['ir2_5'],
  });

  const merged = mergeIrodoriProgressSets(
    'dasar1',
    new Set(['ir1', 'ir2']),
    new Set(['ir3']),
    ['ir3', 'ir4', 'irp1'], // irp1 ignored (wrong book)
    ['ir1', 'ir5']
  );
  assert.deepEqual([...merged.mastered].sort(), ['ir1', 'ir2', 'ir3', 'ir4']);
  assert.deepEqual([...merged.hard], ['ir5']);
  assert.equal(merged.addedFromSecondary, 3);

  // Verify clearStoredProgress clears N4, all 3 Irodori books, and all 4 quiz histories on sign-out
  const mem = new Map<string, string>([
    [LS_KEY, '["n1"]'],
    [HARD_KEY, '["n2"]'],
    [IRODORI_PEMULA_LS_KEY, '["irp1"]'],
    [IRODORI_PEMULA_HARD_KEY, '["irp2"]'],
    [IRODORI_LS_KEY, '["ir1"]'],
    [IRODORI_HARD_KEY, '["ir2"]'],
    [IRODORI_DASAR2_LS_KEY, '["ir2_1"]'],
    [IRODORI_DASAR2_HARD_KEY, '["ir2_2"]'],
    [QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY, '[]'],
    [IRODORI_PEMULA_QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY, '[]'],
    [IRODORI_QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY, '[]'],
    [IRODORI_DASAR2_QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY, '[]'],
    [OWNER_UID_KEY, 'user_01'],
  ]);
  const mockStorage: StorageLike = {
    getItem: k => mem.get(k) ?? null,
    setItem: (k, v) => {
      mem.set(k, v);
    },
    removeItem: k => {
      mem.delete(k);
    },
  };
  clearStoredProgress(mockStorage);
  assert.equal(mem.size, 0);
});

interface MockQuizHistoryDoc {
  uid?: unknown;
  datasetId?: unknown;
  sessions?: unknown;
  createdAt?: unknown;
  updatedAt?: unknown;
  [extra: string]: unknown;
}

function simulateQuizHistoryRulesDecision(
  op: 'get' | 'list' | 'create' | 'update' | 'delete',
  auth: MockAuth | null,
  pathUserId: string,
  pathDatasetId: string,
  parentUserDocExists: boolean,
  incoming?: MockQuizHistoryDoc,
  existing?: MockQuizHistoryDoc
): 'ALLOW' | 'PERMISSION_DENIED' {
  const isSignedIn = auth !== null;
  const isVerifiedUser = isSignedIn && auth.token.email_verified === true;
  const isValidId = (id: unknown): id is string =>
    typeof id === 'string' && id.length >= 1 && id.length <= UID_MAX_LENGTH && UID_PATTERN.test(id);
  const isOwner = isSignedIn && auth.uid === pathUserId;

  if (op === 'list') return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  if (!isValidId(pathUserId) || !isValidQuizDatasetId(pathDatasetId)) {
    return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }
  if (op === 'get') {
    return isSignedIn && isOwner && parentUserDocExists ? 'ALLOW' : 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }
  if (op === 'delete') {
    return isVerifiedUser && isOwner && parentUserDocExists ? 'ALLOW' : 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }
  if (!incoming || !isVerifiedUser || !isOwner || !parentUserDocExists) {
    return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }

  const requiredKeys = new Set(['uid', 'datasetId', 'sessions', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt']);
  const keys = Object.keys(incoming);
  if (keys.length !== requiredKeys.size || !keys.every(k => requiredKeys.has(k))) {
    return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }
  if (
    !isValidId(incoming.uid) ||
    incoming.uid !== auth.uid ||
    !isValidQuizDatasetId(incoming.datasetId) ||
    incoming.datasetId !== pathDatasetId
  ) {
    return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }

  const checkSessions = (arr: unknown): arr is string[] => {
    if (!Array.isArray(arr) || arr.length > MAX_CLOUD_QUIZ_SESSIONS) return false;
    if (new Set(arr).size !== arr.length) return false;
    if (arr.length === 0) return true;
    if (!arr.every(el => typeof el === 'string')) return false;
    const first = arr[0];
    if (
      first.length < 25 ||
      first.length > MAX_CLOUD_QUIZ_SESSION_TOKEN_LENGTH ||
      !QUIZ_SESSION_TOKEN_PATTERN.test(first)
    ) {
      return false;
    }
    const commaJoined = arr.join(',');
    const tildeJoined = arr.join('~');
    return (
      commaJoined.length <= MAX_CLOUD_QUIZ_JOINED_LENGTH &&
      QUIZ_SESSION_COMMA_JOINED_PATTERN.test(commaJoined) &&
      QUIZ_SESSION_TILDE_JOINED_PATTERN.test(tildeJoined)
    );
  };

  if (!checkSessions(incoming.sessions)) {
    return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }

  if (op === 'create') {
    return incoming.uid === pathUserId &&
      incoming.createdAt === SERVER_TIME &&
      incoming.updatedAt === SERVER_TIME
      ? 'ALLOW'
      : 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }

  if (!existing) return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  const changedKeys = keys.filter(k => incoming[k] !== existing[k]);
  const allowedUpdateKeys = new Set(['sessions', 'updatedAt']);
  if (
    !changedKeys.every(k => allowedUpdateKeys.has(k)) ||
    incoming.uid !== existing.uid ||
    incoming.datasetId !== existing.datasetId ||
    incoming.createdAt !== existing.createdAt ||
    incoming.updatedAt !== SERVER_TIME
  ) {
    return 'PERMISSION_DENIED';
  }
  return 'ALLOW';
}

test('QuizHistory subcollection rules enforce Master Gate, full-array token validation, and isolation', () => {
  const userA: MockAuth = { uid: 'userA_123', token: { email_verified: true } };
  const userB: MockAuth = { uid: 'userB_456', token: { email_verified: true } };
  const unverifiedA: MockAuth = { uid: 'userA_123', token: { email_verified: false } };

  const validToken1 = 'q-1|2026-11-18T12:00:00.000Z|all|random|8|10|80||1:5:n1;2:1:n2:n3';
  const validToken2 = 'q-2|2026-11-18T13:00:00.000Z|all|k2m|9|10|90|1.2.5|1:6:ir1;2:1:ir2:ir3';

  const validDoc: MockQuizHistoryDoc = {
    uid: 'userA_123',
    datasetId: 'n4',
    sessions: [validToken1],
    createdAt: SERVER_TIME,
    updatedAt: SERVER_TIME,
  };

  // Master Gate: missing parent /users/{uid} MUST deny
  assert.equal(
    simulateQuizHistoryRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', 'n4', false, validDoc),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateQuizHistoryRulesDecision('get', userA, 'userA_123', 'n4', false),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );

  // Unauth / unverified / cross-user / list / invalid datasetId MUST deny
  assert.equal(
    simulateQuizHistoryRulesDecision('get', null, 'userA_123', 'n4', true),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateQuizHistoryRulesDecision('create', unverifiedA, 'userA_123', 'n4', true, validDoc),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateQuizHistoryRulesDecision('get', userB, 'userA_123', 'n4', true),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateQuizHistoryRulesDecision('list', userA, 'userA_123', 'n4', true),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateQuizHistoryRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', 'dasar3', true, {
      ...validDoc,
      datasetId: 'dasar3',
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );

  // Full-array XSS, comma/tilde smuggling, >15 sessions, duplicate sessions, bad score MUST deny
  assert.equal(
    simulateQuizHistoryRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', 'n4', true, {
      ...validDoc,
      sessions: [validToken1, '<script>alert(1)</script>'],
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateQuizHistoryRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', 'n4', true, {
      ...validDoc,
      sessions: [validToken1, `${validToken2},${validToken2}`], // comma smuggling
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateQuizHistoryRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', 'n4', true, {
      ...validDoc,
      sessions: [validToken1, `${validToken2}~${validToken2}`], // tilde smuggling
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateQuizHistoryRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', 'n4', true, {
      ...validDoc,
      sessions: [validToken1, 'q-bad|2026-11-18T12:00:00.000Z|all|k2r|10|10|101||'], // score > 100
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateQuizHistoryRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', 'n4', true, {
      ...validDoc,
      sessions: [validToken1, validToken1], // duplicate
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateQuizHistoryRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', 'n4', true, {
      ...validDoc,
      sessions: Array.from(
        { length: 16 },
        (_, i) => `q-${i}|2026-11-18T12:00:00.000Z|all|k2r|1|1|100||1:5:n1`
      ),
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateQuizHistoryRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', 'n4', true, {
      ...validDoc,
      isAdmin: true, // shadow field
    }),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );

  // Legitimate create, update, clear (empty sessions), get, delete MUST succeed
  assert.equal(
    simulateQuizHistoryRulesDecision('create', userA, 'userA_123', 'n4', true, validDoc),
    'ALLOW'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateQuizHistoryRulesDecision('get', userA, 'userA_123', 'n4', true),
    'ALLOW'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateQuizHistoryRulesDecision(
      'update',
      userA,
      'userA_123',
      'n4',
      true,
      { ...validDoc, sessions: [validToken2, validToken1] },
      validDoc
    ),
    'ALLOW'
  );
  assert.equal(
    simulateQuizHistoryRulesDecision(
      'update',
      userA,
      'userA_123',
      'n4',
      true,
      { ...validDoc, sessions: [] },
      validDoc
    ),
    'ALLOW'
  );
  // Attempting to mutate immutable datasetId MUST deny
  assert.equal(
    simulateQuizHistoryRulesDecision(
      'update',
      userA,
      'userA_123',
      'n4',
      true,
      { ...validDoc, datasetId: 'pemula' },
      validDoc
    ),
    'PERMISSION_DENIED'
  );
});

FILE: security_spec.md
================================================
# Security Specification — N4 Kanji & Irodori Cloud Sync

## 1. Data Invariants

### A. `/users/{userId}` (`UserProgress`)
1. **Strict Ownership**: Every document in `/users/{userId}` belongs strictly to the authenticated user where `request.auth.uid == userId` and `data.uid == request.auth.uid`. Cross-user reads (`get`), queries (`list`), writes (`create`, `update`), and deletions (`delete`) are forbidden.
2. **Verified Identity**: All mutations (`create`, `update`, `delete`) require an authenticated account with `request.auth.token.email_verified == true`.
3. **Closed Schema & Action-Based Updates**: Only `uid`, `mastered`, `hard`, `createdAt`, `updatedAt`, and optional `displayName` (`<= 60` chars) / `avatarUrl` (`<= 20,000` chars, strictly `""`, `https://...`, or compressed `data:image/(jpeg|png|webp);base64,...`) may exist. Unknown/shadow fields (`role`, `isAdmin`, `email`, etc.) are rejected.
4. **Immutable Identity & Server Timestamps**: `uid` and `createdAt` can never change after creation; `createdAt` (on create) and `updatedAt` (on create/update) must always equal `request.time`.
5. **Full-Array Bounded & Disjoint Lists**: `mastered` and `hard` must be unique lists of `<= 500` items, mutually exclusive (`toSet().intersection(...).size() == 0`), where **every element** is validated via dual-separator join (`','` and `'|'`) against `^n([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-4][0-9]{2}|500)$`.
6. **Zero Listing**: `allow list: if false;` prevents collection enumeration.

### B. `/users/{userId}/irodoriProgress/{bookId}` (`IrodoriBookProgress`)
1. **Master Gate**: All operations (`get`, `create`, `update`, `delete`) require the parent `/users/{userId}` document to exist (`exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(userId))`), evaluated after all cheap static checks.
2. **Strict Book Allowlist**: `bookId` path variable and `data.bookId` field must match and be strictly in `['pemula', 'dasar1', 'dasar2']`.
3. **Closed Schema & Immutability**: Only `uid`, `bookId`, `mastered`, `hard`, `createdAt`, and `updatedAt` are permitted. Updates may only mutate `['mastered', 'hard', 'updatedAt']`; `uid`, `bookId`, and `createdAt` are immutable.
4. **Per-Book Full-Array Validation**: Every element of `mastered` and `hard` is validated via uniqueness (`toSet()`), mutual exclusivity (`intersection().size() == 0`), and dual-separator join (`','` and `'|'`) against exact per-book patterns and bounds:
   - `pemula`: `<= 1,136` items (`^irp([1-9]|...|113[0-6])$`, `join(',').size() <= 9500`)
   - `dasar1`: `<= 1,067` items (`^ir([1-9]|...|106[0-7])$`, `join(',').size() <= 8000`)
   - `dasar2`: `<= 1,288` items (`^ir2_([1-9]|...|128[0-8])$`, `join(',').size() <= 12000`)
5. **Zero Listing**: `allow list: if false;` — the client reads and subscribes directly to the 3 known book IDs.

### C. `/users/{userId}/quizHistory/{datasetId}` (`QuizHistoryBucket`)
1. **Master Gate**: All operations (`get`, `create`, `update`, `delete`) require the parent `/users/{userId}` document to exist (`exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(userId))`), evaluated after all cheap static checks.
2. **Strict Dataset Allowlist**: `datasetId` path variable and `data.datasetId` field must match and be strictly in `['n4', 'pemula', 'dasar1', 'dasar2']`.
3. **Closed Schema & Immutability**: Only `uid`, `datasetId`, `sessions`, `createdAt`, and `updatedAt` are permitted. Updates may only mutate `['sessions', 'updatedAt']`; `uid`, `datasetId`, and `createdAt` are immutable.
4. **Compact ID-Reference Full-Array Validation**: `sessions` must be a unique list of `<= 15` compact ASCII session tokens (`join(',').size() <= 160000`), where **every element** (indices `0..14`) is validated via dual-separator join (`','` and `'~'`) against the strict token grammar (`id|completedAt|level|mode|correct|total|score|scopeCode|answersPacked`). Zero free-form text, HTML, or JSON strings are stored; full Kanji/Irodori labels and conjugated sentence forms are hydrated client-side from static bundled dictionaries.
5. **Zero Listing**: `allow list: if false;` — the client lazy-reads only the specific active dataset document on demand (`1 getDoc` cached per session).

## 2. Adversarial Payloads & Access Attempts Tested (`firestore.rules.test.ts` & `firestore.emulator.test.ts`)
1. **Unauthenticated Read/Write**: `auth = null` attempting `get` or `set` on `/users/userA`, `/users/userA/irodoriProgress/pemula`, or `/users/userA/quizHistory/n4` -> `PERMISSION_DENIED`.
2. **Unverified Email Mutation**: `email_verified: false` attempting `create`/`update` -> `PERMISSION_DENIED`.
3. **Cross-User Read/Write/Delete**: `userB` attempting access to `userA` documents -> `PERMISSION_DENIED`.
4. **Collection Scraping (`list`)**: Listing `/users`, `/users/{userId}/irodoriProgress`, or `/users/{userId}/quizHistory` -> `PERMISSION_DENIED`.
5. **Identity & Dataset Spoofing**: Mismatched `uid`, `bookId`, or `datasetId`, or invalid `datasetId` (e.g. `dasar3`) -> `PERMISSION_DENIED`.
6. **Master Gate Bypass**: Creating/reading `/users/{uid}/irodoriProgress/{bookId}` or `/users/{uid}/quizHistory/{datasetId}` before `/users/{uid}` exists -> `PERMISSION_DENIED`.
7. **Shadow / Ghost Field Injection**: Adding `isAdmin: true` or `role: 'admin'` -> `PERMISSION_DENIED`.
8. **Immortal Field Tampering & Forged Timestamps**: Mutating `uid`, `bookId`, `datasetId`, `createdAt`, or supplying client timestamps -> `PERMISSION_DENIED`.
9. **Full-Array XSS & Separator Smuggling (Index > 0)**: Payloads such as `['n1', 'n2', '<script>alert(1)</script>']`, `['irp1', 'irp2,irp3']`, `['n1', 'n2|n3']`, comma/tilde smuggling inside `sessions` at index > 0, or non-string elements -> `PERMISSION_DENIED`.
10. **Cross-Book Prefix Spoofing & Out-of-Range IDs / Scores**: Writing `ir1` inside `pemula`, `irp1137`, `ir1068`, `ir2_1289`, `n501`, `score > 100`, or `> 15` quiz sessions -> `PERMISSION_DENIED`.
11. **Duplicates & Mastered/Hard Overlap**: Duplicate IDs/sessions or identical IDs in both `mastered` and `hard` -> `PERMISSION_DENIED`.
12. **Unsafe Profile Inputs**: `javascript:` or `data:image/svg+xml` in `avatarUrl`, or `displayName > 60` chars -> `PERMISSION_DENIED`.
