FILE: vite.config.ts
================================================
import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite';
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import {defineConfig} from 'vite';

export default defineConfig(() => {
  return {
    base: process.env.VITE_BASE_PATH || './',
    plugins: [
      react(),
      tailwindcss(),
      // Copies src/assets into dist/ so root-relative URLs like
      // "/src/assets/images/..." (e.g. generate_image output) still resolve
      // in production builds. Do not remove.
      {
        name: 'copy-unbundled-src-assets',
        apply: 'build',
        closeBundle(error?: Error): void {
          if (error) {
            return;
          }
          const distDir = path.resolve(__dirname, 'dist');
          const srcAssets = path.resolve(__dirname, 'src/assets');
          const distAssets = path.resolve(distDir, 'src/assets');
          if (fs.existsSync(distDir) && fs.existsSync(srcAssets)) {
            fs.cpSync(srcAssets, distAssets, {recursive: true, force: true});
          }
        },
      },
    ],
    resolve: {
      alias: {
        '@': path.resolve(__dirname, '.'),
      },
    },
    server: {
      // HMR is disabled in AI Studio via DISABLE_HMR env var.
      // Do not modify—file watching is disabled to prevent flickering during agent edits.
      hmr: process.env.DISABLE_HMR !== 'true',
      // Disable file watching when DISABLE_HMR is true to save CPU during agent edits.
      watch: process.env.DISABLE_HMR === 'true' ? null : {},
    },
  };
});



================================================
FILE: .env.example
================================================
# GEMINI_API_KEY: Required for Gemini AI API calls.
# AI Studio automatically injects this at runtime from user secrets.
# Users configure this via the Secrets panel in the AI Studio UI.
GEMINI_API_KEY="MY_GEMINI_API_KEY"

# APP_URL: The URL where this applet is hosted.
# AI Studio automatically injects this at runtime with the Cloud Run service URL.
# Used for self-referential links, OAuth callbacks, and API endpoints.
APP_URL="MY_APP_URL"



================================================
FILE: src/App.tsx
================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { AppHeader } from './components/AppHeader';
import { AuthModal } from './components/AuthModal';
import { FlashcardPanel } from './components/FlashcardPanel';
import { IrodoriBookMenu } from './components/IrodoriBookMenu';
import { IrodoriEmptyState } from './components/IrodoriEmptyState';
import { KanjiListPanel } from './components/KanjiListPanel';
import { ProfileModal } from './components/ProfileModal';
import { QuizPanel } from './components/QuizPanel';
import { RestoreModal } from './components/RestoreModal';
import { Sidebar } from './components/Sidebar';
import { ToastBanner } from './components/ToastBanner';
import { IRODORI_BOOKS } from './data/irodoriBooks';
import { KANJI, VALID_KANJI_IDS } from './data/kanjiData';
import { useAuthActions } from './hooks/useAuthActions';
import { useBackupRestore } from './hooks/useBackupRestore';
import { useCloudProgressSync } from './hooks/useCloudProgressSync';
import { useTheme } from './hooks/useTheme';
import { useToast } from './hooks/useToast';
import type {
  AppDatasetMode,
  AppTab,
  IrodoriBookId,
  IrodoriChapterFilter,
  KanjiEntry,
  LevelFilter,
} from './types/kanji';
import { markKanjiHard, markKanjiMastered } from './utils/progressSync';
import { getPoolIndices, shuffled } from './utils/quizEngine';
import { speakJP } from './utils/tts';

export default function App() {
  const [datasetMode, setDatasetMode] = useState<AppDatasetMode>('n4');
  const [irodoriBookId, setIrodoriBookId] = useState<IrodoriBookId>('dasar1');
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<AppTab>('card');
  const [levelFilter, setLevelFilter] = useState<LevelFilter>('all');
  const [chapterFilter, setChapterFilter] = useState<IrodoriChapterFilter>('all');

  const [hardMode, setHardMode] = useState(false);
  const [shuffleOn, setShuffleOn] = useState(false);
  const [hideMastered, setHideMastered] = useState(false);

  const [order, setOrder] = useState<number[]>(() => getPoolIndices('all', KANJI));
  const [pos, setPos] = useState(0);
  const [flipped, setFlipped] = useState(false);
  const [sessionHard, setSessionHard] = useState<Set<number>>(() => new Set());
  const [sessionNewMaster, setSessionNewMaster] = useState(0);

  const { toast, showToast, dismissToast } = useToast();
  const { isDark, toggleTheme } = useTheme();

  const {
    user,
    authVersion,
    authReady,
    authBusy,
    authModalOpen,
    setAuthModalOpen,
    authSwitchMode,
    authErrorText,
    authInfoText,
    authConsoleHint,
    justSignedInRef,
    bumpAuthVersion,
    clearAuthNotices,
    handleOpenAuthModal,
    handleGoogleLogin,
    handleGithubLogin,
    handleEmailSignIn,
    handleEmailSignUp,
    handleEmailReset,
    handleResendVerification,
    handleRefreshVerification,
    executeSignOut,
  } = useAuthActions({ showToast });

  const {
    mastered,
    hardSet,
    irodoriProgress,
    quizHistories,
    activeQuizDatasetId,
    syncing,
    quizHistorySyncing,
    fatalError,
    customDisplayName,
    customAvatarUrl,
    profileModalOpen,
    setProfileModalOpen,
    profileSaving,
    applyProgressChange,
    handleRecordQuizSession,
    handleClearQuizHistory,
    handleManualSync,
    handleSaveProfile,
    resetCloudAndLocalProgress,
  } = useCloudProgressSync({
    user,
    authReady,
    authVersion,
    justSignedInRef,
    activeTab,
    datasetMode,
    irodoriBookId,
    bumpAuthVersion,
    showToast,
  });

  if (fatalError) {
    throw fatalError;
  }

  const activeIrodoriBook = IRODORI_BOOKS[irodoriBookId];
  const activeIrodoriProgress = irodoriProgress[irodoriBookId];
  const activeDataset: readonly KanjiEntry[] =
    datasetMode === 'irodori' ? activeIrodoriBook.data : KANJI;
  const activeMastered =
    datasetMode === 'irodori' ? activeIrodoriProgress.mastered : mastered;
  const activeHardSet =
    datasetMode === 'irodori' ? activeIrodoriProgress.hard : hardSet;
  const activeValidIds =
    datasetMode === 'irodori' ? activeIrodoriBook.validIds : VALID_KANJI_IDS;
  const activeScopeLabel =
    datasetMode === 'irodori'
      ? chapterFilter === 'all'
        ? 'Semua Bab (1–18)'
        : activeIrodoriBook.chapters[chapterFilter].label
      : levelFilter;

  const computeCardPoolFor = useCallback(
    (
      mode: AppDatasetMode,
      bookId: IrodoriBookId,
      lv: LevelFilter,
      ch: IrodoriChapterFilter,
      hideM: boolean,
      onlyH: boolean,
      mSet: Set<string>,
      hSet: Set<string>
    ) => {
      const ds = mode === 'irodori' ? IRODORI_BOOKS[bookId].data : KANJI;
      const pool = getPoolIndices(lv, ds, mode === 'irodori' ? ch : undefined);
      return pool.filter(
        i => (!hideM || !mSet.has(ds[i].id)) && (!onlyH || hSet.has(ds[i].id))
      );
    },
    []
  );

  const computeCardPool = useCallback(
    (
      lv: LevelFilter,
      hideM: boolean,
      onlyH: boolean,
      mSet: Set<string>,
      hSet: Set<string>
    ) =>
      computeCardPoolFor(
        datasetMode,
        irodoriBookId,
        lv,
        chapterFilter,
        hideM,
        onlyH,
        mSet,
        hSet
      ),
    [computeCardPoolFor, datasetMode, irodoriBookId, chapterFilter]
  );

  const rebuildOrder = useCallback((indices: number[], shouldShuffle: boolean) => {
    setOrder(shouldShuffle ? shuffled(indices) : indices.slice());
    setPos(0);
    setFlipped(false);
    setSessionHard(new Set());
    setSessionNewMaster(0);
  }, []);

  const onRestoreCommitted = useCallback(
    (nextMastered: Set<string>, nextHard: Set<string>) => {
      const nextPool = computeCardPool(levelFilter, hideMastered, hardMode, nextMastered, nextHard);
      rebuildOrder(nextPool, shuffleOn);
    },
    [computeCardPool, levelFilter, hideMastered, hardMode, rebuildOrder, shuffleOn]
  );

  const {
    pendingRestore,
    clearPendingRestore,
    restoreBtnRef,
    fileInputRef,
    restorePreviews,
    handleExport,
    handleTriggerImport,
    handleFileSelected,
    handleCloseRestoreModal,
    handleConfirmRestore,
  } = useBackupRestore({
    datasetMode,
    irodoriBookId,
    activeMastered,
    activeHardSet,
    activeValidIds,
    applyProgressChange,
    onRestoreCommitted,
    showToast,
    dismissToast,
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (user) {
      clearPendingRestore();
    }
  }, [user, clearPendingRestore]);

  const levelIndices = useMemo(
    () =>
      getPoolIndices(
        levelFilter,
        activeDataset,
        datasetMode === 'irodori' ? chapterFilter : undefined
      ),
    [levelFilter, activeDataset, datasetMode, chapterFilter]
  );
  const levelMasteredCount = useMemo(
    () => levelIndices.filter(i => activeMastered.has(activeDataset[i].id)).length,
    [levelIndices, activeMastered, activeDataset]
  );
  const levelHardCount = useMemo(
    () => levelIndices.filter(i => activeHardSet.has(activeDataset[i].id)).length,
    [levelIndices, activeHardSet, activeDataset]
  );

  const handleSignOut = useCallback(() => {
    void executeSignOut(() => {
      const { emptyM, emptyH } = resetCloudAndLocalProgress();
      const nextPool = computeCardPool(levelFilter, hideMastered, hardMode, emptyM, emptyH);
      rebuildOrder(nextPool, shuffleOn);
    });
  }, [
    executeSignOut,
    resetCloudAndLocalProgress,
    computeCardPool,
    levelFilter,
    hideMastered,
    hardMode,
    rebuildOrder,
    shuffleOn,
  ]);

  const handleToggleDatasetMode = useCallback(() => {
    const nextMode: AppDatasetMode = datasetMode === 'n4' ? 'irodori' : 'n4';
    setDatasetMode(nextMode);
    const nextM = nextMode === 'irodori' ? activeIrodoriProgress.mastered : mastered;
    const nextH = nextMode === 'irodori' ? activeIrodoriProgress.hard : hardSet;
    const nextPool = computeCardPoolFor(
      nextMode,
      irodoriBookId,
      levelFilter,
      chapterFilter,
      hideMastered,
      hardMode,
      nextM,
      nextH
    );
    rebuildOrder(nextPool, shuffleOn);
  }, [
    datasetMode,
    activeIrodoriProgress,
    mastered,
    hardSet,
    computeCardPoolFor,
    irodoriBookId,
    levelFilter,
    chapterFilter,
    hideMastered,
    hardMode,
    rebuildOrder,
    shuffleOn,
  ]);

  const handleSelectIrodoriBook = useCallback(
    (nextBookId: IrodoriBookId) => {
      if (nextBookId === irodoriBookId && datasetMode === 'irodori') return;
      setIrodoriBookId(nextBookId);
      setChapterFilter('all');
      const nextBookProgress = irodoriProgress[nextBookId];
      const nextPool = computeCardPoolFor(
        'irodori',
        nextBookId,
        levelFilter,
        'all',
        hideMastered,
        hardMode,
        nextBookProgress.mastered,
        nextBookProgress.hard
      );
      rebuildOrder(nextPool, shuffleOn);
    },
    [
      irodoriBookId,
      datasetMode,
      irodoriProgress,
      computeCardPoolFor,
      levelFilter,
      hideMastered,
      hardMode,
      rebuildOrder,
      shuffleOn,
    ]
  );

  const handleSelectLevel = useCallback(
    (nextLevel: LevelFilter) => {
      if (nextLevel === levelFilter) return;
      setLevelFilter(nextLevel);
      const nextPool = computeCardPoolFor(
        'n4',
        irodoriBookId,
        nextLevel,
        chapterFilter,
        hideMastered,
        hardMode,
        mastered,
        hardSet
      );
      rebuildOrder(nextPool, shuffleOn);
    },
    [
      levelFilter,
      computeCardPoolFor,
      irodoriBookId,
      chapterFilter,
      hideMastered,
      hardMode,
      mastered,
      hardSet,
      rebuildOrder,
      shuffleOn,
    ]
  );

  const handleSelectChapter = useCallback(
    (nextChapter: IrodoriChapterFilter) => {
      if (nextChapter === chapterFilter) return;
      setChapterFilter(nextChapter);
      const nextPool = computeCardPoolFor(
        'irodori',
        irodoriBookId,
        levelFilter,
        nextChapter,
        hideMastered,
        hardMode,
        activeIrodoriProgress.mastered,
        activeIrodoriProgress.hard
      );
      rebuildOrder(nextPool, shuffleOn);
    },
    [
      chapterFilter,
      computeCardPoolFor,
      irodoriBookId,
      levelFilter,
      hideMastered,
      hardMode,
      activeIrodoriProgress,
      rebuildOrder,
      shuffleOn,
    ]
  );

  const handleMarkMastered = useCallback(() => {
    const idx = order[pos];
    if (idx === undefined) return;
    const item = activeDataset[idx];
    const { nextMastered, nextHard, isNewMastered } = markKanjiMastered(
      activeMastered,
      activeHardSet,
      item.id
    );

    if (!applyProgressChange(nextMastered, nextHard)) return;
    if (isNewMastered) setSessionNewMaster(v => v + 1);
    setPos(p => p + 1);
    setFlipped(false);
  }, [order, pos, activeDataset, activeMastered, activeHardSet, applyProgressChange]);

  const handleMarkHard = useCallback(() => {
    const idx = order[pos];
    if (idx === undefined) return;
    const item = activeDataset[idx];
    const { nextMastered, nextHard } = markKanjiHard(activeMastered, activeHardSet, item.id);

    if (!applyProgressChange(nextMastered, nextHard)) return;
    setSessionHard(prev => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      next.add(idx);
      return next;
    });
    setPos(p => p + 1);
    setFlipped(false);
  }, [order, pos, activeDataset, activeMastered, activeHardSet, applyProgressChange]);

  const handleForgetCurrent = useCallback(() => {
    const idx = order[pos];
    if (idx === undefined) return;
    const item = activeDataset[idx];
    if (!activeMastered.has(item.id)) return;
    const nextM = new Set(activeMastered);
    nextM.delete(item.id);
    applyProgressChange(nextM, new Set(activeHardSet));
  }, [order, pos, activeDataset, activeMastered, activeHardSet, applyProgressChange]);

  const handleResetAllLevelMastered = useCallback(() => {
    const nextM = new Set(activeMastered);
    levelIndices.forEach(i => nextM.delete(activeDataset[i].id));
    if (applyProgressChange(nextM, new Set(activeHardSet))) {
      showToast(
        'info',
        'Hafalan Direset',
        `Data hafalan untuk ${activeScopeLabel} telah dihapus.`
      );
    }
  }, [
    activeMastered,
    activeHardSet,
    levelIndices,
    activeDataset,
    applyProgressChange,
    showToast,
    activeScopeLabel,
  ]);

  const handleUnhardCurrent = useCallback(() => {
    const idx = order[pos];
    if (idx === undefined) return;
    const item = activeDataset[idx];
    if (!activeHardSet.has(item.id)) return;
    const nextH = new Set(activeHardSet);
    nextH.delete(item.id);
    if (!applyProgressChange(new Set(activeMastered), nextH)) return;
    setSessionHard(prev => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      next.delete(idx);
      return next;
    });
  }, [order, pos, activeDataset, activeMastered, activeHardSet, applyProgressChange]);

  const handleClearLevelHard = useCallback(() => {
    const nextH = new Set(activeHardSet);
    levelIndices.forEach(i => nextH.delete(activeDataset[i].id));
    if (applyProgressChange(new Set(activeMastered), nextH)) {
      setSessionHard(new Set());
      showToast(
        'info',
        'Daftar Sulit Dihapus',
        `Daftar sulit untuk ${activeScopeLabel} telah dikosongkan.`
      );
    }
  }, [
    activeHardSet,
    activeMastered,
    levelIndices,
    activeDataset,
    applyProgressChange,
    showToast,
    activeScopeLabel,
  ]);

  const handleToggleHideMastered = useCallback(() => {
    const nextHide = !hideMastered;
    setHideMastered(nextHide);
    const nextPool = computeCardPool(
      levelFilter,
      nextHide,
      hardMode,
      activeMastered,
      activeHardSet
    );
    rebuildOrder(nextPool, shuffleOn);
  }, [
    hideMastered,
    computeCardPool,
    levelFilter,
    hardMode,
    activeMastered,
    activeHardSet,
    rebuildOrder,
    shuffleOn,
  ]);

  const handleToggleShuffle = useCallback(() => {
    const nextShuffle = !shuffleOn;
    setShuffleOn(nextShuffle);
    const nextPool = computeCardPool(
      levelFilter,
      hideMastered,
      hardMode,
      activeMastered,
      activeHardSet
    );
    rebuildOrder(nextPool, nextShuffle);
  }, [
    shuffleOn,
    computeCardPool,
    levelFilter,
    hideMastered,
    hardMode,
    activeMastered,
    activeHardSet,
    rebuildOrder,
  ]);

  const handleToggleHardMode = useCallback(() => {
    const nextHardMode = !hardMode;
    setHardMode(nextHardMode);
    const nextPool = computeCardPool(
      levelFilter,
      hideMastered,
      nextHardMode,
      activeMastered,
      activeHardSet
    );
    rebuildOrder(nextPool, shuffleOn);
  }, [
    hardMode,
    computeCardPool,
    levelFilter,
    hideMastered,
    activeMastered,
    activeHardSet,
    rebuildOrder,
    shuffleOn,
  ]);

  const handleRestartSession = useCallback(() => {
    const nextPool = computeCardPool(
      levelFilter,
      hideMastered,
      hardMode,
      activeMastered,
      activeHardSet
    );
    rebuildOrder(nextPool, shuffleOn);
  }, [
    computeCardPool,
    levelFilter,
    hideMastered,
    hardMode,
    activeMastered,
    activeHardSet,
    rebuildOrder,
    shuffleOn,
  ]);

  const handleRepeatSessionHard = useCallback(() => {
    rebuildOrder([...sessionHard], shuffleOn);
  }, [sessionHard, rebuildOrder, shuffleOn]);

  const anyModalOpen = pendingRestore !== null || authModalOpen || profileModalOpen;

  // Global keyboard shortcuts for Flashcard mode (disabled when any modal is open)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeTab !== 'card' || anyModalOpen) return;

    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey) return;
      const target = e.target as HTMLElement | null;
      const tag = target?.tagName;
      if (tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA' || tag === 'SELECT') return;
      if (pos >= order.length) return;

      if (e.key === ' ') {
        if (target?.closest('#flashcard') || target?.tagName === 'BUTTON') return;
        e.preventDefault();
        setFlipped(f => !f);
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
        if (pos > 0) {
          e.preventDefault();
          setPos(p => p - 1);
          setFlipped(false);
        }
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
        e.preventDefault();
        setPos(p => p + 1);
        setFlipped(false);
      } else if (e.key === 'h' || e.key === 'H') {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleMarkMastered();
      } else if (e.key === 's' || e.key === 'S') {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleMarkHard();
      } else if (e.key === 'a' || e.key === 'A') {
        e.preventDefault();
        const item = activeDataset[order[pos]];
        if (item) speakJP(item.r);
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [activeTab, anyModalOpen, pos, order, activeDataset, handleMarkMastered, handleMarkHard]);

  const effectiveDisplayName =
    customDisplayName || user?.displayName || user?.email?.split('@')[0] || '';
  const effectiveAvatarUrl = customAvatarUrl || user?.photoURL || '';
  const isIrodoriEmptyBook =
    datasetMode === 'irodori' && activeIrodoriBook.data.length === 0;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 transition-colors">
      <div aria-hidden={anyModalOpen} className="flex-1 flex flex-col">
        <AppHeader
          datasetMode={datasetMode}
          irodoriBookTitle={activeIrodoriBook.label}
          onToggleDatasetMode={handleToggleDatasetMode}
          isDark={isDark}
          onToggleTheme={toggleTheme}
          masteredCount={levelMasteredCount}
          totalCount={levelIndices.length}
          restoreBtnRef={restoreBtnRef}
          fileInputRef={fileInputRef}
          user={user}
          displayName={effectiveDisplayName}
          avatarUrl={effectiveAvatarUrl}
          authReady={authReady}
          authBusy={authBusy}
          syncing={syncing}
          onOpenAuthModal={handleOpenAuthModal}
          onOpenProfileModal={() => setProfileModalOpen(true)}
          onManualSync={() => void handleManualSync()}
          onResendVerification={() => void handleResendVerification()}
          onRefreshVerification={() => void handleRefreshVerification()}
          onSignOut={handleSignOut}
          onExport={handleExport}
          onTriggerImport={handleTriggerImport}
          onFileSelected={handleFileSelected}
        />

        <main className="flex-1 w-full max-w-6xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-6 lg:py-8">
          {datasetMode === 'irodori' ? (
            <IrodoriBookMenu
              selectedBookId={irodoriBookId}
              onSelectBook={handleSelectIrodoriBook}
            />
          ) : null}

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-[260px_minmax(0,1fr)] gap-6 lg:gap-8 items-start">
            <Sidebar
              datasetMode={datasetMode}
              irodoriBook={activeIrodoriBook}
              activeTab={activeTab}
              levelFilter={levelFilter}
              chapterFilter={chapterFilter}
              onSelectTab={setActiveTab}
              onSelectLevel={handleSelectLevel}
              onSelectChapter={handleSelectChapter}
            />

            <div className="min-w-0">
              {isIrodoriEmptyBook ? (
                <IrodoriEmptyState
                  book={activeIrodoriBook}
                  onSwitchToDasar1={() => handleSelectIrodoriBook('dasar1')}
                />
              ) : null}

              {!isIrodoriEmptyBook && activeTab === 'card' ? (
                <FlashcardPanel
                  datasetMode={datasetMode}
                  dataset={activeDataset}
                  scopeLabel={activeScopeLabel}
                  levelFilter={levelFilter}
                  order={order}
                  pos={pos}
                  flipped={flipped}
                  hideMastered={hideMastered}
                  shuffleOn={shuffleOn}
                  hardMode={hardMode}
                  mastered={activeMastered}
                  hardSet={activeHardSet}
                  levelHardCount={levelHardCount}
                  levelMasteredCount={levelMasteredCount}
                  sessionHardCount={sessionHard.size}
                  sessionNewMaster={sessionNewMaster}
                  onFlip={() => setFlipped(f => !f)}
                  onPrev={() => {
                    if (pos > 0) {
                      setPos(p => p - 1);
                      setFlipped(false);
                    }
                  }}
                  onNext={() => {
                    setPos(p => p + 1);
                    setFlipped(false);
                  }}
                  onMarkMastered={handleMarkMastered}
                  onMarkHard={handleMarkHard}
                  onForgetCurrent={handleForgetCurrent}
                  onResetAllLevelMastered={handleResetAllLevelMastered}
                  onUnhardCurrent={handleUnhardCurrent}
                  onClearLevelHard={handleClearLevelHard}
                  onToggleHideMastered={handleToggleHideMastered}
                  onToggleShuffle={handleToggleShuffle}
                  onToggleHardMode={handleToggleHardMode}
                  onRestartSession={handleRestartSession}
                  onRepeatSessionHard={handleRepeatSessionHard}
                />
              ) : null}

              {!isIrodoriEmptyBook && activeTab === 'list' ? (
                <KanjiListPanel
                  datasetMode={datasetMode}
                  irodoriBook={activeIrodoriBook}
                  levelFilter={levelFilter}
                  chapterFilter={chapterFilter}
                  mastered={activeMastered}
                  hardSet={activeHardSet}
                />
              ) : null}

              {!isIrodoriEmptyBook && activeTab === 'quiz' ? (
                <QuizPanel
                  datasetMode={datasetMode}
                  irodoriBook={activeIrodoriBook}
                  levelFilter={levelFilter}
                  chapterFilter={chapterFilter}
                  modalOpen={anyModalOpen}
                  quizHistory={quizHistories[activeQuizDatasetId]}
                  cloudSyncEnabled={Boolean(user && user.emailVerified)}
                  quizHistorySyncing={quizHistorySyncing}
                  onRecordQuizSession={handleRecordQuizSession}
                  onClearQuizHistory={handleClearQuizHistory}
                />
              ) : null}
            </div>
          </div>
        </main>

        <footer className="border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-white/70 dark:bg-slate-900/80 py-5 px-4 text-center text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
          {datasetMode === 'irodori'
            ? activeIrodoriBook.data.length > 0
              ? user
                ? `${activeIrodoriBook.data.length} kosakata & kanji ${activeIrodoriBook.label} (Bab 1–18) · akun ${effectiveDisplayName || user.email || 'terhubung'}${user.emailVerified ? ' (cloud aktif)' : ' (menunggu verifikasi email)'}`
                : `${activeIrodoriBook.data.length} kosakata & kanji ${activeIrodoriBook.label} (Bab 1–18) · tersimpan lokal (masuk untuk sinkronisasi cloud)`
              : `${activeIrodoriBook.label} · belum ada data materi`
            : user
              ? `500 kosakata kanji JLPT N4 · hingga 2.500 variasi soal kuis · akun ${effectiveDisplayName || user.email || 'terhubung'}${user.emailVerified ? ' (cloud aktif)' : ' (menunggu verifikasi email)'}`
              : '500 kosakata kanji JLPT N4 · hingga 2.500 variasi soal kuis · tersimpan lokal (masuk untuk sinkronisasi cloud)'}
        </footer>
      </div>

      <AuthModal
        open={authModalOpen}
        isSwitchingAccount={authSwitchMode}
        busy={authBusy}
        errorText={authErrorText}
        infoText={authInfoText}
        showConsoleHint={authConsoleHint}
        onClearNotice={clearAuthNotices}
        onGoogleLogin={forceSelect => void handleGoogleLogin(forceSelect)}
        onGithubLogin={() => void handleGithubLogin()}
        onEmailSignIn={(em, pw) => void handleEmailSignIn(em, pw)}
        onEmailSignUp={(nm, em, pw) => void handleEmailSignUp(nm, em, pw)}
        onEmailReset={em => void handleEmailReset(em)}
        onClose={() => setAuthModalOpen(false)}
      />

      <ProfileModal
        open={profileModalOpen}
        initialName={effectiveDisplayName}
        initialAvatarUrl={customAvatarUrl}
        fallbackAuthPhoto={user?.photoURL || ''}
        saving={profileSaving}
        onSave={handleSaveProfile}
        onClose={() => setProfileModalOpen(false)}
      />

      <RestoreModal
        pending={pendingRestore}
        currentMasteredCount={activeMastered.size}
        currentHardCount={activeHardSet.size}
        mergedMasteredCount={restorePreviews.mergedMastered}
        mergedHardCount={restorePreviews.mergedHard}
        cleanBackupHardCount={restorePreviews.cleanImpHard}
        onConfirm={handleConfirmRestore}
        onClose={handleCloseRestoreModal}
      />

      <ToastBanner toast={toast} onDismiss={dismissToast} />
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/firebase.ts
================================================
import { initializeApp } from 'firebase/app';
import { GithubAuthProvider, GoogleAuthProvider, getAuth } from 'firebase/auth';
import { doc, getDocFromServer, getFirestore } from 'firebase/firestore';
import firebaseConfig from '../firebase-applet-config.json';

const app = initializeApp(firebaseConfig);
export const db = getFirestore(app, firebaseConfig.firestoreDatabaseId);
export const auth = getAuth(app);
export const googleProvider = new GoogleAuthProvider();
export const githubProvider = new GithubAuthProvider();

export function createGoogleAccountPickerProvider(): GoogleAuthProvider {
  const provider = new GoogleAuthProvider();
  provider.setCustomParameters({ prompt: 'select_account' });
  return provider;
}

export const FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID = firebaseConfig.projectId;
export const FIRESTORE_DATABASE_ID = firebaseConfig.firestoreDatabaseId;
export const FIREBASE_AUTH_PROVIDERS_URL = `https://console.firebase.google.com/project/${FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID}/authentication/providers`;

export enum OperationType {
  CREATE = 'create',
  UPDATE = 'update',
  DELETE = 'delete',
  LIST = 'list',
  GET = 'get',
  WRITE = 'write',
}

export interface FirestoreErrorInfo {
  error: string;
  operationType: OperationType;
  path: string | null;
  authInfo: {
    userId?: string | null;
    email?: string | null;
    emailVerified?: boolean | null;
    isAnonymous?: boolean | null;
    tenantId?: string | null;
    providerInfo?: {
      providerId?: string | null;
      email?: string | null;
    }[];
  };
}

export function isFirestoreQuotaError(error: unknown): boolean {
  const msg = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
  return (
    msg.includes('Quota exceeded') ||
    msg.includes('resource-exhausted') ||
    msg.includes('RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED')
  );
}

export function getFirestoreUpgradeUrl(): string {
  return `https://console.firebase.google.com/project/${FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID}/firestore/databases/${FIRESTORE_DATABASE_ID}/data?openUpgradeDialog=true`;
}

export function handleFirestoreError(
  error: unknown,
  operationType: OperationType,
  path: string | null
): never {
  const errInfo: FirestoreErrorInfo = {
    error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
    authInfo: {
      userId: auth.currentUser?.uid,
      email: auth.currentUser?.email,
      emailVerified: auth.currentUser?.emailVerified,
      isAnonymous: auth.currentUser?.isAnonymous,
      tenantId: auth.currentUser?.tenantId,
      providerInfo:
        auth.currentUser?.providerData?.map(provider => ({
          providerId: provider.providerId,
          email: provider.email,
        })) || [],
    },
    operationType,
    path,
  };
  const serialized = JSON.stringify(errInfo);
  console.error('Firestore Error: ', serialized);
  throw new Error(serialized);
}

async function testConnection(): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await getDocFromServer(doc(db, 'test', 'connection'));
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof Error && error.message.includes('the client is offline')) {
      console.error('Please check your Firebase configuration.');
    }
  }
}

void testConnection();

FILE: src/components/AppHeader.tsx

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type RefObject } from 'react';
import type { User } from 'firebase/auth';
import {
  AlertCircle,
  ArrowLeft,
  BookOpen,
  Camera,
  ChevronDown,
  Cloud,
  Download,
  FolderOpen,
  LogIn,
  LogOut,
  Moon,
  RefreshCw,
  Sun,
  Users,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { AppDatasetMode } from '../types/kanji';

interface AppHeaderProps {
  datasetMode: AppDatasetMode;
  irodoriBookTitle?: string;
  onToggleDatasetMode: () => void;
  isDark: boolean;
  onToggleTheme: () => void;
  masteredCount: number;
  totalCount: number;
  restoreBtnRef: RefObject<HTMLButtonElement | null>;
  fileInputRef: RefObject<HTMLInputElement | null>;
  user: User | null;
  displayName: string;
  avatarUrl: string;
  authReady: boolean;
  authBusy: boolean;
  syncing: boolean;
  onOpenAuthModal: (isSwitch: boolean) => void;
  onOpenProfileModal: () => void;
  onManualSync: () => void;
  onResendVerification: () => void;
  onRefreshVerification: () => void;
  onSignOut: () => void;
  onExport: () => void;
  onTriggerImport: () => void;
  onFileSelected: (file: File) => void;
}

function getProviderLabel(user: User): string {
  const providerId = user.providerData?.[0]?.providerId;
  if (providerId === 'google.com') return 'Google';
  if (providerId === 'github.com') return 'GitHub';
  if (providerId === 'password') return 'Email & Sandi';
  return 'Akun Terhubung';
}

export function AppHeader({
  datasetMode,
  irodoriBookTitle = 'Irodori Dasar 1',
  onToggleDatasetMode,
  isDark,
  onToggleTheme,
  masteredCount,
  totalCount,
  restoreBtnRef,
  fileInputRef,
  user,
  displayName,
  avatarUrl,
  authReady,
  authBusy,
  syncing,
  onOpenAuthModal,
  onOpenProfileModal,
  onManualSync,
  onResendVerification,
  onRefreshVerification,
  onSignOut,
  onExport,
  onTriggerImport,
  onFileSelected,
}: AppHeaderProps) {
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [avatarBroken, setAvatarBroken] = useState(false);

  const menuWrapperRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const menuBtnRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    setAvatarBroken(false);
  }, [avatarUrl, user?.uid]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) {
      setMenuOpen(false);
    }
  }, [user]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!menuOpen) return;

    const handlePointerDown = (e: PointerEvent) => {
      if (menuWrapperRef.current && !menuWrapperRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setMenuOpen(false);
      }
    };

    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        e.preventDefault();
        setMenuOpen(false);
        requestAnimationFrame(() => menuBtnRef.current?.focus());
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown);
    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown);
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    };
  }, [menuOpen]);

  const resolvedName =
    displayName.trim() || user?.displayName?.trim() || user?.email?.split('@')[0] || 'Pengguna';
  const initial = resolvedName.charAt(0).toUpperCase() || 'K';
  const resolvedAvatar = avatarUrl || user?.photoURL || '';
  const isVerified = Boolean(user?.emailVerified);

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 bg-white/95 dark:bg-slate-900/95 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 transition-colors">
      <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-3 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-2 lg:py-0 lg:h-16 flex flex-wrap lg:flex-nowrap items-center justify-between gap-x-2 gap-y-2 sm:gap-x-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 sm:gap-2.5 min-w-0 shrink-0 order-1">
          <span
            aria-hidden="true"
            className={`inline-flex items-center justify-center w-8 h-8 sm:w-9 sm:h-9 rounded-xl text-white font-jp font-bold text-base sm:text-lg shrink-0 ${
              datasetMode === 'irodori' ? 'bg-teal-600' : 'bg-indigo-600'
            }`}
          >
            {datasetMode === 'irodori' ? '彩' : '日'}
          </span>
          <span className="font-bold text-sm sm:text-lg tracking-tight text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 whitespace-nowrap">
            {datasetMode === 'irodori' ? irodoriBookTitle : 'N4 Kanji'}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="w-full lg:w-auto order-4 lg:order-2 pt-1.5 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 lg:border-t-0 lg:pt-0 flex items-center">
          <button
            type="button"
            id="btnToggleIrodori"
            onClick={onToggleDatasetMode}
            aria-pressed={datasetMode === 'irodori'}
            className={`w-full lg:w-auto min-h-[40px] px-3 sm:px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 ${
              datasetMode === 'irodori'
                ? 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-900 text-white hover:bg-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-100 dark:border-slate-100 dark:text-slate-900 dark:hover:bg-white'
                : 'bg-teal-50 border-teal-200 text-teal-800 hover:bg-teal-100 dark:bg-teal-950/70 dark:border-teal-800 dark:text-teal-200 dark:hover:bg-teal-900/70'
            }`}
          >
            {datasetMode === 'irodori' ? (
              <>
                <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                <span>Kembali ke Kanji N4</span>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <BookOpen className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                <span>Buka Kotoba dan Kanji Irodori</span>
              </>
            )}
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 font-mono-num whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 order-2 lg:order-3">
          <strong className="text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 font-bold" id="mCount">
            {masteredCount}
          </strong>
          /<span id="mTotal">{totalCount}</span>
          <span className="hidden xs:inline sm:inline"> hafal</span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 shrink-0 order-3 lg:order-4">
          <button
            type="button"
            id="btnToggleTheme"
            onClick={onToggleTheme}
            aria-pressed={isDark}
            aria-label={isDark ? 'Aktifkan mode terang' : 'Aktifkan mode gelap'}
            title={isDark ? 'Ganti ke mode terang' : 'Ganti ke mode gelap'}
            className="min-h-[40px] min-w-[40px] px-2.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-amber-300 inline-flex items-center justify-center transition-colors shrink-0 cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500"
          >
            {isDark ? (
              <Sun className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
            ) : (
              <Moon className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
            )}
          </button>

          {authReady && !user ? (
            <>
              <button
                type="button"
                id="btnExportData"
                onClick={onExport}
                aria-label="Backup progres (.json)"
                className="min-h-[40px] px-2.5 sm:px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:text-indigo-700 dark:hover:text-indigo-300 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500"
                title="Backup progres (.json)"
              >
                <Download className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                <span className="hidden md:inline">Backup</span>
              </button>

              <button
                ref={restoreBtnRef}
                type="button"
                id="btnImportData"
                onClick={onTriggerImport}
                aria-label="Restore progres (.json)"
                className="min-h-[40px] px-2.5 sm:px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:text-indigo-700 dark:hover:text-indigo-300 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500"
                title="Restore progres (.json)"
              >
                <FolderOpen className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                <span className="hidden md:inline">Restore</span>
              </button>

              <input
                ref={fileInputRef}
                type="file"
                id="fileImportData"
                accept=".json,application/json"
                className="sr-only"
                aria-label="Pilih file backup JSON"
                onChange={e => {
                  const file = e.target.files?.[0];
                  if (file) onFileSelected(file);
                }}
              />
            </>
          ) : null}

          {!authReady ? (
            <div
              aria-hidden="true"
              className="h-10 w-[86px] sm:w-[104px] rounded-xl bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 animate-pulse shrink-0"
            />
          ) : user ? (
            <div ref={menuWrapperRef} className="relative">
              <button
                ref={menuBtnRef}
                type="button"
                id="btnAccountMenu"
                aria-haspopup="menu"
                aria-expanded={menuOpen}
                aria-label={`Menu akun ${resolvedName}`}
                onClick={() => setMenuOpen(o => !o)}
                className="min-h-[40px] px-2 sm:px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 inline-flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500"
              >
                <span className="relative shrink-0">
                  {resolvedAvatar && !avatarBroken ? (
                    <img
                      src={resolvedAvatar}
                      alt={resolvedName}
                      referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                      onError={() => setAvatarBroken(true)}
                      className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 object-cover bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-700"
                    />
                  ) : (
                    <span
                      aria-hidden="true"
                      className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full border border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-700 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/70 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-200 font-bold text-xs inline-flex items-center justify-center"
                    >
                      {initial}
                    </span>
                  )}
                  {!isVerified ? (
                    <span
                      aria-hidden="true"
                      className="absolute -top-0.5 -right-0.5 w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500 border-2 border-white dark:border-slate-800"
                    />
                  ) : syncing ? (
                    <span
                      aria-hidden="true"
                      className="absolute -bottom-0.5 -right-0.5 w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-indigo-500 border-2 border-white dark:border-slate-800 animate-pulse"
                    />
                  ) : null}
                </span>

                <span className="hidden md:inline text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 max-w-[130px] truncate">
                  {resolvedName}
                </span>

                <ChevronDown
                  className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 transition-transform shrink-0 ${
                    menuOpen ? 'rotate-180' : ''
                  }`}
                  aria-hidden="true"
                />
              </button>

              {menuOpen ? (
                <div
                  id="accountMenuDropdown"
                  role="menu"
                  aria-labelledby="btnAccountMenu"
                  className="absolute right-0 mt-2 w-72 sm:w-80 rounded-2xl bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 shadow-xl py-2 z-40"
                >
                  <div className="px-4 py-3 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-start gap-3">
                    {resolvedAvatar && !avatarBroken ? (
                      <img
                        src={resolvedAvatar}
                        alt={resolvedName}
                        referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                        onError={() => setAvatarBroken(true)}
                        className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 object-cover shrink-0 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800"
                      />
                    ) : (
                      <span
                        aria-hidden="true"
                        className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full border border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-700 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/70 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-200 font-bold text-sm inline-flex items-center justify-center shrink-0"
                      >
                        {initial}
                      </span>
                    )}
                    <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                      <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 truncate">{resolvedName}</p>
                      {user.email ? (
                        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 truncate">{user.email}</p>
                      ) : null}
                      <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <span>{getProviderLabel(user)}</span>
                        <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                        {isVerified ? (
                          <span className="text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 font-medium inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                            <Cloud className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                            {syncing ? 'Menyinkronkan...' : 'Cloud aktif'}
                          </span>
                        ) : (
                          <span className="text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400 font-medium inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                            <AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                            Belum verifikasi email
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {!isVerified ? (
                    <div className="mx-3 my-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/50 border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-800/80 p-2.5 space-y-2">
                      <p className="text-[11px] text-amber-900 dark:text-amber-200 leading-relaxed">
                        Verifikasi email Anda agar progres bisa disinkronkan ke cloud.
                      </p>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={onResendVerification}
                          disabled={authBusy}
                          className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-amber-300 dark:border-amber-700 text-[11px] font-semibold text-amber-900 dark:text-amber-200 hover:bg-amber-100 dark:hover:bg-amber-900/50 cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          Kirim Ulang
                        </button>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={onRefreshVerification}
                          disabled={authBusy}
                          className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-amber-600 text-[11px] font-semibold text-white hover:bg-amber-700 cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          Cek Status
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ) : null}

                  <div className="py-1">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      role="menuitem"
                      id="menuEditProfile"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setMenuOpen(false);
                        onOpenProfileModal();
                      }}
                      className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 text-left text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-indigo-700 dark:hover:text-indigo-300 flex items-center gap-2.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      <Camera className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400" aria-hidden="true" />
                      <span>Ubah Nama &amp; Foto Profil</span>
                    </button>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      role="menuitem"
                      id="menuSwitchAccount"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setMenuOpen(false);
                        onOpenAuthModal(true);
                      }}
                      className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 text-left text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-indigo-700 dark:hover:text-indigo-300 flex items-center gap-2.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      <Users className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400" aria-hidden="true" />
                      <span>Ganti Akun</span>
                    </button>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      role="menuitem"
                      id="menuSyncNow"
                      disabled={!isVerified || syncing}
                      onClick={() => {
                        setMenuOpen(false);
                        onManualSync();
                      }}
                      className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 text-left text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-indigo-700 dark:hover:text-indigo-300 flex items-center gap-2.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-45 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                    >
                      <RefreshCw
                        className={`w-4 h-4 shrink-0 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 ${syncing ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`}
                        aria-hidden="true"
                      />
                      <span>Sinkronkan Progres Sekarang</span>
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  <div className="my-1 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800" />

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    role="menuitem"
                    id="btnGoogleLogout"
                    disabled={authBusy}
                    onClick={() => {
                      setMenuOpen(false);
                      onSignOut();
                    }}
                    className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 text-left text-sm font-semibold text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400 hover:bg-rose-50 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/50 flex items-center gap-2.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
                  >
                    <LogOut className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                    <span>Keluar</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              ) : null}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <button
              type="button"
              id="btnOpenAuthModal"
              onClick={() => onOpenAuthModal(false)}
              disabled={authBusy}
              aria-label="Masuk ke akun untuk sinkronisasi progres"
              title="Masuk atau daftar akun"
              className="min-h-[40px] px-3 sm:px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500"
            >
              <LogIn className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
              <span>Masuk</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/AuthModal.tsx

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Eye, EyeOff, KeyRound, Mail, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { FIREBASE_AUTH_PROVIDERS_URL } from '../firebase';

export type AuthModalView = 'signin' | 'signup' | 'reset';

interface AuthModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  isSwitchingAccount: boolean;
  busy: boolean;
  errorText: string | null;
  infoText: string | null;
  showConsoleHint: boolean;
  onClearNotice: () => void;
  onGoogleLogin: (forceSelectAccount: boolean) => void;
  onGithubLogin: () => void;
  onEmailSignIn: (email: string, password: string) => void;
  onEmailSignUp: (name: string, email: string, password: string) => void;
  onEmailReset: (email: string) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}

function GoogleGlyph() {
  return (
    <svg className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
      <path
        fill="#4285F4"
        d="M23.745 12.27c0-.7-.06-1.4-.19-2.07H12v4.51h6.6c-.29 1.52-1.14 2.82-2.4 3.68v3.05h3.88c2.27-2.09 3.665-5.17 3.665-9.17Z"
      />
      <path
        fill="#34A853"
        d="M12 24c3.24 0 5.95-1.08 7.93-2.91l-3.88-3.05c-1.08.72-2.45 1.16-4.05 1.16-3.12 0-5.77-2.11-6.72-4.96H1.29v3.14C3.26 21.3 7.31 24 12 24Z"
      />
      <path
        fill="#FBBC05"
        d="M5.28 14.24c-.24-.72-.38-1.49-.38-2.24s.14-1.52.38-2.24V6.62H1.29C.47 8.24 0 10.06 0 12s.47 3.76 1.29 5.38l3.99-3.14Z"
      />
      <path
        fill="#EA4335"
        d="M12 4.75c1.77 0 3.35.61 4.6 1.8l3.42-3.42C17.95 1.19 15.24 0 12 0 7.31 0 3.26 2.7 1.29 6.62l3.99 3.14c.95-2.85 3.6-4.96 6.72-4.96Z"
      />
    </svg>
  );
}

function GithubGlyph() {
  return (
    <svg className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 fill-current" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
      <path d="M12 0C5.37 0 0 5.37 0 12c0 5.31 3.435 9.795 8.205 11.385.6.105.825-.255.825-.57 0-.285-.015-1.23-.015-2.235-3.015.555-3.795-.735-4.035-1.41-.135-.345-.72-1.41-1.23-1.695-.42-.225-1.02-.78-.015-.795.945-.015 1.62.87 1.845 1.23 1.08 1.815 2.805 1.305 3.495.99.105-.78.42-1.305.765-1.605-2.67-.3-5.46-1.335-5.46-5.925 0-1.305.465-2.385 1.23-3.225-.12-.3-.54-1.53.12-3.18 0 0 1.005-.315 3.3 1.23.96-.27 1.98-.405 3-.405s2.04.135 3 .405c2.295-1.56 3.3-1.23 3.3-1.23.66 1.65.24 2.88.12 3.18.765.84 1.23 1.905 1.23 3.225 0 4.605-2.805 5.625-5.475 5.925.435.375.81 1.095.81 2.22 0 1.605-.015 2.895-.015 3.3 0 .315.225.69.825.57A12.02 12.02 0 0 0 24 12c0-6.63-5.37-12-12-12Z" />
    </svg>
  );
}

export function AuthModal({
  open,
  isSwitchingAccount,
  busy,
  errorText,
  infoText,
  showConsoleHint,
  onClearNotice,
  onGoogleLogin,
  onGithubLogin,
  onEmailSignIn,
  onEmailSignUp,
  onEmailReset,
  onClose,
}: AuthModalProps) {
  const [view, setView] = useState<AuthModalView>('signin');
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [showPass, setShowPass] = useState(false);

  const modalRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const closeBtnRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    setView('signin');
    setPassword('');
    setShowPass(false);
    const frame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      closeBtnRef.current?.focus();
    });
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
  }, [open]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        e.preventDefault();
        onClose();
        return;
      }
      if (e.key !== 'Tab' || !modalRef.current) return;
      const focusables = Array.from(
        modalRef.current.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
          'button:not([disabled]), input:not([disabled]), a[href], [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])'
        )
      ).filter(el => el.offsetParent !== null);
      if (focusables.length === 0) return;
      const first = focusables[0];
      const last = focusables[focusables.length - 1];
      if (e.shiftKey) {
        if (document.activeElement === first || !modalRef.current.contains(document.activeElement)) {
          e.preventDefault();
          last.focus();
        }
      } else if (
        document.activeElement === last ||
        !modalRef.current.contains(document.activeElement)
      ) {
        e.preventDefault();
        first.focus();
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [open, onClose]);

  if (!open) return null;

  const switchView = (next: AuthModalView) => {
    setView(next);
    onClearNotice();
  };

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (view === 'signin') {
      onEmailSignIn(email, password);
    } else if (view === 'signup') {
      onEmailSignUp(name, email, password);
    } else {
      onEmailReset(email);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      id="authModalOverlay"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby="authModalTitle"
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/50 dark:bg-slate-950/75 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4"
      onClick={e => {
        if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose();
      }}
    >
      <div
        ref={modalRef}
        className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden"
      >
        <div className="px-5 py-4 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <h2 id="authModalTitle" className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
              {isSwitchingAccount ? 'Ganti Akun Belajar' : 'Masuk & Sinkronisasi Progres'}
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
              {isSwitchingAccount
                ? 'Pilih akun lain untuk melanjutkan atau berpindah progres.'
                : 'Simpan progres hafalan kanji N4 secara otomatis ke cloud.'}
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            ref={closeBtnRef}
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Tutup dialog login"
            className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-300 hover:text-slate-800 dark:hover:text-slate-50 inline-flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer shrink-0"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-5 space-y-4">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
            <button
              type="button"
              id="btnAuthGoogle"
              disabled={busy}
              onClick={() => onGoogleLogin(isSwitchingAccount)}
              className="min-h-[42px] px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 text-sm font-semibold inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              <GoogleGlyph />
              <span>{isSwitchingAccount ? 'Pilih Akun Google' : 'Google'}</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              id="btnAuthGithub"
              disabled={busy}
              onClick={onGithubLogin}
              className="min-h-[42px] px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-600 bg-slate-900 dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-800 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-white text-sm font-semibold inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              <GithubGlyph />
              <span>GitHub</span>
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="relative flex items-center py-0.5">
            <div className="grow border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700" />
            <span className="shrink mx-3 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">
              atau dengan email
            </span>
            <div className="grow border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700" />
          </div>

          <div
            role="tablist"
            aria-label="Mode autentikasi email"
            className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 p-1"
          >
            <button
              type="button"
              role="tab"
              aria-selected={view === 'signin'}
              onClick={() => switchView('signin')}
              className={`min-h-[34px] rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                view === 'signin'
                  ? 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 shadow-xs'
                  : 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-slate-50'
              }`}
            >
              Masuk
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              role="tab"
              aria-selected={view === 'signup'}
              onClick={() => switchView('signup')}
              className={`min-h-[34px] rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                view === 'signup'
                  ? 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 shadow-xs'
                  : 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-slate-50'
              }`}
            >
              Daftar Baru
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              role="tab"
              aria-selected={view === 'reset'}
              onClick={() => switchView('reset')}
              className={`min-h-[34px] rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                view === 'reset'
                  ? 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 shadow-xs'
                  : 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-slate-50'
              }`}
            >
              Lupa Sandi
            </button>
          </div>

          {errorText ? (
            <div
              role="alert"
              className="rounded-xl border border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-800/80 bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/50 px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs text-rose-800 dark:text-rose-200 space-y-1.5"
            >
              <p className="font-semibold">{errorText}</p>
              {showConsoleHint ? (
                <p className="text-[11px] text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-300 leading-relaxed">
                  Aktifkan metode login ini di{' '}
                  <a
                    href={FIREBASE_AUTH_PROVIDERS_URL}
                    target="_blank"
                    rel="noopener noreferrer"
                    className="underline font-semibold"
                  >
                    Firebase Console → Authentication → Sign-in method
                  </a>
                  .
                </p>
              ) : null}
            </div>
          ) : null}

          {infoText ? (
            <div
              role="status"
              className="rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800/80 bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/50 px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-200 font-medium"
            >
              {infoText}
            </div>
          ) : null}

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-3">
            {view === 'signup' ? (
              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="authNameInput"
                  className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 mb-1"
                >
                  Nama Tampilan
                </label>
                <input
                  id="authNameInput"
                  type="text"
                  maxLength={60}
                  value={name}
                  onChange={e => setName(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Contoh: Budi Santoso"
                  className="w-full h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-950 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:placeholder:text-slate-500 focus:outline-2 focus:outline-indigo-500"
                />
              </div>
            ) : null}

            <div>
              <label
                htmlFor="authEmailInput"
                className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 mb-1"
              >
                Alamat Email
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Mail
                  className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none"
                  aria-hidden="true"
                />
                <input
                  id="authEmailInput"
                  type="email"
                  required
                  autoComplete="email"
                  value={email}
                  onChange={e => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="nama@contoh.com"
                  className="w-full h-10 pl-9 pr-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-950 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:placeholder:text-slate-500 focus:outline-2 focus:outline-indigo-500"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {view !== 'reset' ? (
              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="authPasswordInput"
                  className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 mb-1"
                >
                  Kata Sandi
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <KeyRound
                    className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none"
                    aria-hidden="true"
                  />
                  <input
                    id="authPasswordInput"
                    type={showPass ? 'text' : 'password'}
                    required
                    minLength={6}
                    autoComplete={view === 'signup' ? 'new-password' : 'current-password'}
                    value={password}
                    onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Minimal 6 karakter"
                    className="w-full h-10 pl-9 pr-10 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-950 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:placeholder:text-slate-500 focus:outline-2 focus:outline-indigo-500"
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setShowPass(s => !s)}
                    aria-label={showPass ? 'Sembunyikan sandi' : 'Lihat sandi'}
                    className="absolute right-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 p-1.5 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700 dark:hover:text-slate-200 cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    {showPass ? (
                      <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                    ) : (
                      <Eye className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                    )}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : null}

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={busy}
              className="w-full min-h-[42px] px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-sm font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {busy
                ? 'Memproses...'
                : view === 'signin'
                  ? 'Masuk dengan Email'
                  : view === 'signup'
                    ? 'Buat Akun & Kirim Verifikasi'
                    : 'Kirim Link Reset Sandi'}
            </button>
          </form>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx
================================================
import { Component, type ErrorInfo, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { getFirestoreUpgradeUrl } from '../firebase';

interface ErrorBoundaryProps {
  children: ReactNode;
}

interface ErrorBoundaryState {
  error: Error | null;
}

export class ErrorBoundary extends Component<ErrorBoundaryProps, ErrorBoundaryState> {
  state: ErrorBoundaryState = { error: null };

  static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error): ErrorBoundaryState {
    return { error };
  }

  componentDidCatch(error: Error, info: ErrorInfo): void {
    console.error('Application ErrorBoundary caught:', error, info);
  }

  render() {
    const { error } = this.state;
    if (!error) {
      return this.props.children;
    }

    const rawMsg = error.message || 'Terjadi kesalahan pada aplikasi.';
    const isQuotaExceeded = rawMsg.includes('Quota exceeded');
    const isFreeDailyReadLimit = rawMsg.includes('Free daily read units per project');

    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
        <div
          role="alert"
          className="w-full max-w-lg rounded-2xl bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-800/80 shadow-lg p-6 space-y-4"
        >
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <h1 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
              {isQuotaExceeded
                ? 'Batas Kuota Firestore Tercapai'
                : 'Terjadi Gangguan Sinkronisasi'}
            </h1>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
              {isQuotaExceeded
                ? 'Kuota harian gratis Firestore telah habis. Kuota akan direset keesokan hari (info paket Spark edisi Enterprise tersedia di halaman harga Firebase Firestore).'
                : 'Aplikasi mengalami kendala saat memproses data. Muat ulang halaman untuk melanjutkan belajar.'}
            </p>
          </div>

          {isFreeDailyReadLimit ? (
            <div className="rounded-xl bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/50 border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-800/80 p-3 text-xs text-amber-900 dark:text-amber-200 break-all">
              Kelola kuota database:{' '}
              <a
                href={getFirestoreUpgradeUrl()}
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
                className="underline font-semibold"
              >
                {getFirestoreUpgradeUrl()}
              </a>
            </div>
          ) : null}

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 pt-1">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => window.location.reload()}
              className="min-h-[42px] px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-sm font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              Muat Ulang Halaman
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }
}

FILE: src/components/FlashcardPanel.tsx
