FILE: src/components/KanjiListPanel.tsx
================================================
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { ChevronDown, Search, Volume2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { IRODORI_BOOKS } from '../data/irodoriBooks';
import { IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS } from '../data/irodoriShared';
import { KANJI, LEVELS } from '../data/kanjiData';
import type {
  AppDatasetMode,
  IrodoriBookConfig,
  IrodoriChapterFilter,
  IrodoriChapterNumber,
  KanjiEntry,
  LevelFilter,
} from '../types/kanji';
import { speakJP } from '../utils/tts';

interface KanjiListPanelProps {
  datasetMode?: AppDatasetMode;
  irodoriBook?: IrodoriBookConfig;
  levelFilter: LevelFilter;
  chapterFilter?: IrodoriChapterFilter;
  mastered: Set<string>;
  hardSet: Set<string>;
}

export function KanjiListPanel({
  datasetMode = 'n4',
  irodoriBook = IRODORI_BOOKS.dasar1,
  levelFilter,
  chapterFilter = 'all',
  mastered,
  hardSet,
}: KanjiListPanelProps) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [openIds, setOpenIds] = useState<Set<string>>(() => new Set());

  const filteredItems = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    const source = datasetMode === 'irodori' ? irodoriBook.data : KANJI;

    return source.filter(it => {
      if (datasetMode === 'irodori') {
        if (chapterFilter !== 'all' && it.chapter !== chapterFilter) return false;
      } else {
        if (levelFilter !== 'all' && it.dif !== levelFilter) return false;
      }

      if (!q) return true;
      const chapterLabel = it.chapter ? `bab ${it.chapter}` : '';
      return (
        it.k.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        it.r.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        it.m.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        it.exi.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (it.romaji?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ?? false) ||
        (it.subSection?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ?? false) ||
        chapterLabel.includes(q)
      );
    });
  }, [datasetMode, irodoriBook.data, levelFilter, chapterFilter, query]);

  const groupedByChapter = useMemo(() => {
    if (datasetMode !== 'irodori') return [];
    const map = new Map<IrodoriChapterNumber, KanjiEntry[]>();
    for (const item of filteredItems) {
      if (!item.chapter) continue;
      const arr = map.get(item.chapter);
      if (arr) arr.push(item);
      else map.set(item.chapter, [item]);
    }
    return IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS.map(ch => ({
      chapter: ch,
      meta: irodoriBook.chapters[ch],
      items: map.get(ch) ?? [],
    })).filter(g => g.items.length > 0);
  }, [datasetMode, irodoriBook.chapters, filteredItems]);

  const toggleOpen = (id: string) => {
    setOpenIds(prev => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(id)) next.delete(id);
      else next.add(id);
      return next;
    });
  };

  const renderCard = (item: KanjiEntry) => {
    const isOpen = openIds.has(item.id);
    const isMastered = mastered.has(item.id);
    const isHard = hardSet.has(item.id);
    const isLongWord = item.k.length >= 5;

    return (
      <div
        key={item.id}
        className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 p-3.5 sm:p-4 transition-colors hover:border-slate-300 dark:hover:border-slate-700"
      >
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2.5 sm:gap-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-expanded={isOpen}
            onClick={() => toggleOpen(item.id)}
            className="flex-1 min-w-0 flex items-center gap-3 text-left cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 rounded-lg"
          >
            <span
              className={`font-jp font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 shrink-0 text-center break-words leading-snug ${
                datasetMode === 'irodori'
                  ? isLongWord
                    ? 'text-base sm:text-lg w-20 sm:w-24'
                    : 'text-xl sm:text-2xl w-20 sm:w-24'
                  : 'text-2xl sm:text-3xl min-w-[3.5rem]'
              }`}
            >
              {item.k}
            </span>
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <div className="font-jp text-sm sm:text-base font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 truncate">
                {item.r}
                {item.accent ? (
                  <span className="ml-1.5 text-[11px] font-normal text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                    {item.accent}
                  </span>
                ) : null}
              </div>
              {item.romaji ? (
                <div className="text-[11px] sm:text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 truncate">
                  {item.romaji}
                </div>
              ) : null}
              <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 line-clamp-2">{item.m}</div>
              <div className="mt-1 text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
                {datasetMode === 'irodori' ? (
                  <span className="truncate max-w-full">
                    {item.subSection ?? (item.chapter ? `Bab ${item.chapter}` : '')}
                  </span>
                ) : (
                  <span>{LEVELS[item.dif].label}</span>
                )}
                {item.kanjiList && item.kanjiList.length > 0 ? (
                  <>
                    <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                    <span className="font-jp text-teal-700 dark:text-teal-300 font-semibold">
                      Kanji: {item.kanjiList.join(' ')}
                    </span>
                  </>
                ) : null}
                {isMastered ? (
                  <>
                    <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                    <span className="text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 font-semibold">✓ Hafal</span>
                  </>
                ) : null}
                {isHard ? (
                  <>
                    <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                    <span className="text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400 font-semibold">⚠ Sulit</span>
                  </>
                ) : null}
              </div>
            </div>
            <ChevronDown
              className={`w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 shrink-0 transition-transform ${
                isOpen ? 'rotate-180' : ''
              }`}
              aria-hidden="true"
            />
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label={`Putar pelafalan ${item.k}`}
            onClick={() => speakJP(item.r)}
            className="min-h-[40px] min-w-[40px] rounded-xl bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/70 hover:bg-indigo-100 dark:hover:bg-indigo-900/70 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-200 inline-flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500"
          >
            <Volume2 className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {isOpen ? (
          <div className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 space-y-1.5">
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
              <p className="font-jp text-sm sm:text-base text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 leading-relaxed">
                {item.ex}
              </p>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => speakJP(item.ex)}
                className="shrink-0 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer"
              >
                <Volume2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                <span>Kalimat</span>
              </button>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">{item.exi}</p>
          </div>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    );
  };

  return (
    <section
      aria-label={
        datasetMode === 'irodori' ? 'Daftar Kosakata dan Kanji Irodori' : 'Daftar Kosakata Kanji'
      }
      className="space-y-4"
    >
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
          Ketuk baris untuk melihat contoh kalimat. Progres tersimpan di perangkat ini.
        </p>
        <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 font-mono-num whitespace-nowrap">
          {filteredItems.length} kosakata
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="relative max-w-xl">
        <Search
          className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none"
          aria-hidden="true"
        />
        <input
          type="search"
          id="kanjiSearch"
          value={query}
          onChange={e => setQuery(e.target.value)}
          placeholder={
            datasetMode === 'irodori'
              ? 'Cari kotoba, kanji, hiragana, atau arti…'
              : 'Cari kanji, hiragana, atau arti…'
          }
          aria-label="Cari kosakata"
          className="w-full min-h-[46px] pl-11 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 text-sm sm:text-base font-medium text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:placeholder:text-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500"
        />
      </div>

      {filteredItems.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 p-10 text-center text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
          {query.trim()
            ? `Tidak ada hasil untuk “${query.trim()}”.`
            : 'Belum ada data kosakata pada bab ini.'}
        </div>
      ) : datasetMode === 'irodori' ? (
        <div className="space-y-6" id="kanjiList">
          {groupedByChapter.map(group => (
            <section
              key={group.chapter}
              aria-label={`${group.meta.label}: ${group.meta.idTitle}`}
              className="space-y-3"
            >
              <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 px-4 py-3 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5 min-w-0">
                  <span
                    aria-hidden="true"
                    className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full shrink-0 mt-1.5"
                    style={{ backgroundColor: group.meta.dotColor }}
                  />
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <h3 className="text-sm sm:text-base font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
                      {group.meta.label} · {group.meta.idTitle}
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                      <span className="font-jp font-semibold text-teal-700 dark:text-teal-300">
                        {group.meta.jpTitle}
                      </span>
                      <span aria-hidden="true"> · </span>
                      <span>{group.meta.topicId}</span>
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 font-mono-num shrink-0">
                  {group.items.length} kosakata
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                {group.items.map(renderCard)}
              </div>
            </section>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3" id="kanjiList">
          {filteredItems.map(renderCard)}
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}



================================================
FILE: src/components/ProfileModal.tsx
================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Camera, RotateCcw, Upload, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { PRESET_AVATARS, compressAvatarFile, renderPresetKanjiAvatar } from '../utils/avatarImage';
import { DISPLAY_NAME_MAX_LENGTH, isValidAvatarUrl } from '../utils/progressSync';

interface ProfileModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  initialName: string;
  initialAvatarUrl: string;
  fallbackAuthPhoto: string;
  saving: boolean;
  onSave: (displayName: string, avatarUrl: string) => Promise<boolean>;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export function ProfileModal({
  open,
  initialName,
  initialAvatarUrl,
  fallbackAuthPhoto,
  saving,
  onSave,
  onClose,
}: ProfileModalProps) {
  const [nameInput, setNameInput] = useState(initialName);
  const [avatarInput, setAvatarInput] = useState(initialAvatarUrl);
  const [urlInput, setUrlInput] = useState(
    initialAvatarUrl.startsWith('https://') ? initialAvatarUrl : ''
  );
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [previewBroken, setPreviewBroken] = useState(false);

  const modalRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const nameInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    setNameInput(initialName);
    setAvatarInput(initialAvatarUrl);
    setUrlInput(initialAvatarUrl.startsWith('https://') ? initialAvatarUrl : '');
    setErrorMsg(null);
    setPreviewBroken(false);
    const frame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      nameInputRef.current?.focus();
    });
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
  }, [open, initialName, initialAvatarUrl]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        e.preventDefault();
        onClose();
        return;
      }
      if (e.key !== 'Tab' || !modalRef.current) return;
      const focusables = Array.from(
        modalRef.current.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
          'button:not([disabled]), input:not([disabled]), [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])'
        )
      ).filter(el => el.offsetParent !== null);
      if (focusables.length === 0) return;
      const first = focusables[0];
      const last = focusables[focusables.length - 1];
      if (e.shiftKey) {
        if (document.activeElement === first || !modalRef.current.contains(document.activeElement)) {
          e.preventDefault();
          last.focus();
        }
      } else if (
        document.activeElement === last ||
        !modalRef.current.contains(document.activeElement)
      ) {
        e.preventDefault();
        first.focus();
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [open, onClose]);

  if (!open) return null;

  const activePreview = avatarInput || fallbackAuthPhoto;
  const previewInitial = (nameInput.trim() || 'K').charAt(0).toUpperCase();

  const handleFileChange = async (file: File) => {
    setErrorMsg(null);
    try {
      const compressed = await compressAvatarFile(file);
      setAvatarInput(compressed);
      setUrlInput('');
      setPreviewBroken(false);
    } catch (err) {
      setErrorMsg(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Gagal memproses gambar.');
    }
  };

  const handleApplyUrl = (nextUrl: string) => {
    setUrlInput(nextUrl);
    setErrorMsg(null);
    const trimmed = nextUrl.trim();
    if (!trimmed) {
      setAvatarInput('');
      setPreviewBroken(false);
      return;
    }
    if (!trimmed.startsWith('https://')) {
      setErrorMsg('URL foto harus diawali dengan https://');
      return;
    }
    if (!isValidAvatarUrl(trimmed)) {
      setErrorMsg('URL foto terlalu panjang atau tidak valid.');
      return;
    }
    setAvatarInput(trimmed);
    setPreviewBroken(false);
  };

  const handleSelectPreset = (index: number) => {
    setErrorMsg(null);
    try {
      const dataUrl = renderPresetKanjiAvatar(PRESET_AVATARS[index]);
      setAvatarInput(dataUrl);
      setUrlInput('');
      setPreviewBroken(false);
    } catch (err) {
      setErrorMsg(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Gagal memilih avatar.');
    }
  };

  const handleResetDefault = () => {
    setErrorMsg(null);
    setAvatarInput('');
    setUrlInput('');
    setPreviewBroken(false);
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setErrorMsg(null);
    const trimmedName = nameInput.trim();
    if (!trimmedName) {
      setErrorMsg('Nama tampilan tidak boleh kosong.');
      return;
    }
    if (urlInput.trim() && !urlInput.trim().startsWith('https://')) {
      setErrorMsg('URL foto harus menggunakan protokol https://');
      return;
    }
    const ok = await onSave(trimmedName, avatarInput);
    if (ok) onClose();
  };

  return (
    <div
      id="profileModalOverlay"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby="profileModalTitle"
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/50 dark:bg-slate-950/75 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4"
      onClick={e => {
        if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose();
      }}
    >
      <div
        ref={modalRef}
        className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden"
      >
        <div className="px-5 py-4 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <h2 id="profileModalTitle" className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
              Ubah Nama &amp; Foto Profil
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
              Perubahan tersinkronisasi ke akun Anda.
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Tutup editor profil"
            className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-300 hover:text-slate-800 dark:hover:text-slate-50 inline-flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={e => void handleSubmit(e)} className="p-5 space-y-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <div className="relative shrink-0">
              {activePreview && !previewBroken ? (
                <img
                  src={activePreview}
                  alt="Pratinjau foto profil"
                  referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                  onError={() => setPreviewBroken(true)}
                  className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full border-2 border-indigo-100 dark:border-indigo-800 object-cover bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800"
                />
              ) : (
                <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full border-2 border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-700 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/70 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-200 font-bold text-xl flex items-center justify-center">
                  {previewInitial}
                </div>
              )}
              <span
                aria-hidden="true"
                className="absolute -bottom-1 -right-1 w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-indigo-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center border-2 border-white dark:border-slate-900"
              >
                <Camera className="w-3 h-3" />
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()}
                className="min-h-[36px] px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer"
              >
                <Upload className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                Unggah Foto
              </button>

              {avatarInput ? (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleResetDefault}
                  className="min-h-[36px] px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 text-xs font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                  Bawaan Akun
                </button>
              ) : null}

              <input
                ref={fileRef}
                type="file"
                accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp"
                className="sr-only"
                aria-label="Pilih file foto profil"
                onChange={e => {
                  const file = e.target.files?.[0];
                  if (file) void handleFileChange(file);
                  e.target.value = '';
                }}
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 mb-2">Atau Pilih Avatar Kanji</p>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-6 gap-2">
              {PRESET_AVATARS.map((preset, idx) => (
                <button
                  key={preset.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => handleSelectPreset(idx)}
                  aria-label={`Pilih avatar ${preset.label}`}
                  title={preset.label}
                  style={{
                    background: `linear-gradient(135deg, ${preset.bgFrom}, ${preset.bgTo})`,
                  }}
                  className="aspect-square rounded-full text-white font-jp font-bold text-base flex items-center justify-center hover:scale-105 transition-transform cursor-pointer border-2 border-white dark:border-slate-800 shadow-xs focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500"
                >
                  {preset.kanji}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
              <label
                htmlFor="profileNameInput"
                className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200"
              >
                Nama Tampilan
              </label>
              <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 font-mono-num">
                {nameInput.length}/{DISPLAY_NAME_MAX_LENGTH}
              </span>
            </div>
            <input
              ref={nameInputRef}
              id="profileNameInput"
              type="text"
              required
              maxLength={DISPLAY_NAME_MAX_LENGTH}
              value={nameInput}
              onChange={e => setNameInput(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Nama Anda"
              className="w-full h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-950 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:placeholder:text-slate-500 focus:outline-2 focus:outline-indigo-500"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label
              htmlFor="profileUrlInput"
              className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 mb-1"
            >
              URL Foto Profil (opsional, HTTPS)
            </label>
            <input
              id="profileUrlInput"
              type="url"
              value={urlInput}
              onChange={e => handleApplyUrl(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="https://contoh.com/foto.jpg"
              className="w-full h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-950 text-sm text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:placeholder:text-slate-500 focus:outline-2 focus:outline-indigo-500"
            />
          </div>

          {errorMsg ? (
            <div
              role="alert"
              className="rounded-xl border border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-800/80 bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-rose-800 dark:text-rose-200"
            >
              {errorMsg}
            </div>
          ) : null}

          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="min-h-[40px] px-4 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 text-sm font-semibold cursor-pointer"
            >
              Batal
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={saving}
              className="min-h-[40px] px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-sm font-semibold cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {saving ? 'Menyimpan...' : 'Simpan Profil'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



================================================
FILE: src/components/QuizPanel.tsx
================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { IRODORI_BOOKS } from '../data/irodoriBooks';
import { IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS } from '../data/irodoriShared';
import { KANJI, LEVELS } from '../data/kanjiData';
import type {
  AppDatasetMode,
  IrodoriBookConfig,
  IrodoriChapterFilter,
  IrodoriChapterNumber,
  IrodoriChapterSelection,
  LevelFilter,
  QuizAnswerSnapshot,
  QuizDatasetId,
  QuizOption,
  QuizQuestion,
  QuizRecord,
  QuizSessionHistoryEntry,
  QuizTypeChoice,
} from '../types/kanji';
import {
  buildQuizBank,
  buildQuizSession,
  formatChapterSelectionLabel,
  getActiveTypeKeys,
  getPoolIndices,
} from '../utils/quizEngine';
import {
  QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY,
  buildQuizAnswerSnapshots,
  clearQuizHistory,
  computeQuizPerformanceStats,
  loadQuizHistory,
  recordQuizSession,
} from '../utils/quizStats';
import { QuizResultView } from './quiz/QuizResultView';
import { QuizRunView } from './quiz/QuizRunView';
import { QuizSetupView } from './quiz/QuizSetupView';
import { QuizStatsPanel } from './quiz/QuizStatsPanel';
import {
  PRESET_COUNTS,
  loadFuriganaPreference,
  saveFuriganaPreference,
} from './quiz/quizHelpers';

export interface RecordQuizSessionInput {
  level: LevelFilter;
  scopeLabel?: string;
  chapters?: IrodoriChapterNumber[];
  mode: QuizTypeChoice;
  correct: number;
  total: number;
  answers?: QuizAnswerSnapshot[];
}

interface QuizPanelProps {
  datasetMode?: AppDatasetMode;
  irodoriBook?: IrodoriBookConfig;
  levelFilter: LevelFilter;
  chapterFilter?: IrodoriChapterFilter;
  modalOpen: boolean;
  quizHistory?: QuizSessionHistoryEntry[];
  cloudSyncEnabled?: boolean;
  quizHistorySyncing?: boolean;
  onRecordQuizSession?: (datasetId: QuizDatasetId, input: RecordQuizSessionInput) => void;
  onClearQuizHistory?: (datasetId: QuizDatasetId) => void;
}

type QuizStage = 'setup' | 'run' | 'result';

export function QuizPanel({
  datasetMode = 'n4',
  irodoriBook = IRODORI_BOOKS.dasar1,
  levelFilter,
  chapterFilter = 'all',
  modalOpen,
  quizHistory: controlledQuizHistory,
  cloudSyncEnabled = false,
  quizHistorySyncing = false,
  onRecordQuizSession,
  onClearQuizHistory,
}: QuizPanelProps) {
  const activeDataset = datasetMode === 'irodori' ? irodoriBook.data : KANJI;
  const activeDatasetId: QuizDatasetId = datasetMode === 'irodori' ? irodoriBook.id : 'n4';
  const historyStorageKey =
    datasetMode === 'irodori' ? irodoriBook.quizHistoryKey : QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY;

  const [selectedChapters, setSelectedChapters] = useState<IrodoriChapterNumber[]>(() =>
    chapterFilter === 'all' ? [...IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS] : [chapterFilter]
  );

  const chapterCounts = useMemo(() => {
    const counts = {} as Record<IrodoriChapterNumber, number>;
    for (const ch of IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS) counts[ch] = 0;
    if (datasetMode === 'irodori') {
      for (const item of irodoriBook.data) {
        if (item.chapter !== undefined) {
          counts[item.chapter] = (counts[item.chapter] ?? 0) + 1;
        }
      }
    }
    return counts;
  }, [datasetMode, irodoriBook.data]);

  const activeChapterFilter: IrodoriChapterSelection | undefined = useMemo(() => {
    if (datasetMode !== 'irodori') return undefined;
    if (selectedChapters.length === IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS.length) return 'all';
    return selectedChapters;
  }, [datasetMode, selectedChapters]);

  const activeScopeLabel =
    datasetMode === 'irodori'
      ? formatChapterSelectionLabel(selectedChapters, IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS.length)
      : `Level ${LEVELS[levelFilter].label}`;

  const [stage, setStage] = useState<QuizStage>('setup');
  const [quizTypeChoice, setQuizTypeChoice] = useState<QuizTypeChoice>('random');
  const [customCount, setCustomCount] = useState('');
  const [questions, setQuestions] = useState<QuizQuestion[]>([]);
  const [pos, setPos] = useState(0);
  const [wrongList, setWrongList] = useState<QuizRecord[]>([]);
  const [rightList, setRightList] = useState<QuizRecord[]>([]);
  const [locked, setLocked] = useState(false);
  const [selectedIdx, setSelectedIdx] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [wrongFeedback, setWrongFeedback] = useState<QuizRecord | null>(null);
  const [showFurigana, setShowFurigana] = useState<boolean>(() => loadFuriganaPreference());

  const [localQuizHistory, setLocalQuizHistory] = useState<QuizSessionHistoryEntry[]>(
    () => controlledQuizHistory ?? loadQuizHistory(undefined, historyStorageKey)
  );
  const quizHistory = controlledQuizHistory ?? localQuizHistory;
  const [confirmClearHistory, setConfirmClearHistory] = useState(false);
  const [historyModalSessionId, setHistoryModalSessionId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [historyAnswerFilter, setHistoryAnswerFilter] = useState<'all' | 'wrong' | 'right'>('all');

  const timerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  const customInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const sessionSavedRef = useRef(false);
  const activeSessionMetaRef = useRef<{
    datasetId: QuizDatasetId;
    level: LevelFilter;
    scopeLabel: string;
    chapters?: IrodoriChapterNumber[];
    mode: QuizTypeChoice;
    storageKey: string;
  }>({
    datasetId: activeDatasetId,
    level: levelFilter,
    scopeLabel: activeScopeLabel,
    chapters: datasetMode === 'irodori' ? [...selectedChapters] : undefined,
    mode: quizTypeChoice,
    storageKey: historyStorageKey,
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (controlledQuizHistory === undefined) {
      setLocalQuizHistory(loadQuizHistory(undefined, historyStorageKey));
    }
    setConfirmClearHistory(false);
    setHistoryModalSessionId(null);
  }, [historyStorageKey, controlledQuizHistory]);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (timerRef.current) clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
    };
  }, []);

  // Reset back to setup and sync chapter selection when dataset mode, book, level filter, or sidebar chapter filter changes
  useEffect(() => {
    if (timerRef.current) clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
    setStage('setup');
    setLocked(false);
    setSelectedIdx(null);
    setWrongFeedback(null);
    setSelectedChapters(
      chapterFilter === 'all' ? [...IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS] : [chapterFilter]
    );
  }, [datasetMode, irodoriBook.id, levelFilter, chapterFilter]);

  const toggleFurigana = useCallback(() => {
    setShowFurigana(prev => {
      const next = !prev;
      saveFuriganaPreference(next);
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const toggleChapterSelection = useCallback((ch: IrodoriChapterNumber) => {
    setSelectedChapters(prev => {
      if (prev.includes(ch)) {
        return prev.filter(item => item !== ch);
      }
      return [...prev, ch].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    });
  }, []);

  // Persist completed quiz session once when transitioning to result stage
  useEffect(() => {
    if (stage !== 'result' || sessionSavedRef.current) return;
    const answered = rightList.length + wrongList.length;
    if (answered <= 0) return;
    sessionSavedRef.current = true;
    const { datasetId, level, scopeLabel, chapters, mode, storageKey } =
      activeSessionMetaRef.current;
    const answers = buildQuizAnswerSnapshots(rightList, wrongList);
    const input: RecordQuizSessionInput = {
      level,
      scopeLabel,
      ...(chapters ? { chapters } : {}),
      mode,
      correct: rightList.length,
      total: answered,
      answers,
    };
    if (onRecordQuizSession) {
      onRecordQuizSession(datasetId, input);
    } else {
      setLocalQuizHistory(prev => recordQuizSession(prev, input, undefined, storageKey));
    }
  }, [stage, rightList, wrongList, onRecordQuizSession]);

  const performanceStats = useMemo(
    () => computeQuizPerformanceStats(quizHistory),
    [quizHistory]
  );

  const kanjiCount = useMemo(
    () => getPoolIndices(levelFilter, activeDataset, activeChapterFilter).length,
    [levelFilter, activeDataset, activeChapterFilter]
  );
  const modelCount = getActiveTypeKeys(quizTypeChoice).length;
  const totalBank = useMemo(
    () => buildQuizBank(levelFilter, quizTypeChoice, activeDataset, activeChapterFilter).length,
    [levelFilter, quizTypeChoice, activeDataset, activeChapterFilter]
  );
  const hasKanaFilteredModels = totalBank < kanjiCount * modelCount;
  const disabledPresets = PRESET_COUNTS.filter(c => c > totalBank);

  const startQuiz = useCallback(
    (requested: number | 'all') => {
      if (timerRef.current) clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
      const session = buildQuizSession(
        levelFilter,
        quizTypeChoice,
        requested,
        activeDataset,
        Math.random,
        activeChapterFilter
      );
      if (!session.length) return;
      sessionSavedRef.current = false;
      activeSessionMetaRef.current = {
        datasetId: activeDatasetId,
        level: levelFilter,
        scopeLabel: activeScopeLabel,
        chapters: datasetMode === 'irodori' ? [...selectedChapters] : undefined,
        mode: quizTypeChoice,
        storageKey: historyStorageKey,
      };
      setConfirmClearHistory(false);
      setQuestions(session);
      setPos(0);
      setWrongList([]);
      setRightList([]);
      setLocked(false);
      setSelectedIdx(null);
      setWrongFeedback(null);
      setStage('run');
    },
    [
      activeDatasetId,
      datasetMode,
      selectedChapters,
      levelFilter,
      quizTypeChoice,
      activeDataset,
      activeChapterFilter,
      activeScopeLabel,
      historyStorageKey,
    ]
  );

  const advanceToNextQuestion = useCallback(() => {
    if (timerRef.current) clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
    setLocked(false);
    setSelectedIdx(null);
    setWrongFeedback(null);
    if (pos + 1 < questions.length) {
      setPos(p => p + 1);
    } else {
      setStage('result');
    }
  }, [pos, questions.length]);

  const handleAnswer = useCallback(
    (optObj: QuizOption, optIdx: number) => {
      if (locked) return;
      const q = questions[pos];
      if (!q) return;

      setLocked(true);
      setSelectedIdx(optIdx);

      const isCorrect = optObj.item.id === q.item.id;
      const rec: QuizRecord = {
        no: pos + 1,
        item: q.item,
        type: q.type,
        exMeta: q.exMeta,
        chosen: optObj.item,
        chosenLabel: optObj.label,
      };

      if (isCorrect) {
        setRightList(prev => [...prev, rec]);
        setWrongFeedback(null);
        timerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
          setLocked(false);
          setSelectedIdx(null);
          if (pos + 1 < questions.length) {
            setPos(p => p + 1);
          } else {
            setStage('result');
          }
        }, 420);
      } else {
        setWrongList(prev => [...prev, rec]);
        setWrongFeedback(rec);
      }
    },
    [locked, pos, questions]
  );

  // Keyboard shortcuts A-D or 1-4 while quiz is running, plus Enter/Space to continue after wrong feedback
  useEffect(() => {
    if (stage !== 'run' || modalOpen) return;

    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey) return;
      const tag = (e.target as HTMLElement | null)?.tagName;
      if (tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA' || tag === 'SELECT') return;

      if (wrongFeedback && (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ')) {
        e.preventDefault();
        advanceToNextQuestion();
        return;
      }

      let optIdx = -1;
      if (/^[1-4]$/.test(e.key)) {
        optIdx = Number(e.key) - 1;
      } else if (/^[a-dA-D]$/.test(e.key)) {
        optIdx = e.key.toUpperCase().charCodeAt(0) - 65;
      }

      if (optIdx >= 0 && !locked) {
        const q = questions[pos];
        const opt = q?.options[optIdx];
        if (opt) {
          e.preventDefault();
          handleAnswer(opt, optIdx);
        }
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [stage, modalOpen, locked, wrongFeedback, questions, pos, handleAnswer, advanceToNextQuestion]);

  const openAnswerHistoryModal = useCallback(
    (sessionId?: string) => {
      const targetId = sessionId ?? quizHistory[0]?.id ?? null;
      if (!targetId) return;
      setHistoryAnswerFilter('all');
      setHistoryModalSessionId(targetId);
    },
    [quizHistory]
  );

  const handleConfirmClearHistory = useCallback(() => {
    if (onClearQuizHistory) {
      onClearQuizHistory(activeDatasetId);
    } else {
      setLocalQuizHistory(clearQuizHistory(undefined, historyStorageKey));
    }
    setConfirmClearHistory(false);
    setHistoryModalSessionId(null);
  }, [onClearQuizHistory, activeDatasetId, historyStorageKey]);

  const handleFinishEarly = useCallback(() => {
    if (timerRef.current) clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
    setWrongFeedback(null);
    if (rightList.length + wrongList.length === 0) {
      setStage('setup');
    } else {
      setStage('result');
    }
  }, [rightList.length, wrongList.length]);

  const performancePanel = (
    <QuizStatsPanel
      datasetMode={datasetMode}
      irodoriBook={irodoriBook}
      quizHistory={quizHistory}
      performanceStats={performanceStats}
      cloudSyncEnabled={cloudSyncEnabled}
      quizHistorySyncing={quizHistorySyncing}
      confirmClearHistory={confirmClearHistory}
      historyModalSessionId={historyModalSessionId}
      historyAnswerFilter={historyAnswerFilter}
      onRequestClearHistory={setConfirmClearHistory}
      onConfirmClearHistory={handleConfirmClearHistory}
      onOpenAnswerHistory={openAnswerHistoryModal}
      onSelectHistorySession={id => {
        setHistoryModalSessionId(id);
        setHistoryAnswerFilter('all');
      }}
      onSelectHistoryFilter={setHistoryAnswerFilter}
      onCloseAnswerHistory={() => setHistoryModalSessionId(null)}
    />
  );

  if (stage === 'setup') {
    return (
      <QuizSetupView
        datasetMode={datasetMode}
        irodoriBook={irodoriBook}
        selectedChapters={selectedChapters}
        chapterCounts={chapterCounts}
        quizTypeChoice={quizTypeChoice}
        showFurigana={showFurigana}
        activeScopeLabel={activeScopeLabel}
        kanjiCount={kanjiCount}
        modelCount={modelCount}
        totalBank={totalBank}
        hasKanaFilteredModels={hasKanaFilteredModels}
        disabledPresets={disabledPresets}
        customCount={customCount}
        customInputRef={customInputRef}
        onSelectChapters={setSelectedChapters}
        onToggleChapter={toggleChapterSelection}
        onSelectQuizType={setQuizTypeChoice}
        onToggleFurigana={toggleFurigana}
        onCustomCountChange={setCustomCount}
        onStartQuiz={startQuiz}
        performancePanel={performancePanel}
      />
    );
  }

  if (stage === 'run') {
    const q = questions[pos];
    if (!q) return null;
    return (
      <QuizRunView
        datasetMode={datasetMode}
        question={q}
        pos={pos}
        totalQuestions={questions.length}
        locked={locked}
        selectedIdx={selectedIdx}
        wrongFeedback={wrongFeedback}
        showFurigana={showFurigana}
        onToggleFurigana={toggleFurigana}
        onFinishEarly={handleFinishEarly}
        onAnswer={handleAnswer}
        onAdvanceAfterWrong={advanceToNextQuestion}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <QuizResultView
      datasetMode={datasetMode}
      quizTypeChoice={quizTypeChoice}
      rightList={rightList}
      wrongList={wrongList}
      onPlayAgain={() => setStage('setup')}
      performancePanel={performancePanel}
    />
  );
}



================================================
FILE: src/components/RestoreModal.tsx
================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { AlertTriangle, FolderInput, Info, Merge, RefreshCw } from 'lucide-react';
import type { BackupParseResult } from '../types/kanji';

export interface PendingRestoreState {
  parsed: BackupParseResult;
  fileName: string;
}

interface RestoreModalProps {
  pending: PendingRestoreState | null;
  currentMasteredCount: number;
  currentHardCount: number;
  mergedMasteredCount: number;
  mergedHardCount: number;
  cleanBackupHardCount: number;
  onConfirm: (mode: 'merge' | 'replace') => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export function RestoreModal({
  pending,
  currentMasteredCount,
  currentHardCount,
  mergedMasteredCount,
  mergedHardCount,
  cleanBackupHardCount,
  onConfirm,
  onClose,
}: RestoreModalProps) {
  const modalBoxRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const mergeBtnRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const [confirmEmptyReplace, setConfirmEmptyReplace] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setConfirmEmptyReplace(false);
  }, [pending]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pending) return;

    const focusTimer = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      mergeBtnRef.current?.focus();
    });

    const getFocusables = (): HTMLElement[] => {
      const box = modalBoxRef.current;
      if (!box) return [];
      return Array.from(
        box.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
          'button:not([disabled]), [href], input:not([disabled]), select:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled]), [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])'
        )
      ).filter(el => el.offsetParent !== null);
    };

    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        onClose();
        return;
      }

      const focusables = getFocusables();
      if (!focusables.length) return;
      const active = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;
      const idx = active ? focusables.indexOf(active) : -1;

      if (e.key === 'Tab') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (e.shiftKey) {
          const prevIdx = idx <= 0 ? focusables.length - 1 : idx - 1;
          focusables[prevIdx]?.focus();
        } else {
          const nextIdx = idx === -1 || idx >= focusables.length - 1 ? 0 : idx + 1;
          focusables[nextIdx]?.focus();
        }
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowDown' || e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
        e.preventDefault();
        const nextIdx = idx === -1 || idx >= focusables.length - 1 ? 0 : idx + 1;
        focusables[nextIdx]?.focus();
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp' || e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
        e.preventDefault();
        const prevIdx = idx <= 0 ? focusables.length - 1 : idx - 1;
        focusables[prevIdx]?.focus();
      }
    };

    const handleFocusIn = (e: FocusEvent) => {
      const box = modalBoxRef.current;
      if (!box) return;
      if (!box.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        const focusables = getFocusables();
        focusables[0]?.focus();
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown, true);
    document.addEventListener('focusin', handleFocusIn, true);

    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(focusTimer);
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown, true);
      document.removeEventListener('focusin', handleFocusIn, true);
    };
  }, [pending, onClose]);

  if (!pending) return null;

  const { parsed, fileName } = pending;
  const { impMastered, rejectedIds, exportedAt, formatType, isEmptyBackup } = parsed;
  const hasCurrentProgress = currentMasteredCount > 0 || currentHardCount > 0;

  const dateLabel =
    exportedAt.length >= 10
      ? `Backup ${exportedAt.slice(0, 10)}`
      : formatType !== 'v1'
        ? 'Format backup versi lama'
        : fileName;

  const handleReplaceClick = () => {
    if (isEmptyBackup && hasCurrentProgress && !confirmEmptyReplace) {
      setConfirmEmptyReplace(true);
      return;
    }
    onConfirm('replace');
  };

  return (
    <div
      id="restoreModal"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby="restoreModalTitle"
      aria-describedby="restoreModalDesc"
      onClick={e => {
        if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose();
      }}
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/55 dark:bg-slate-950/75 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4"
    >
      <div
        ref={modalBoxRef}
        className="w-full max-w-md bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <FolderInput className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
          <h2 id="restoreModalTitle" className="text-lg font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
            Pulihkan Data Progres
          </h2>
        </div>
        <p id="restoreModalDesc" className="mt-1.5 text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
          Pilih cara memulihkan data dari file backup agar progres belajar yang sudah ada tidak
          hilang tanpa sengaja.
        </p>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 mt-4">
          <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/80 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 p-3">
            <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Progres Saat Ini</div>
            <div className="mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 font-mono-num">
              {currentMasteredCount} hafal · {currentHardCount} sulit
            </div>
            <div className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Tersimpan di browser</div>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-xl bg-indigo-50/70 dark:bg-indigo-950/50 border border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-800/80 p-3">
            <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300">File Backup</div>
            <div className="mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 font-mono-num">
              {impMastered.length} hafal · {cleanBackupHardCount} sulit
            </div>
            <div className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-indigo-700/80 dark:text-indigo-300/90 truncate" title={dateLabel}>
              {dateLabel}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {isEmptyBackup ? (
          <div
            id="rcEmptyWarn"
            role="alert"
            className={`mt-3 rounded-xl border p-3 text-xs leading-relaxed font-medium flex items-start gap-2 ${
              hasCurrentProgress
                ? 'bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/50 border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-800/80 text-rose-900 dark:text-rose-200'
                : 'bg-sky-50 dark:bg-sky-950/50 border-sky-200 dark:border-sky-800/80 text-sky-900 dark:text-sky-200'
            }`}
          >
            {hasCurrentProgress ? (
              <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 mt-0.5 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400" aria-hidden="true" />
            ) : (
              <Info className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 mt-0.5 text-sky-600 dark:text-sky-400" aria-hidden="true" />
            )}
            <span>
              {hasCurrentProgress
                ? `Peringatan Backup Kosong: File ini berisi 0 kanji hafal dan 0 kanji sulit. Jika memilih "Timpa Semua", seluruh progres saat ini (${currentMasteredCount} hafal, ${currentHardCount} sulit) akan terhapus menjadi 0.`
                : 'File backup ini kosong (0 kanji hafal dan 0 kanji sulit). Tidak ada data progres baru yang akan ditambahkan.'}
            </span>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {rejectedIds.length > 0 ? (
          <div
            id="rcRejectedWarn"
            role="status"
            className="mt-2.5 rounded-xl border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-800/80 bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/50 p-3 text-xs leading-relaxed font-medium text-amber-900 dark:text-amber-200 flex items-start gap-2"
          >
            <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 mt-0.5 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400" aria-hidden="true" />
            <span>
              {rejectedIds.length} ID data tidak dikenali dan akan diabaikan:{' '}
              {rejectedIds.slice(0, 5).join(', ')}
              {rejectedIds.length > 5 ? ` (+${rejectedIds.length - 5} lainnya)` : ''}.
            </span>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {confirmEmptyReplace ? (
          <div
            role="alert"
            className="mt-3 rounded-xl border border-rose-300 dark:border-rose-700 bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/60 p-3 text-xs text-rose-950 dark:text-rose-100"
          >
            <p className="font-bold">Konfirmasi Timpa Progres</p>
            <p className="mt-1 leading-relaxed">
              Anda yakin ingin menimpa {currentMasteredCount} hafal &amp; {currentHardCount} sulit
              dengan backup kosong (menjadi 0)? Klik tombol &ldquo;Konfirmasi Hapus &amp; Timpa&rdquo;
              di bawah untuk melanjutkan.
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2.5 mt-5">
          <button
            ref={mergeBtnRef}
            type="button"
            id="btnRestoreMerge"
            onClick={() => onConfirm('merge')}
            className="w-full rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white px-4 py-3 text-left transition-colors focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 cursor-pointer"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-sm font-bold">
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Merge className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                Gabungkan (Merge)
              </span>
              <span className="text-xs font-medium text-emerald-100 font-mono-num whitespace-nowrap">
                → {mergedMasteredCount} hafal · {mergedHardCount} sulit
              </span>
            </div>
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-emerald-50/90 leading-snug">
              Simpan progres saat ini dan tambahkan data dari file backup (aman).
            </p>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            id="btnRestoreReplace"
            onClick={handleReplaceClick}
            className={`w-full rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 text-left transition-colors focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 cursor-pointer ${
              confirmEmptyReplace
                ? 'bg-rose-600 border-rose-700 text-white hover:bg-rose-700'
                : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-800/80 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 hover:bg-rose-50 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/50 hover:border-rose-300 dark:hover:border-rose-700'
            }`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-sm font-bold">
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                {confirmEmptyReplace ? 'Konfirmasi Hapus & Timpa Semua' : 'Timpa Semua (Replace)'}
              </span>
              <span
                className={`text-xs font-semibold font-mono-num whitespace-nowrap ${
                  confirmEmptyReplace ? 'text-rose-100' : 'text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400'
                }`}
              >
                → {impMastered.length} hafal · {cleanBackupHardCount} sulit
              </span>
            </div>
            <p
              className={`mt-1 text-xs leading-snug ${
                confirmEmptyReplace ? 'text-rose-50' : 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300'
              }`}
            >
              Hapus progres saat ini dan ganti seluruhnya dengan isi file backup.
            </p>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            id="btnRestoreCancel"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="w-full rounded-xl px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-slate-50 transition-colors focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 cursor-pointer"
          >
            Batal
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



================================================
FILE: src/components/Sidebar.tsx
================================================
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { BookOpen, FileText, HelpCircle, Layers } from 'lucide-react';
import { IRODORI_BOOKS } from '../data/irodoriBooks';
import { IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS } from '../data/irodoriShared';
import { KANJI, LEVEL_KEYS, LEVELS } from '../data/kanjiData';
import type {
  AppDatasetMode,
  AppTab,
  IrodoriBookConfig,
  IrodoriChapterFilter,
  IrodoriChapterNumber,
  LevelFilter,
} from '../types/kanji';

interface SidebarProps {
  datasetMode: AppDatasetMode;
  irodoriBook?: IrodoriBookConfig;
  activeTab: AppTab;
  levelFilter: LevelFilter;
  chapterFilter: IrodoriChapterFilter;
  onSelectTab: (tab: AppTab) => void;
  onSelectLevel: (level: LevelFilter) => void;
  onSelectChapter: (chapter: IrodoriChapterFilter) => void;
}

export function Sidebar({
  datasetMode,
  irodoriBook = IRODORI_BOOKS.dasar1,
  activeTab,
  levelFilter,
  chapterFilter,
  onSelectTab,
  onSelectLevel,
  onSelectChapter,
}: SidebarProps) {
  const levelKeys: LevelFilter[] = ['all', ...LEVEL_KEYS];
  const counts: Record<LevelFilter, number> = useMemo(
    () => ({
      all: KANJI.length,
      easy: KANJI.filter(it => it.dif === 'easy').length,
      medium: KANJI.filter(it => it.dif === 'medium').length,
      hard: KANJI.filter(it => it.dif === 'hard').length,
    }),
    []
  );

  const chapterCounts = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<IrodoriChapterNumber, number>();
    for (const ch of IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS) map.set(ch, 0);
    for (const it of irodoriBook.data) {
      if (it.chapter) {
        map.set(it.chapter, (map.get(it.chapter) ?? 0) + 1);
      }
    }
    return map;
  }, [irodoriBook.data]);

  const topicGroups = useMemo(() => {
    const groups: { topicJp: string; topicId: string; chapters: IrodoriChapterNumber[] }[] = [];
    for (const ch of IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS) {
      const meta = irodoriBook.chapters[ch];
      const existing = groups.find(g => g.topicId === meta.topicId);
      if (existing) {
        existing.chapters.push(ch);
      } else {
        groups.push({ topicJp: meta.topicJp, topicId: meta.topicId, chapters: [ch] });
      }
    }
    return groups;
  }, [irodoriBook.chapters]);

  const tabs: { key: AppTab; label: string; icon: typeof Layers }[] = [
    { key: 'card', label: 'Kartu', icon: Layers },
    { key: 'list', label: 'Daftar', icon: BookOpen },
    { key: 'quiz', label: 'Kuis', icon: HelpCircle },
  ];

  const activeChapterMeta =
    chapterFilter === 'all' ? null : irodoriBook.chapters[chapterFilter];
  const hasIrodoriData = irodoriBook.data.length > 0;

  return (
    <aside className="lg:sticky lg:top-22 lg:self-start space-y-4">
      <nav
        aria-label="Tampilan utama"
        role="tablist"
        className="grid grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-1 gap-1.5 bg-slate-200/70 dark:bg-slate-800/80 p-1.5 rounded-xl"
      >
        {tabs.map(({ key, label, icon: Icon }) => {
          const active = activeTab === key;
          return (
            <button
              key={key}
              type="button"
              role="tab"
              aria-selected={active}
              onClick={() => onSelectTab(key)}
              className={`min-h-[44px] px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold inline-flex items-center justify-center lg:justify-start gap-2 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 ${
                active
                  ? 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300 shadow-xs'
                  : 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-slate-50 hover:bg-white/60 dark:hover:bg-slate-700/60'
              }`}
            >
              <Icon className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
              <span>{label}</span>
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </nav>

      {datasetMode === 'n4' ? (
        <div>
          <div
            role="tablist"
            aria-label="Level kesulitan"
            className="flex lg:flex-col gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1 lg:pb-0"
          >
            {levelKeys.map(key => {
              const active = levelFilter === key;
              return (
                <button
                  key={key}
                  type="button"
                  role="tab"
                  aria-selected={active}
                  onClick={() => onSelectLevel(key)}
                  className={`min-h-[42px] px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border text-sm font-semibold inline-flex items-center justify-between gap-2.5 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 ${
                    active
                      ? 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-900 text-white dark:bg-indigo-600 dark:border-indigo-500 dark:text-white'
                      : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800'
                  }`}
                >
                  <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2">
                    {key !== 'all' ? (
                      <span
                        aria-hidden="true"
                        className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full shrink-0"
                        style={{ backgroundColor: LEVELS[key].dotColor }}
                      />
                    ) : null}
                    <span>{LEVELS[key].label}</span>
                  </span>
                  <span
                    className={`text-xs font-mono-num ${
                      active ? 'text-slate-300 dark:text-indigo-100' : 'text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {counts[key]}
                  </span>
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          <p className="mt-3 text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 leading-relaxed px-1">
            {LEVELS[levelFilter].desc}
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : !hasIrodoriData ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 p-3.5 space-y-1">
          <div className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
            {irodoriBook.label} ({irodoriBook.cefrLevel})
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
            Belum ada data bab atau kosakata untuk buku ini.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {/* Mobile: horizontal scrollable chapter strip + quick select dropdown */}
          <div className="lg:hidden space-y-2.5">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <label htmlFor="irodoriChapterSelect" className="sr-only">
                Pilih Bab Irodori
              </label>
              <select
                id="irodoriChapterSelect"
                value={chapterFilter === 'all' ? 'all' : String(chapterFilter)}
                onChange={e => {
                  const val = e.target.value;
                  onSelectChapter(
                    val === 'all' ? 'all' : (Number(val) as IrodoriChapterNumber)
                  );
                }}
                className="w-full min-h-[42px] px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-teal-500"
              >
                <option value="all">Semua Bab (1–18 · {irodoriBook.data.length} kosakata)</option>
                {IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS.map(ch => {
                  const meta = irodoriBook.chapters[ch];
                  return (
                    <option key={ch} value={String(ch)}>
                      {meta.label}: {meta.idTitle} ({chapterCounts.get(ch) ?? 0})
                    </option>
                  );
                })}
              </select>
            </div>

            <div
              role="tablist"
              aria-label="Filter Bab Irodori"
              className="flex gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto pb-1"
            >
              <button
                type="button"
                role="tab"
                aria-selected={chapterFilter === 'all'}
                onClick={() => onSelectChapter('all')}
                className={`min-h-[38px] px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border text-xs font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                  chapterFilter === 'all'
                    ? 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-900 text-white dark:bg-teal-600 dark:border-teal-500 dark:text-white'
                    : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200'
                }`}
              >
                <span>Semua</span>
                <span className="font-mono-num opacity-80">{irodoriBook.data.length}</span>
              </button>
              {IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS.map(ch => {
                const active = chapterFilter === ch;
                const meta = irodoriBook.chapters[ch];
                return (
                  <button
                    key={ch}
                    type="button"
                    role="tab"
                    aria-selected={active}
                    onClick={() => onSelectChapter(ch)}
                    className={`min-h-[38px] px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border text-xs font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                      active
                        ? 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-900 text-white dark:bg-teal-600 dark:border-teal-500 dark:text-white'
                        : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span
                      aria-hidden="true"
                      className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full shrink-0"
                      style={{ backgroundColor: meta.dotColor }}
                    />
                    <span>{meta.shortLabel}</span>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Desktop: grouped chapter list per topic */}
          <div className="hidden lg:block space-y-2.5">
            <button
              type="button"
              id="btnIrodoriAllChapters"
              onClick={() => onSelectChapter('all')}
              className={`w-full min-h-[42px] px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border text-sm font-semibold inline-flex items-center justify-between gap-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 ${
                chapterFilter === 'all'
                  ? 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-900 text-white dark:bg-teal-600 dark:border-teal-500 dark:text-white'
                  : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800'
              }`}
            >
              <span>Semua Bab (1–18)</span>
              <span
                className={`text-xs font-mono-num ${
                  chapterFilter === 'all' ? 'text-slate-300 dark:text-teal-100' : 'text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400'
                }`}
              >
                {irodoriBook.data.length}
              </span>
            </button>

            <div
              role="tablist"
              aria-label="Daftar Bab Irodori"
              className="max-h-[calc(100vh-22rem)] overflow-y-auto pr-1 space-y-3"
            >
              {topicGroups.map(group => (
                <div key={group.topicId} className="space-y-1.5">
                  <div className="px-1 text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                    {group.topicId} · <span className="font-jp">{group.topicJp}</span>
                  </div>
                  {group.chapters.map(ch => {
                    const active = chapterFilter === ch;
                    const meta = irodoriBook.chapters[ch];
                    return (
                      <button
                        key={ch}
                        type="button"
                        role="tab"
                        aria-selected={active}
                        onClick={() => onSelectChapter(ch)}
                        className={`w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border text-left transition-colors flex items-start justify-between gap-2 cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 ${
                          active
                            ? 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-900 text-white dark:bg-teal-600 dark:border-teal-500 dark:text-white'
                            : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <span className="flex items-start gap-2 min-w-0">
                          <span
                            aria-hidden="true"
                            className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full shrink-0 mt-1.5"
                            style={{ backgroundColor: meta.dotColor }}
                          />
                          <span className="min-w-0">
                            <span className="block text-xs font-bold">
                              {meta.label} · {meta.idTitle}
                            </span>
                            <span
                              className={`block text-[11px] font-jp truncate ${
                                active ? 'text-slate-300 dark:text-teal-100' : 'text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400'
                              }`}
                            >
                              {meta.jpTitle}
                            </span>
                          </span>
                        </span>
                        <span
                          className={`text-xs font-mono-num shrink-0 mt-0.5 ${
                            active ? 'text-slate-300 dark:text-teal-100' : 'text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {chapterCounts.get(ch) ?? 0}
                        </span>
                      </button>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 p-3 space-y-1.5">
            {activeChapterMeta ? (
              <>
                <div className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                  {activeChapterMeta.label}: {activeChapterMeta.idTitle}
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs font-jp text-teal-700 dark:text-teal-300 font-semibold">
                  {activeChapterMeta.jpTitle}
                </div>
                <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                  Topik: {activeChapterMeta.topicId} ({activeChapterMeta.topicJp})
                </div>
              </>
            ) : (
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
                Menampilkan seluruh 18 bab {irodoriBook.label} ({irodoriBook.data.length} kosakata
                &amp; kanji).
              </p>
            )}
            {irodoriBook.pdfPath ? (
              <a
                href={irodoriBook.pdfPath}
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
                className="pt-1 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-teal-700 dark:text-teal-300 hover:text-teal-800 dark:hover:text-teal-200 hover:underline"
              >
                <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                <span>Buka Daftar Kosakata Resmi (PDF)</span>
              </a>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </aside>
  );
}



================================================
FILE: src/components/ToastBanner.tsx
================================================
import { AlertTriangle, CheckCircle2, Info, X, XCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import type { ToastMessage } from '../types/kanji';

interface ToastBannerProps {
  toast: ToastMessage | null;
  onDismiss: () => void;
}

export function ToastBanner({ toast, onDismiss }: ToastBannerProps) {
  if (!toast) return null;

  const toneStyles: Record<ToastMessage['tone'], string> = {
    success: 'bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/95 border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800 text-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-100',
    error: 'bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/95 border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-800 text-rose-950 dark:text-rose-100',
    warning: 'bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/95 border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-800 text-amber-950 dark:text-amber-100',
    info: 'bg-sky-50 dark:bg-sky-950/95 border-sky-200 dark:border-sky-800 text-sky-950 dark:text-sky-100',
  };

  const Icon =
    toast.tone === 'success'
      ? CheckCircle2
      : toast.tone === 'error'
        ? XCircle
        : toast.tone === 'warning'
          ? AlertTriangle
          : Info;

  return (
    <div
      role={toast.tone === 'error' || toast.tone === 'warning' ? 'alert' : 'status'}
      aria-live="polite"
      className="fixed top-16 sm:top-auto sm:bottom-4 right-4 left-4 sm:left-auto sm:max-w-md z-50"
    >
      <div
        className={`flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border p-4 shadow-lg ${toneStyles[toast.tone]}`}
      >
        <Icon className="w-5 h-5 shrink-0 mt-0.5" aria-hidden="true" />
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 text-sm">
          <p className="font-semibold leading-snug">{toast.title}</p>
          {toast.detail ? (
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs opacity-90 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line">
              {toast.detail}
            </p>
          ) : null}
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onDismiss}
          aria-label="Tutup notifikasi"
          className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-black/5 dark:hover:bg-white/10 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
        >
          <X className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
