FILE: src/components/quiz/QuizAnswerHistoryModal.tsx
================================================
import { useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
import { CheckCircle2, History, X, XCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { LEVELS } from '../../data/kanjiData';
import type {
  AppDatasetMode,
  IrodoriBookConfig,
  QuizSessionHistoryEntry,
} from '../../types/kanji';
import {
  MODE_SHORT_LABEL,
  formatQuizTimestamp,
  getQuizModelLabel,
} from './quizHelpers';

interface QuizAnswerHistoryModalProps {
  datasetMode: AppDatasetMode;
  irodoriBook: IrodoriBookConfig;
  quizHistory: QuizSessionHistoryEntry[];
  activeSession: QuizSessionHistoryEntry;
  answerFilter: 'all' | 'wrong' | 'right';
  cloudSyncEnabled: boolean;
  quizHistorySyncing: boolean;
  onSelectSession: (sessionId: string) => void;
  onSelectFilter: (filter: 'all' | 'wrong' | 'right') => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export function QuizAnswerHistoryModal({
  datasetMode,
  irodoriBook,
  quizHistory,
  activeSession,
  answerFilter,
  cloudSyncEnabled,
  quizHistorySyncing,
  onSelectSession,
  onSelectFilter,
  onClose,
}: QuizAnswerHistoryModalProps) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        onClose();
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [onClose]);

  const activeSessionAnswers = activeSession.answers ?? [];

  const filteredHistoryAnswers = useMemo(() => {
    if (answerFilter === 'wrong') return activeSessionAnswers.filter(a => !a.correct);
    if (answerFilter === 'right') return activeSessionAnswers.filter(a => a.correct);
    return activeSessionAnswers;
  }, [activeSessionAnswers, answerFilter]);

  const wrongCount = useMemo(
    () => activeSessionAnswers.filter(a => !a.correct).length,
    [activeSessionAnswers]
  );
  const rightCount = useMemo(
    () => activeSessionAnswers.filter(a => a.correct).length,
    [activeSessionAnswers]
  );

  return (
    <div
      id="quizAnswerHistoryModal"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby="quizAnswerHistoryTitle"
      onClick={e => {
        if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose();
      }}
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/50 dark:bg-slate-950/75 backdrop-blur-[1px] flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-5"
    >
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 shadow-2xl w-full max-w-2xl max-h-[85vh] flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        <div className="px-5 py-4 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <h4
              id="quizAnswerHistoryTitle"
              className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 flex items-center gap-2"
            >
              <History className="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
              <span>Riwayat Jawaban Kuis</span>
            </h4>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
              {datasetMode === 'irodori' ? irodoriBook.label : 'JLPT N4 Kanji'} ·{' '}
              {quizHistory.length} sesi tersimpan
              {cloudSyncEnabled
                ? quizHistorySyncing
                  ? ' · menyinkronkan...'
                  : ' · sinkron cloud aktif'
                : ''}
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            id="btnCloseQuizAnswerHistory"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Tutup riwayat jawaban"
            className="min-h-[36px] min-w-[36px] rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-200 inline-flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {quizHistory.length > 1 ? (
          <div className="px-5 py-3 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 bg-slate-50/70 dark:bg-slate-800/60">
            <label
              htmlFor="quizHistorySessionSelect"
              className="block text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1.5"
            >
              Pilih Sesi Kuis
            </label>
            <select
              id="quizHistorySessionSelect"
              value={activeSession.id}
              onChange={e => onSelectSession(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full min-h-[38px] px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500"
            >
              {quizHistory.map((entry, idx) => (
                <option key={entry.id} value={entry.id}>
                  Sesi #{quizHistory.length - idx} · {MODE_SHORT_LABEL[entry.mode]} ·{' '}
                  {entry.scopeLabel ?? `Level ${LEVELS[entry.level].label}`} · {entry.correct}/
                  {entry.total} ({entry.score}%) · {formatQuizTimestamp(entry.completedAt)}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        <div className="px-5 py-3 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
            <strong className="font-jp text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
              {MODE_SHORT_LABEL[activeSession.mode]}
            </strong>{' '}
            · {activeSession.scopeLabel ?? `Level ${LEVELS[activeSession.level].label}`} ·{' '}
            <strong className="font-mono-num text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-400">{activeSession.score}%</strong>{' '}
            <span className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 font-mono-num">
              ({activeSession.correct}/{activeSession.total} benar)
            </span>
          </div>

          {activeSessionAnswers.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5" role="group" aria-label="Filter jawaban">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onSelectFilter('all')}
                aria-pressed={answerFilter === 'all'}
                className={`min-h-[30px] px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer ${
                  answerFilter === 'all'
                    ? 'bg-indigo-600 border-indigo-600 text-white'
                    : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700'
                }`}
              >
                Semua ({activeSessionAnswers.length})
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onSelectFilter('wrong')}
                aria-pressed={answerFilter === 'wrong'}
                className={`min-h-[30px] px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer ${
                  answerFilter === 'wrong'
                    ? 'bg-rose-600 border-rose-600 text-white'
                    : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-rose-50 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/60 hover:text-rose-700 dark:hover:text-rose-300'
                }`}
              >
                Salah ({wrongCount})
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onSelectFilter('right')}
                aria-pressed={answerFilter === 'right'}
                className={`min-h-[30px] px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer ${
                  answerFilter === 'right'
                    ? 'bg-emerald-600 border-emerald-600 text-white'
                    : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-emerald-50 dark:hover:bg-emerald-950/60 hover:text-emerald-700 dark:hover:text-emerald-300'
                }`}
              >
                Benar ({rightCount})
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : null}
        </div>

        <div className="p-5 overflow-y-auto space-y-2.5 flex-1" id="quizAnswerHistoryList">
          {activeSessionAnswers.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 leading-relaxed text-center py-8">
              Detail jawaban per soal belum tersimpan untuk sesi lama ini. Selesaikan sesi kuis
              baru untuk melihat riwayat jawaban lengkap.
            </p>
          ) : filteredHistoryAnswers.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 leading-relaxed text-center py-8">
              {answerFilter === 'wrong'
                ? 'Tidak ada jawaban salah pada sesi ini. Sempurna!'
                : 'Tidak ada jawaban benar pada filter ini.'}
            </p>
          ) : (
            filteredHistoryAnswers.map(ans => {
              const showReading = ans.itemR && ans.itemR !== ans.itemK;
              return (
                <div
                  key={`${activeSession.id}-${ans.no}`}
                  className={`rounded-xl border p-3.5 text-sm leading-relaxed ${
                    ans.correct
                      ? 'border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800/70 bg-emerald-50/40 dark:bg-emerald-950/35'
                      : 'border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-800/70 bg-rose-50/40 dark:bg-rose-950/35'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs font-semibold">
                    <span className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                      No. {ans.no} · {getQuizModelLabel(ans.type, datasetMode)}
                    </span>
                    <span
                      className={
                        ans.correct
                          ? 'text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 inline-flex items-center gap-1'
                          : 'text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400 inline-flex items-center gap-1'
                      }
                    >
                      {ans.correct ? (
                        <>
                          <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                          <span>Benar</span>
                        </>
                      ) : (
                        <>
                          <XCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                          <span>Salah</span>
                        </>
                      )}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  {ans.type === 'ex2k' && ans.ex ? (
                    <div className="mt-1 space-y-1">
                      <p className="font-jp font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">{ans.ex}</p>
                      <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200">
                        Benar:{' '}
                        <strong className="text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 font-jp">{ans.expectedLabel}</strong> (
                        {ans.itemR}, {ans.itemM})
                        {!ans.correct ? (
                          <>
                            {' '}
                            · Jawabanmu:{' '}
                            <strong className="text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400 font-jp">{ans.chosenLabel}</strong>
                            {ans.chosenM ? ` (${ans.chosenM})` : ''}
                          </>
                        ) : null}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <p className="mt-1 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">
                      <strong className="font-jp text-base text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">{ans.itemK}</strong>
                      {showReading ? ` (${ans.itemR})` : ''} —{' '}
                      <span className="text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 font-semibold">{ans.itemM}</span>
                      {!ans.correct ? (
                        <>
                          {' '}
                          · Jawabanmu:{' '}
                          <span className="text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400 font-semibold">{ans.chosenLabel}</span>
                          {ans.type !== 'k2m' && ans.chosenM ? ` (${ans.chosenM})` : ''}
                        </>
                      ) : null}
                    </p>
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/quiz/quizHelpers.tsx
================================================
import type { AppDatasetMode, QuizTypeChoice } from '../../types/kanji';
import { QTYPES, splitFuriganaParts } from '../../utils/quizEngine';

export const PRESET_COUNTS = [10, 20, 50, 100, 500] as const;

export const QUIZ_TYPE_OPTIONS: {
  key: QuizTypeChoice;
  short: string;
  label: string;
  fullWidth?: boolean;
}[] = [
  { key: 'random', short: 'Acak 4', label: 'Acak (4 Model Utama)' },
  { key: 'random5', short: 'Acak 5', label: 'Acak Semua (+Kalimat)' },
  { key: 'k2r', short: '漢→あ', label: 'Kanji → Hiragana' },
  { key: 'k2m', short: '漢→ID', label: 'Kanji → Arti' },
  { key: 'm2k', short: 'ID→漢', label: 'Arti → Kanji' },
  { key: 'r2k', short: 'あ→漢', label: 'Hiragana → Kanji' },
  {
    key: 'ex2k',
    short: '📖→漢',
    label: 'Contoh Kalimat → Kanji (Sesuai Konjugasi)',
    fullWidth: true,
  },
];

export const MODE_SHORT_LABEL: Record<QuizTypeChoice, string> = {
  random: 'Acak 4',
  random5: 'Acak 5',
  k2r: '漢→あ',
  k2m: '漢→ID',
  m2k: 'ID→漢',
  r2k: 'あ→漢',
  ex2k: '📖→漢',
};

export function getQuizModelLabel(
  typeKey: keyof typeof QTYPES,
  datasetMode: AppDatasetMode
): string {
  if (datasetMode === 'irodori') {
    if (typeKey === 'k2m') return 'Kosakata → Arti';
    if (typeKey === 'm2k') return 'Arti → Kosakata';
    if (typeKey === 'ex2k') return 'Kalimat → Kosakata';
  }
  return QTYPES[typeKey].label;
}

const QUIZ_FURIGANA_STORAGE_KEY = 'n4kanji_quiz_furigana_v1';

export function loadFuriganaPreference(): boolean {
  try {
    return (
      typeof window !== 'undefined' &&
      window.localStorage.getItem(QUIZ_FURIGANA_STORAGE_KEY) === '1'
    );
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export function saveFuriganaPreference(enabled: boolean): void {
  try {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      window.localStorage.setItem(QUIZ_FURIGANA_STORAGE_KEY, enabled ? '1' : '0');
    }
  } catch {
    // Non-fatal if storage is blocked
  }
}

export function renderRubyText(surface: string, reading: string, rtClassName: string) {
  const parts = splitFuriganaParts(surface, reading);
  if (!parts) return surface;
  return (
    <>
      {parts.prefix}
      <ruby>
        {parts.base}
        <rt className={rtClassName}>{parts.ruby}</rt>
      </ruby>
      {parts.suffix}
    </>
  );
}

export function formatQuizTimestamp(iso: string): string {
  const date = new Date(iso);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return '';
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit',
  }).format(date);
}

FILE: src/components/quiz/QuizResultView.tsx
================================================
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { CheckCircle2, RotateCcw, XCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import type { AppDatasetMode, QuizRecord, QuizTypeChoice } from '../../types/kanji';
import { QTYPES, calculateQuizScore } from '../../utils/quizEngine';
import { getQuizModelLabel } from './quizHelpers';

interface QuizResultViewProps {
  datasetMode: AppDatasetMode;
  quizTypeChoice: QuizTypeChoice;
  rightList: QuizRecord[];
  wrongList: QuizRecord[];
  onPlayAgain: () => void;
  performancePanel: ReactNode;
}

export function QuizResultView({
  datasetMode,
  quizTypeChoice,
  rightList,
  wrongList,
  onPlayAgain,
  performancePanel,
}: QuizResultViewProps) {
  const answered = rightList.length + wrongList.length;
  const score = calculateQuizScore(rightList.length, answered);
  const modeSummary =
    quizTypeChoice === 'random'
      ? 'Acak 4 Model Utama'
      : quizTypeChoice === 'random5'
        ? 'Acak 5 Model (+Kalimat)'
        : getQuizModelLabel(quizTypeChoice, datasetMode);

  let msg = 'Jangan menyerah — ulangi kartunya lalu coba lagi!';
  if (score === 100) msg = 'Sempurna! Semua benar.';
  else if (score >= 80) msg = 'Hebat! Sedikit lagi sempurna.';
  else if (score >= 60) msg = 'Lumayan! Terus latihan ya.';
  else if (score >= 40) msg = 'Masih perlu dipelajari lagi, semangat!';

  return (
    <section aria-label="Hasil Kuis" className="max-w-4xl space-y-6">
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 p-8 text-center">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Skor Akhir</p>
        <div className="mt-2 text-5xl font-extrabold text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-400 font-mono-num" id="quizScore">
          {score}
        </div>
        <p className="mt-2 text-base font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">{msg}</p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 font-mono-num">
          {rightList.length} benar · {wrongList.length} salah (dari {answered} soal) · {modeSummary}
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6 items-start">
        {wrongList.length > 0 ? (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400 flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <XCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
              <span>Perlu Diperbaiki ({wrongList.length})</span>
            </h3>
            <div className="space-y-2.5 max-h-[420px] overflow-y-auto pr-1">
              {wrongList.map(rec => (
                <div
                  key={`w-${rec.no}`}
                  className="rounded-xl border border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-800/70 bg-rose-50/40 dark:bg-rose-950/35 p-3.5 text-sm leading-relaxed"
                >
                  <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                    No. {rec.no} · {getQuizModelLabel(rec.type, datasetMode)}
                  </div>
                  {rec.type === 'ex2k' ? (
                    <div className="mt-1 space-y-1">
                      <p className="font-jp font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">{rec.item.ex}</p>
                      <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200">
                        Benar:{' '}
                        <strong className="text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 font-jp">
                          {rec.exMeta?.targetSurface ?? rec.item.k}
                        </strong>{' '}
                        ({rec.item.r}, {rec.item.m}) · Jawabanmu:{' '}
                        <strong className="text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400 font-jp">{rec.chosenLabel}</strong> (
                        {rec.chosen.m})
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <p className="mt-1 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">
                      <strong className="font-jp text-base text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">{rec.item.k}</strong>
                      {rec.item.r && rec.item.r !== rec.item.k ? ` (${rec.item.r})` : ''} —{' '}
                      <span className="text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 font-semibold">{rec.item.m}</span> ·
                      Jawabanmu:{' '}
                      <span className="text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400 font-semibold">
                        {rec.type === 'k2r'
                          ? rec.chosen.r
                          : rec.type === 'k2m'
                            ? rec.chosen.m
                            : rec.chosen.k}
                      </span>
                    </p>
                  )}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {rightList.length > 0 ? (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
              <span>Jawaban Benar ({rightList.length})</span>
            </h3>
            <div className="space-y-2.5 max-h-[420px] overflow-y-auto pr-1">
              {rightList.map(rec => {
                const ansShow =
                  rec.type === 'ex2k' &&
                  rec.exMeta &&
                  rec.exMeta.targetSurface !== rec.item.k
                    ? `${rec.exMeta.targetSurface} (${rec.item.k})`
                    : rec.item.k;
                const showReading = rec.item.r && rec.item.r !== ansShow;
                return (
                  <div
                    key={`r-${rec.no}`}
                    className="rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800/70 bg-emerald-50/40 dark:bg-emerald-950/35 p-3.5 text-sm"
                  >
                    <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                      No. {rec.no} · {QTYPES[rec.type].short}
                    </span>
                    <div className="mt-0.5 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                      <strong className="font-jp text-base text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">{ansShow}</strong>
                      {showReading ? ` · ${rec.item.r}` : ''} — {rec.item.m}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : null}
      </div>

      <button
        type="button"
        id="btnQuizAgain"
        onClick={onPlayAgain}
        className="w-full sm:w-auto min-h-[46px] px-6 py-3 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-sm font-bold inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500"
      >
        <RotateCcw className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
        <span>Kuis Baru (Acak Ulang)</span>
      </button>

      {performancePanel}
    </section>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/quiz/QuizRunView.tsx
================================================
import { ArrowRight, XCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import type {
  AppDatasetMode,
  QuizOption,
  QuizQuestion,
  QuizRecord,
} from '../../types/kanji';
import { ABC_LABELS, QTYPES } from '../../utils/quizEngine';
import { getQuizModelLabel, renderRubyText } from './quizHelpers';

interface QuizRunViewProps {
  datasetMode: AppDatasetMode;
  question: QuizQuestion;
  pos: number;
  totalQuestions: number;
  locked: boolean;
  selectedIdx: number | null;
  wrongFeedback: QuizRecord | null;
  showFurigana: boolean;
  onToggleFurigana: () => void;
  onFinishEarly: () => void;
  onAnswer: (optObj: QuizOption, optIdx: number) => void;
  onAdvanceAfterWrong: () => void;
}

export function QuizRunView({
  datasetMode,
  question: q,
  pos,
  totalQuestions,
  locked,
  selectedIdx,
  wrongFeedback,
  showFurigana,
  onToggleFurigana,
  onFinishEarly,
  onAnswer,
  onAdvanceAfterWrong,
}: QuizRunViewProps) {
  const qInfo = QTYPES[q.type];
  const progressPct = (pos / totalQuestions) * 100;
  const isMeaningList = q.type === 'k2m';
  const supportsFurigana = q.type === 'k2m' || q.type === 'm2k';
  const showPromptFurigana = showFurigana && q.type === 'k2m';
  const showOptionFurigana = showFurigana && q.type === 'm2k';
  const isLastQuestion = pos + 1 >= totalQuestions;

  return (
    <section aria-label="Sesi Kuis Berlangsung" className="max-w-3xl space-y-4">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 font-mono-num" id="quizCounter">
          Soal {pos + 1} dari {totalQuestions}
        </span>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 sm:gap-3">
          <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300" id="quizModeBadge">
            {qInfo.short} · {getQuizModelLabel(q.type, datasetMode)}
          </span>
          {supportsFurigana ? (
            <button
              type="button"
              id="btnQuizRunningFurigana"
              aria-pressed={showFurigana}
              onClick={onToggleFurigana}
              className={`min-h-[34px] px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                showFurigana
                  ? 'bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/60 border-indigo-300 dark:border-indigo-700 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-200 hover:bg-indigo-100 dark:hover:bg-indigo-900/60'
                  : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800'
              }`}
            >
              Furigana: {showFurigana ? 'Aktif' : 'Mati'}
            </button>
          ) : null}
          <button
            type="button"
            id="btnQuizFinishEarly"
            onClick={onFinishEarly}
            className="min-h-[36px] px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 hover:bg-rose-50 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/60 hover:text-rose-700 dark:hover:text-rose-300 hover:border-rose-200 dark:hover:border-rose-800 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 cursor-pointer"
          >
            Selesai
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="h-2 rounded-full bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 overflow-hidden" aria-hidden="true">
        <div
          className="h-full bg-indigo-600 dark:bg-indigo-500 rounded-full transition-all duration-300"
          style={{ width: `${progressPct}%` }}
        />
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 p-6 sm:p-10 text-center">
        <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 tracking-wide">
          {datasetMode === 'irodori' && q.type === 'm2k'
            ? 'Pilih kosakata yang artinya:'
            : datasetMode === 'irodori' && q.type === 'ex2k'
              ? 'Kosakata mana yang melengkapi kalimat ini?'
              : qInfo.prompt}
        </p>

        <div
          id="quizMain"
          className={`mt-3 font-jp font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 break-words leading-snug ${
            q.type === 'r2k'
              ? 'text-3xl sm:text-5xl text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300'
              : q.type === 'm2k'
                ? 'text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-sans'
                : q.type === 'ex2k'
                  ? 'text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold leading-relaxed'
                  : q.item.k.length >= 6
                    ? 'text-3xl sm:text-5xl'
                    : q.item.k.length >= 4
                      ? 'text-4xl sm:text-6xl'
                      : 'text-5xl sm:text-7xl'
          }`}
        >
          {q.type === 'k2m' && showPromptFurigana
            ? renderRubyText(
                q.item.k,
                q.item.r,
                'text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-300 select-none'
              )
            : q.type === 'k2r' || q.type === 'k2m'
              ? q.item.k
              : q.type === 'm2k'
                ? q.item.m
                : q.type === 'r2k'
                  ? q.item.r
                  : q.exMeta?.maskedEx}
        </div>

        {q.promptHint ? (
          <p
            id="quizPromptHint"
            className="mt-2.5 inline-block rounded-lg bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/70 px-3 py-1 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-200"
          >
            {q.promptHint}
          </p>
        ) : null}

        {q.type === 'ex2k' ? (
          <p className="mt-3 text-sm sm:text-base text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 font-medium">{q.item.exi}</p>
        ) : null}
      </div>

      <div
        id="quizOptions"
        className={`grid gap-3 ${isMeaningList ? 'grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2' : 'grid-cols-2'}`}
      >
        {q.options.map((optObj, idx) => {
          const isCorrectOpt = optObj.item.id === q.item.id;
          const isChosen = selectedIdx === idx;
          let optClass =
            'bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 hover:border-indigo-300 dark:hover:border-indigo-500 hover:bg-indigo-50/40 dark:hover:bg-indigo-950/40';
          if (locked) {
            if (isCorrectOpt) {
              optClass = 'bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/60 border-emerald-500 text-emerald-900 dark:text-emerald-100';
            } else if (isChosen) {
              optClass = 'bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/60 border-rose-500 text-rose-900 dark:text-rose-100';
            } else {
              optClass = 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 opacity-60';
            }
          }

          return (
            <button
              key={`${optObj.item.id}-${idx}`}
              type="button"
              disabled={locked}
              onClick={() => onAnswer(optObj, idx)}
              className={`min-h-[56px] p-4 rounded-xl border-2 text-left transition-colors flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer disabled:cursor-default focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 ${optClass}`}
            >
              <span
                className={`inline-flex items-center justify-center w-8 h-8 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold shrink-0 font-mono-num ${
                  locked && isCorrectOpt
                    ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white'
                    : locked && isChosen && !isCorrectOpt
                      ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white'
                      : 'bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/70 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300'
                }`}
              >
                {ABC_LABELS[idx]}
              </span>
              <span className="font-jp text-base sm:text-lg font-bold break-words leading-snug">
                {showOptionFurigana
                  ? renderRubyText(
                      optObj.label,
                      optObj.item.r,
                      'text-[11px] font-medium text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-300 select-none'
                    )
                  : optObj.label}
              </span>
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {wrongFeedback ? (
        <div
          id="quizWrongFeedback"
          role="status"
          aria-live="polite"
          className="rounded-2xl border-2 border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-800/80 bg-rose-50/80 dark:bg-rose-950/40 p-4 sm:p-5 space-y-3"
        >
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm sm:text-base font-bold text-rose-800 dark:text-rose-200">
              <XCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
              <span>Kurang tepat — cek pembahasannya:</span>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              id="btnQuizNextAfterWrong"
              onClick={onAdvanceAfterWrong}
              className="min-h-[38px] px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500"
            >
              <span>{isLastQuestion ? 'Lihat Hasil Kuis' : 'Soal Berikutnya'}</span>
              <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2.5 text-sm">
            <div className="rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800/80 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 p-3 space-y-1">
              <div className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400">
                Jawaban Benar
              </div>
              <div className="text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
                <strong className="font-jp text-base">
                  {wrongFeedback.type === 'ex2k'
                    ? (wrongFeedback.exMeta?.targetSurface ?? wrongFeedback.item.k)
                    : wrongFeedback.item.k}
                </strong>
                {wrongFeedback.item.r && wrongFeedback.item.r !== wrongFeedback.item.k ? (
                  <span className="font-jp text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300"> ({wrongFeedback.item.r})</span>
                ) : null}
              </div>
              <div className="text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-300 font-semibold">{wrongFeedback.item.m}</div>
            </div>

            <div className="rounded-xl border border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-800/80 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 p-3 space-y-1">
              <div className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400">
                Pilihanmu
              </div>
              <div className="text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">
                <strong className="font-jp text-base text-rose-800 dark:text-rose-300">
                  {wrongFeedback.chosenLabel}
                </strong>
                {wrongFeedback.type === 'k2m' ? (
                  <span className="font-jp text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                    {' '}
                    ← {wrongFeedback.chosen.k}
                    {wrongFeedback.chosen.r && wrongFeedback.chosen.r !== wrongFeedback.chosen.k
                      ? ` (${wrongFeedback.chosen.r})`
                      : ''}
                  </span>
                ) : wrongFeedback.chosen.r && wrongFeedback.chosen.r !== wrongFeedback.chosenLabel ? (
                  <span className="font-jp text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300"> ({wrongFeedback.chosen.r})</span>
                ) : null}
              </div>
              {wrongFeedback.type !== 'k2m' ? (
                <div className="text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 text-xs sm:text-sm">
                  Artinya:{' '}
                  <span className="font-medium text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100">{wrongFeedback.chosen.m}</span>
                </div>
              ) : null}
            </div>
          </div>

          {wrongFeedback.type === 'ex2k' && wrongFeedback.item.ex ? (
            <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200">
              <p className="font-jp font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50">{wrongFeedback.item.ex}</p>
              {wrongFeedback.item.exi ? (
                <p className="text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mt-0.5">{wrongFeedback.item.exi}</p>
              ) : null}
            </div>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <p className="hidden md:block text-center text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
        {wrongFeedback
          ? 'Tekan Enter atau Spasi untuk lanjut ke soal berikutnya.'
          : 'Tekan tombol A–D atau 1–4 pada keyboard untuk memilih jawaban.'}
      </p>
    </section>
  );
}



================================================
FILE: src/components/quiz/QuizSetupView.tsx
