import type { ReactNode, RefObject } from 'react';
import { HelpCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS } from '../../data/irodoriShared';
import type {
  AppDatasetMode,
  IrodoriBookConfig,
  IrodoriChapterNumber,
  QuizTypeChoice,
} from '../../types/kanji';
import { PRESET_COUNTS, QUIZ_TYPE_OPTIONS } from './quizHelpers';

interface QuizSetupViewProps {
  datasetMode: AppDatasetMode;
  irodoriBook: IrodoriBookConfig;
  selectedChapters: IrodoriChapterNumber[];
  chapterCounts: Record<IrodoriChapterNumber, number>;
  quizTypeChoice: QuizTypeChoice;
  showFurigana: boolean;
  activeScopeLabel: string;
  kanjiCount: number;
  modelCount: number;
  totalBank: number;
  hasKanaFilteredModels: boolean;
  disabledPresets: readonly number[];
  customCount: string;
  customInputRef: RefObject<HTMLInputElement | null>;
  onSelectChapters: (chapters: IrodoriChapterNumber[]) => void;
  onToggleChapter: (ch: IrodoriChapterNumber) => void;
  onSelectQuizType: (choice: QuizTypeChoice) => void;
  onToggleFurigana: () => void;
  onCustomCountChange: (value: string) => void;
  onStartQuiz: (requested: number | 'all') => void;
  performancePanel: ReactNode;
}

export function QuizSetupView({
  datasetMode,
  irodoriBook,
  selectedChapters,
  chapterCounts,
  quizTypeChoice,
  showFurigana,
  activeScopeLabel,
  kanjiCount,
  modelCount,
  totalBank,
  hasKanaFilteredModels,
  disabledPresets,
  customCount,
  customInputRef,
  onSelectChapters,
  onToggleChapter,
  onSelectQuizType,
  onToggleFurigana,
  onCustomCountChange,
  onStartQuiz,
  performancePanel,
}: QuizSetupViewProps) {
  return (
    <section aria-label="Pengaturan Kuis ABC" className="max-w-3xl space-y-6">
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 p-6 sm:p-7 space-y-5">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 flex items-center gap-2">
            <HelpCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
            <span>
              {datasetMode === 'irodori'
                ? `Kuis Kotoba & Kanji ${irodoriBook.label}`
                : 'Kuis ABC Multi-Model'}
            </span>
          </h2>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
            Setiap {datasetMode === 'irodori' ? 'kosakata' : 'kanji'} memiliki hingga 5 variasi
            soal otomatis. Untuk soal melengkapi kalimat, pilihan jawaban sudah disesuaikan dengan{' '}
            <strong className="text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">konjugasi</strong> dan kelas kata dalam pilihan yang aktif.
          </p>
        </div>

        {datasetMode === 'irodori' ? (
          <div
            id="quizChapterPicker"
            className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-slate-50/70 dark:bg-slate-800/60 p-4 space-y-3"
          >
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">Pilih Bab Materi Kuis</span>
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300 font-mono-num">
                  {selectedChapters.length}/{IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS.length} bab
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  id="btnQuizSelectAllChapters"
                  onClick={() => onSelectChapters([...IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS])}
                  className={`min-h-[30px] px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                    selectedChapters.length === IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS.length
                      ? 'bg-indigo-600 border-indigo-600 text-white'
                      : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-700'
                  }`}
                >
                  Semua Bab
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  id="btnQuizSelectFirstHalf"
                  onClick={() => onSelectChapters(IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS.filter(ch => ch <= 9))}
                  className="min-h-[30px] px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Bab 1–9
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  id="btnQuizSelectSecondHalf"
                  onClick={() => onSelectChapters(IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS.filter(ch => ch >= 10))}
                  className="min-h-[30px] px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Bab 10–18
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  id="btnQuizClearChapters"
                  onClick={() => onSelectChapters([])}
                  className="min-h-[30px] px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 hover:bg-rose-50 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/60 hover:text-rose-700 dark:hover:text-rose-300 hover:border-rose-200 dark:hover:border-rose-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Kosongkan
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              Klik satu atau beberapa bab untuk menggabungkan materi kuis sesuai kebutuhan belajar.
            </p>

            <div
              id="quizChapterGrid"
              className="grid grid-cols-3 sm:grid-cols-6 gap-1.5"
              role="group"
              aria-label="Pilihan bab materi kuis"
            >
              {IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS.map(ch => {
                const isSelected = selectedChapters.includes(ch);
                const meta = irodoriBook.chapters[ch];
                const count = chapterCounts[ch] ?? 0;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={ch}
                    type="button"
                    aria-pressed={isSelected}
                    title={`${meta.label}: ${meta.jpTitle} — ${meta.idTitle} (${count} kosakata)`}
                    onClick={() => onToggleChapter(ch)}
                    className={`min-h-[40px] px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg border text-xs font-semibold flex items-center justify-between gap-1 transition-colors cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'bg-indigo-600 border-indigo-600 text-white shadow-xs'
                        : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 hover:border-slate-300 dark:hover:border-slate-600'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span>Bab {ch}</span>
                    <span
                      className={`text-[11px] font-mono-num ${
                        isSelected ? 'text-indigo-100' : 'text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-400'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {count}
                    </span>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2.5" id="quizTypeGrid">
          {QUIZ_TYPE_OPTIONS.map(opt => {
            const active = quizTypeChoice === opt.key;
            const optionLabel =
              datasetMode === 'irodori'
                ? opt.key === 'k2m'
                  ? 'Kosakata → Arti'
                  : opt.key === 'm2k'
                    ? 'Arti → Kosakata'
                    : opt.key === 'ex2k'
                      ? 'Contoh Kalimat → Kosakata (Sesuai Konjugasi)'
                      : opt.label
                : opt.label;
            return (
              <button
                key={opt.key}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onSelectQuizType(opt.key)}
                className={`min-h-[46px] px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border text-left text-sm font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-2.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 ${
                  opt.fullWidth ? 'sm:col-span-2' : ''
                } ${
                  active
                    ? 'bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/60 border-indigo-500 text-indigo-800 dark:text-indigo-200'
                    : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800'
                }`}
              >
                <span className="font-jp text-xs font-bold text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300 shrink-0">
                  {opt.short}
                </span>
                <span>{optionLabel}</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-slate-50/70 dark:bg-slate-800/60 px-4 py-3 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
              Bantuan Furigana{' '}
              <span className="font-jp font-semibold text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300">(ふりがな)</span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-300 mt-0.5">
              Opsional — tampilkan cara baca hiragana di atas kanji pada soal{' '}
              <strong className="text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100">{datasetMode === 'irodori' ? 'Arti ↔ Kosakata' : 'Arti ↔ Kanji'}</strong>{' '}
              (漢→ID &amp; ID→漢).
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            id="btnToggleQuizFurigana"
            aria-pressed={showFurigana}
            onClick={onToggleFurigana}
            className={`min-h-[36px] px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl border text-xs font-bold transition-colors cursor-pointer shrink-0 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500 ${
              showFurigana
                ? 'bg-indigo-600 border-indigo-600 text-white'
                : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-700'
            }`}
          >
            {showFurigana ? 'Furigana: Aktif' : 'Furigana: Mati'}
          </button>
        </div>

        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
          Bank soal aktif:{' '}
          <strong className="text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 font-mono-num">
            {hasKanaFilteredModels
              ? `${activeScopeLabel} (${totalBank.toLocaleString('id-ID')} soal unik)`
              : `${activeScopeLabel} (${kanjiCount} ${datasetMode === 'irodori' ? 'kosakata' : 'kanji'} × ${modelCount} model = ${totalBank.toLocaleString('id-ID')} soal unik)`}
          </strong>
          {hasKanaFilteredModels
            ? ' · kosakata tanpa kanji & kalimat penuh otomatis dilewati pada model 漢↔あ agar soal selalu relevan.'
            : '.'}
        </p>

        <div>
          <div className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 mb-2.5">Pilih jumlah soal:</div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2.5">
            {PRESET_COUNTS.map(cnt => (
              <button
                key={cnt}
                type="button"
                disabled={cnt > totalBank}
                onClick={() => onStartQuiz(cnt)}
                className="min-h-[46px] px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-sm font-bold font-mono-num transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500"
              >
                {cnt} soal
              </button>
            ))}
            <button
              type="button"
              id="btnQuizAllBank"
              disabled={totalBank === 0}
              onClick={() => onStartQuiz('all')}
              className="min-h-[46px] px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white text-sm font-bold font-mono-num transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500"
            >
              Semua ({totalBank})
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-3 flex gap-2">
            <input
              ref={customInputRef}
              type="number"
              id="quizCustomCount"
              min={1}
              max={totalBank}
              value={customCount}
              onChange={e => onCustomCountChange(e.target.value)}
              onKeyDown={e => {
                if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  const parsed = parseInt(customCount, 10);
                  if (parsed >= 1) onStartQuiz(parsed);
                }
              }}
              placeholder={`Atau ketik jumlah soal (1–${totalBank})…`}
              aria-label="Jumlah soal kuis kustom"
              className="flex-1 min-h-[44px] px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 placeholder:text-slate-400 dark:placeholder:text-slate-500 focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-slate-950 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500"
            />
            <button
              type="button"
              id="btnStartCustomQuiz"
              disabled={totalBank === 0}
              onClick={() => {
                const parsed = parseInt(customCount, 10);
                if (!parsed || parsed < 1) {
                  customInputRef.current?.focus();
                  return;
                }
                onStartQuiz(parsed);
              }}
              className="min-h-[44px] px-5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 text-sm font-bold cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500"
            >
              Mulai
            </button>
          </div>

          {totalBank === 0 ? (
            <p className="mt-2.5 text-xs font-medium text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-300">
              {datasetMode === 'irodori' && selectedChapters.length === 0
                ? 'Pilih minimal 1 bab di atas untuk memulai kuis.'
                : 'Belum ada data kosakata pada pilihan ini untuk dijadikan kuis.'}
            </p>
          ) : disabledPresets.length > 0 ? (
            <p className="mt-2.5 text-xs font-medium text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-300">
              Total bank soal pada filter ini adalah {totalBank} soal, sehingga opsi{' '}
              {disabledPresets.join(', ')} dinonaktifkan.
            </p>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      </div>

      {performancePanel}
    </section>
  );
}



================================================
FILE: src/components/quiz/QuizStatsPanel.tsx
================================================
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { BarChart3, History, Trash2, TrendingDown, TrendingUp } from 'lucide-react';
import { LEVELS } from '../../data/kanjiData';
import type {
  AppDatasetMode,
  IrodoriBookConfig,
  QuizSessionHistoryEntry,
} from '../../types/kanji';
import type { QuizPerformanceStats } from '../../utils/quizStats';
import { QuizAnswerHistoryModal } from './QuizAnswerHistoryModal';
import { MODE_SHORT_LABEL, formatQuizTimestamp } from './quizHelpers';

interface QuizStatsPanelProps {
  datasetMode: AppDatasetMode;
  irodoriBook: IrodoriBookConfig;
  quizHistory: QuizSessionHistoryEntry[];
  performanceStats: QuizPerformanceStats;
  cloudSyncEnabled: boolean;
  quizHistorySyncing: boolean;
  confirmClearHistory: boolean;
  historyModalSessionId: string | null;
  historyAnswerFilter: 'all' | 'wrong' | 'right';
  onRequestClearHistory: (confirm: boolean) => void;
  onConfirmClearHistory: () => void;
  onOpenAnswerHistory: (sessionId?: string) => void;
  onSelectHistorySession: (sessionId: string) => void;
  onSelectHistoryFilter: (filter: 'all' | 'wrong' | 'right') => void;
  onCloseAnswerHistory: () => void;
}

export function QuizStatsPanel({
  datasetMode,
  irodoriBook,
  quizHistory,
  performanceStats,
  cloudSyncEnabled,
  quizHistorySyncing,
  confirmClearHistory,
  historyModalSessionId,
  historyAnswerFilter,
  onRequestClearHistory,
  onConfirmClearHistory,
  onOpenAnswerHistory,
  onSelectHistorySession,
  onSelectHistoryFilter,
  onCloseAnswerHistory,
}: QuizStatsPanelProps) {
  const recentScores = quizHistory.slice(0, 6);

  const activeHistorySession = useMemo(
    () =>
      historyModalSessionId
        ? (quizHistory.find(entry => entry.id === historyModalSessionId) ?? quizHistory[0] ?? null)
        : null,
    [historyModalSessionId, quizHistory]
  );

  return (
    <div
      id="quizStatsPanel"
      className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 p-5 sm:p-6 space-y-4"
    >
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 flex items-center gap-2">
          <BarChart3 className="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
          <span>Statistik Performa Kuis</span>
        </h3>

        {quizHistory.length > 0 ? (
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              id="btnOpenQuizAnswerHistory"
              onClick={() => onOpenAnswerHistory()}
              className="min-h-[32px] px-3 py-1 rounded-lg border border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-800 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/70 hover:bg-indigo-100 dark:hover:bg-indigo-900/70 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-200 text-xs font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-indigo-500"
            >
              <History className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
              <span>Riwayat Jawaban</span>
            </button>

            {confirmClearHistory ? (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  id="btnConfirmClearQuizStats"
                  onClick={onConfirmClearHistory}
                  className="min-h-[32px] px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 text-white text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Ya, Hapus
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onRequestClearHistory(false)}
                  className="min-h-[32px] px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Batal
                </button>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <button
                type="button"
                id="btnClearQuizStats"
                onClick={() => onRequestClearHistory(true)}
                className="min-h-[32px] px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-rose-50 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/60 hover:text-rose-700 dark:hover:text-rose-300 hover:border-rose-200 dark:hover:border-rose-800 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-300 text-xs font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                <span>Hapus Riwayat</span>
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : null}
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-slate-50/70 dark:bg-slate-800/60 p-3.5 space-y-2">
          <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Persentase Jawaban Benar</div>
          <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-2">
            <span
              id="quizStatAccuracy"
              className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 font-mono-num"
            >
              {performanceStats.overallAccuracyPct}%
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 font-mono-num">
              {performanceStats.totalCorrect}/{performanceStats.totalAnswered} benar
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="h-1.5 rounded-full bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-700 overflow-hidden" aria-hidden="true">
            <div
              className="h-full bg-emerald-600 dark:bg-emerald-500 rounded-full transition-all duration-300"
              style={{ width: `${performanceStats.overallAccuracyPct}%` }}
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-slate-50/70 dark:bg-slate-800/60 p-3.5 space-y-1.5">
          <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Skor Terakhir</div>
          <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-2">
            <span
              id="quizStatLatest"
              className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-400 font-mono-num"
            >
              {performanceStats.latestScore !== null ? `${performanceStats.latestScore}%` : '—'}
            </span>
            {performanceStats.trendDelta !== null ? (
              <span
                className={`text-xs font-bold font-mono-num inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 ${
                  performanceStats.trendDelta > 0
                    ? 'text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400'
                    : performanceStats.trendDelta < 0
                      ? 'text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400'
                      : 'text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400'
                }`}
              >
                {performanceStats.trendDelta > 0 ? (
                  <TrendingUp className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                ) : performanceStats.trendDelta < 0 ? (
                  <TrendingDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                ) : null}
                <span>
                  {performanceStats.trendDelta > 0
                    ? `+${performanceStats.trendDelta}%`
                    : `${performanceStats.trendDelta}%`}
                </span>
              </span>
            ) : (
              <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                {performanceStats.totalSessions === 1 ? 'Sesi pertama' : 'Belum ada sesi'}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
            {performanceStats.trendDelta !== null
              ? 'Dibanding skor kuis sebelumnya'
              : 'Selesaikan kuis untuk melihat tren'}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-slate-50/70 dark:bg-slate-800/60 p-3.5 space-y-1.5">
          <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Skor Terbaik</div>
          <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-2">
            <span
              id="quizStatBest"
              className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 font-mono-num"
            >
              {performanceStats.totalSessions > 0 ? `${performanceStats.bestScore}%` : '—'}
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 font-mono-num">
              {performanceStats.totalSessions} sesi
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Rekor akurasi tertinggi yang tersimpan</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {recentScores.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="space-y-2 pt-1">
          <div className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
            Riwayat Skor Terakhir
          </div>
          <ul
            id="quizRecentHistory"
            className="divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-xl overflow-hidden"
          >
            {recentScores.map(entry => {
              const barColor =
                entry.score >= 80
                  ? 'bg-emerald-600 dark:bg-emerald-500'
                  : entry.score >= 60
                    ? 'bg-indigo-600 dark:bg-indigo-500'
                    : 'bg-amber-500';
              const textColor =
                entry.score >= 80
                  ? 'text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400'
                  : entry.score >= 60
                    ? 'text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-400'
                    : 'text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400';

              return (
                <li
                  key={entry.id}
                  className="px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-x-4 gap-y-1.5 text-xs sm:text-sm"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                    <span className="font-jp font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 shrink-0">
                      {MODE_SHORT_LABEL[entry.mode]}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-slate-300 dark:text-slate-600" aria-hidden="true">
                      ·
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 font-medium">
                      {entry.scopeLabel ?? `Level ${LEVELS[entry.level].label}`}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-slate-300 dark:text-slate-600 hidden sm:inline" aria-hidden="true">
                      ·
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 hidden sm:inline">
                      {formatQuizTimestamp(entry.completedAt)}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 ml-auto shrink-0">
                    <span className="text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 font-mono-num">
                      {entry.correct}/{entry.total}
                    </span>
                    <div
                      className="w-16 sm:w-20 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 overflow-hidden"
                      aria-hidden="true"
                    >
                      <div
                        className={`h-full rounded-full ${barColor}`}
                        style={{ width: `${entry.score}%` }}
                      />
                    </div>
                    <span className={`font-bold font-mono-num w-10 text-right ${textColor}`}>
                      {entry.score}%
                    </span>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => onOpenAnswerHistory(entry.id)}
                      aria-label={`Lihat detail jawaban sesi ${entry.scopeLabel ?? LEVELS[entry.level].label} (${entry.score}%)`}
                      className="min-h-[28px] px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-indigo-50 dark:hover:bg-indigo-950/70 hover:border-indigo-200 dark:hover:border-indigo-700 hover:text-indigo-700 dark:hover:text-indigo-200 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      Detail
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </li>
              );
            })}
          </ul>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
          Belum ada riwayat skor. Selesaikan satu sesi kuis untuk memantau persentase jawaban benar
          dan riwayat skor Anda.
        </p>
      )}

      {activeHistorySession ? (
        <QuizAnswerHistoryModal
          datasetMode={datasetMode}
          irodoriBook={irodoriBook}
          quizHistory={quizHistory}
          activeSession={activeHistorySession}
          answerFilter={historyAnswerFilter}
          cloudSyncEnabled={cloudSyncEnabled}
          quizHistorySyncing={quizHistorySyncing}
          onSelectSession={onSelectHistorySession}
          onSelectFilter={onSelectHistoryFilter}
          onClose={onCloseAnswerHistory}
        />
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}



================================================
FILE: src/hooks/useAuthActions.ts
================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  createUserWithEmailAndPassword,
  onAuthStateChanged,
  sendEmailVerification,
  sendPasswordResetEmail,
  signInWithEmailAndPassword,
  signInWithPopup,
  signOut,
  updateProfile,
  type User,
} from 'firebase/auth';
import {
  auth,
  createGoogleAccountPickerProvider,
  githubProvider,
  googleProvider,
} from '../firebase';
import { mapFirebaseAuthError } from '../utils/authErrors';
import { sanitizeDisplayName } from '../utils/progressSync';
import type { ShowToastFn } from './useToast';

interface UseAuthActionsOptions {
  showToast: ShowToastFn;
}

export function useAuthActions({ showToast }: UseAuthActionsOptions) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [authVersion, setAuthVersion] = useState(0);
  const [authReady, setAuthReady] = useState(false);
  const [authBusy, setAuthBusy] = useState(false);

  const [authModalOpen, setAuthModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [authSwitchMode, setAuthSwitchMode] = useState(false);
  const [authErrorText, setAuthErrorText] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [authInfoText, setAuthInfoText] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [authConsoleHint, setAuthConsoleHint] = useState(false);

  const justSignedInRef = useRef(false);

  const bumpAuthVersion = useCallback(() => {
    setAuthVersion(v => v + 1);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const unsubscribe = onAuthStateChanged(auth, currentUser => {
      setUser(currentUser);
      setAuthVersion(v => v + 1);
      setAuthReady(true);
    });
    return () => unsubscribe();
  }, []);

  const clearAuthNotices = useCallback(() => {
    setAuthErrorText(null);
    setAuthInfoText(null);
    setAuthConsoleHint(false);
  }, []);

  const handleOpenAuthModal = useCallback(
    (isSwitch: boolean) => {
      clearAuthNotices();
      setAuthSwitchMode(isSwitch);
      setAuthModalOpen(true);
    },
    [clearAuthNotices]
  );

  const handleGoogleLogin = useCallback(
    async (forceSelectAccount: boolean) => {
      if (authBusy) return;
      clearAuthNotices();
      setAuthBusy(true);
      justSignedInRef.current = true;
      try {
        const provider = forceSelectAccount
          ? createGoogleAccountPickerProvider()
          : googleProvider;
        await signInWithPopup(auth, provider);
        setAuthModalOpen(false);
      } catch (err) {
        justSignedInRef.current = false;
        const mapped = mapFirebaseAuthError(err);
        setAuthErrorText(mapped.message);
        setAuthConsoleHint(mapped.showConsoleHint);
      } finally {
        setAuthBusy(false);
      }
    },
    [authBusy, clearAuthNotices]
  );

  const handleGithubLogin = useCallback(async () => {
    if (authBusy) return;
    clearAuthNotices();
    setAuthBusy(true);
    justSignedInRef.current = true;
    try {
      await signInWithPopup(auth, githubProvider);
      setAuthModalOpen(false);
    } catch (err) {
      justSignedInRef.current = false;
      const mapped = mapFirebaseAuthError(err);
      setAuthErrorText(mapped.message);
      setAuthConsoleHint(mapped.showConsoleHint);
    } finally {
      setAuthBusy(false);
    }
  }, [authBusy, clearAuthNotices]);

  const handleEmailSignIn = useCallback(
    async (email: string, password: string) => {
      if (authBusy) return;
      clearAuthNotices();
      setAuthBusy(true);
      justSignedInRef.current = true;
      try {
        const cred = await signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, email.trim(), password);
        setAuthModalOpen(false);
        if (!cred.user.emailVerified) {
          showToast(
            'warning',
            'Verifikasi Email Diperlukan',
            `Cek kotak masuk ${cred.user.email} untuk mengaktifkan sinkronisasi cloud.`
          );
        }
      } catch (err) {
        justSignedInRef.current = false;
        const mapped = mapFirebaseAuthError(err);
        setAuthErrorText(mapped.message);
        setAuthConsoleHint(mapped.showConsoleHint);
      } finally {
        setAuthBusy(false);
      }
    },
    [authBusy, clearAuthNotices, showToast]
  );

  const handleEmailSignUp = useCallback(
    async (name: string, email: string, password: string) => {
      if (authBusy) return;
      clearAuthNotices();
      setAuthBusy(true);
      justSignedInRef.current = true;
      try {
        const cred = await createUserWithEmailAndPassword(auth, email.trim(), password);
        const cleanName = sanitizeDisplayName(name) || email.trim().split('@')[0];
        if (cleanName) {
          await updateProfile(cred.user, { displayName: cleanName });
        }
        try {
          await sendEmailVerification(cred.user);
        } catch {
          // Non-fatal if rate-limited
        }
        setAuthModalOpen(false);
        showToast(
          'info',
          'Akun Dibuat · Cek Email',
          `Tautan verifikasi dikirim ke ${cred.user.email}. Verifikasi untuk mulai sinkronisasi cloud.`
        );
      } catch (err) {
        justSignedInRef.current = false;
        const mapped = mapFirebaseAuthError(err);
        setAuthErrorText(mapped.message);
        setAuthConsoleHint(mapped.showConsoleHint);
      } finally {
        setAuthBusy(false);
      }
    },
    [authBusy, clearAuthNotices, showToast]
  );

  const handleEmailReset = useCallback(
    async (email: string) => {
      if (authBusy) return;
      clearAuthNotices();
      setAuthBusy(true);
      try {
        await sendPasswordResetEmail(auth, email.trim());
        setAuthInfoText(`Tautan reset kata sandi telah dikirim ke ${email.trim()}.`);
      } catch (err) {
        const mapped = mapFirebaseAuthError(err);
        setAuthErrorText(mapped.message);
        setAuthConsoleHint(mapped.showConsoleHint);
      } finally {
        setAuthBusy(false);
      }
    },
    [authBusy, clearAuthNotices]
  );

  const handleResendVerification = useCallback(async () => {
    const current = auth.currentUser;
    if (!current || authBusy) return;
    setAuthBusy(true);
    try {
      await sendEmailVerification(current);
      showToast('success', 'Email Verifikasi Terkirim', `Periksa kotak masuk ${current.email}.`);
    } catch (err) {
      const mapped = mapFirebaseAuthError(err);
      showToast('error', 'Gagal Mengirim Email', mapped.message);
    } finally {
      setAuthBusy(false);
    }
  }, [authBusy, showToast]);

  const handleRefreshVerification = useCallback(async () => {
    const current = auth.currentUser;
    if (!current || authBusy) return;
    setAuthBusy(true);
    try {
      await current.reload();
      await current.getIdToken(true);
      setAuthVersion(v => v + 1);
      if (current.emailVerified) {
        showToast('success', 'Email Terverifikasi', 'Sinkronisasi cloud kini aktif.');
      } else {
        showToast(
          'info',
          'Belum Terverifikasi',
          'Silakan klik tautan verifikasi di email Anda lalu tekan Cek Status lagi.'
        );
      }
    } catch (err) {
      const mapped = mapFirebaseAuthError(err);
      showToast('error', 'Gagal Memeriksa Status', mapped.message);
    } finally {
      setAuthBusy(false);
    }
  }, [authBusy, showToast]);

  const executeSignOut = useCallback(
    async (onAfterSignOut: () => void) => {
      if (authBusy) return;
      setAuthBusy(true);
      try {
        await signOut(auth);
        justSignedInRef.current = false;
        onAfterSignOut();
        showToast(
          'info',
          'Berhasil Keluar',
          'Progres & riwayat kuis N4 dan Irodori Anda tetap tersimpan aman di akun Anda.'
        );
      } catch (err) {
        showToast(
          'error',
          'Gagal Keluar',
          err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Tidak dapat keluar dari akun saat ini.'
        );
      } finally {
        setAuthBusy(false);
      }
    },
    [authBusy, showToast]
  );

  return {
    user,
    authVersion,
    authReady,
    authBusy,
    authModalOpen,
    setAuthModalOpen,
    authSwitchMode,
    authErrorText,
    authInfoText,
    authConsoleHint,
    justSignedInRef,
    bumpAuthVersion,
    clearAuthNotices,
    handleOpenAuthModal,
    handleGoogleLogin,
    handleGithubLogin,
    handleEmailSignIn,
    handleEmailSignUp,
    handleEmailReset,
    handleResendVerification,
    handleRefreshVerification,
    executeSignOut,
  };
}



================================================
FILE: src/hooks/useBackupRestore.ts
================================================
import { useCallback, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { PendingRestoreState } from '../components/RestoreModal';
import type { AppDatasetMode, IrodoriBookId } from '../types/kanji';
import { buildBackupPayload, validateAndParseBackup } from '../utils/storage';
import type { ShowToastFn } from './useToast';

interface UseBackupRestoreOptions {
  datasetMode: AppDatasetMode;
  irodoriBookId: IrodoriBookId;
  activeMastered: Set<string>;
  activeHardSet: Set<string>;
  activeValidIds: Set<string>;
  applyProgressChange: (nextMastered: Set<string>, nextHard: Set<string>) => boolean;
  onRestoreCommitted: (nextMastered: Set<string>, nextHard: Set<string>) => void;
  showToast: ShowToastFn;
  dismissToast: () => void;
}

export function useBackupRestore({
  datasetMode,
  irodoriBookId,
  activeMastered,
  activeHardSet,
  activeValidIds,
  applyProgressChange,
  onRestoreCommitted,
  showToast,
  dismissToast,
}: UseBackupRestoreOptions) {
  const [pendingRestore, setPendingRestore] = useState<PendingRestoreState | null>(null);
  const restoreBtnRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const restoreFocusToButton = useCallback(() => {
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      restoreBtnRef.current?.focus();
    });
  }, []);

  const clearPendingRestore = useCallback(() => {
    setPendingRestore(null);
  }, []);

  const handleExport = useCallback(() => {
    try {
      const payload = buildBackupPayload(activeMastered, activeHardSet);
      const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = url;
      const prefix =
        datasetMode === 'irodori' ? `backup-irodori-${irodoriBookId}` : 'backup-kanji-n4';
      a.download = `${prefix}-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.json`;
      document.body.appendChild(a);
      a.click();
      document.body.removeChild(a);
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      showToast(
        'success',
        'Backup Diunduh',
        `${activeMastered.size} hafal & ${activeHardSet.size} sulit diekspor ke JSON.`
      );
    } catch {
      showToast('error', 'Backup Gagal', 'Gagal membuat file backup JSON.');
    }
  }, [activeMastered, activeHardSet, datasetMode, irodoriBookId, showToast]);

  const handleTriggerImport = useCallback(() => {
    if (fileInputRef.current) {
      fileInputRef.current.value = '';
      fileInputRef.current.click();
    }
  }, []);

  const handleFileSelected = useCallback(
    (file: File) => {
      if (file.size > 1024 * 1024) {
        showToast('error', 'Restore Ditolak', 'Ukuran file terlalu besar (maksimal 1 MB).');
        restoreFocusToButton();
        return;
      }

      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onerror = () => {
        showToast(
          'error',
          'Gagal Membaca File',
          'File tidak dapat dibaca. Progres saat ini tetap aman.'
        );
        restoreFocusToButton();
      };
      reader.onabort = () => {
        showToast('warning', 'Restore Dibatalkan', 'Pembacaan file dihentikan.');
        restoreFocusToButton();
      };
      reader.onload = ev => {
        try {
          const text = typeof ev.target?.result === 'string' ? ev.target.result : '';
          const parsed = validateAndParseBackup(text, activeValidIds);
          dismissToast();
          setPendingRestore({ parsed, fileName: file.name });
        } catch (err) {
          const msg =
            err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'File backup tidak valid atau rusak.';
          showToast(
            'error',
            'Restore Ditolak',
            `${msg} Progres saat ini tetap aman dan tidak berubah.`
          );
          restoreFocusToButton();
        }
      };
      reader.readAsText(file);
    },
    [showToast, dismissToast, restoreFocusToButton, activeValidIds]
  );

  const restorePreviews = useMemo(() => {
    if (!pendingRestore) {
      return { mergedMastered: 0, mergedHard: 0, cleanImpHard: 0 };
    }
    const { impMastered, impHard } = pendingRestore.parsed;
    const mergedM = new Set([...activeMastered, ...impMastered]);
    const mergedH = new Set([...activeHardSet, ...impHard].filter(id => !mergedM.has(id)));
    const cleanImpH = impHard.filter(id => !impMastered.includes(id));
    return {
      mergedMastered: mergedM.size,
      mergedHard: mergedH.size,
      cleanImpHard: cleanImpH.length,
    };
  }, [pendingRestore, activeMastered, activeHardSet]);

  const handleCloseRestoreModal = useCallback(() => {
    setPendingRestore(null);
    restoreFocusToButton();
  }, [restoreFocusToButton]);

  const handleConfirmRestore = useCallback(
    (mode: 'merge' | 'replace') => {
      if (!pendingRestore) return;
      const { impMastered, impHard, rejectedIds } = pendingRestore.parsed;

      let nextMastered: Set<string>;
      let nextHard: Set<string>;

      if (mode === 'merge') {
        nextMastered = new Set(activeMastered);
        nextHard = new Set(activeHardSet);
        impMastered.forEach(id => {
          nextMastered.add(id);
          nextHard.delete(id);
        });
        impHard.forEach(id => {
          if (!nextMastered.has(id)) nextHard.add(id);
        });
      } else {
        nextMastered = new Set(impMastered);
        nextHard = new Set(impHard.filter(id => !nextMastered.has(id)));
      }

      const committed = applyProgressChange(nextMastered, nextHard);
      setPendingRestore(null);
      restoreFocusToButton();
      if (!committed) return;

      onRestoreCommitted(nextMastered, nextHard);

      const modeLabel = mode === 'merge' ? 'digabungkan' : 'ditimpa';
      const rejectedNote = rejectedIds.length
        ? `${rejectedIds.length} ID tidak dikenali diabaikan.`
        : undefined;

      showToast(
        'success',
        `Progres berhasil ${modeLabel}: ${nextMastered.size} hafal & ${nextHard.size} sulit.`,
        rejectedNote
      );
    },
    [
      pendingRestore,
      activeMastered,
      activeHardSet,
      applyProgressChange,
      restoreFocusToButton,
      onRestoreCommitted,
      showToast,
    ]
  );

  return {
    pendingRestore,
    clearPendingRestore,
    restoreBtnRef,
    fileInputRef,
    restorePreviews,
    handleExport,
    handleTriggerImport,
    handleFileSelected,
    handleCloseRestoreModal,
    handleConfirmRestore,
  };
}



================================================
FILE: src/hooks/useCloudProgressSync.ts
