================================================
FILE: src/utils/progressSync.ts
================================================
import { IRODORI_BOOKS } from '../data/irodoriBooks';
import { VALID_KANJI_IDS } from '../data/kanjiData';
import type { IrodoriBookId } from '../types/kanji';

// Volumetric & regex boundaries synchronized verbatim with firebase-blueprint.json
export const UID_MIN_LENGTH = 1;
export const UID_MAX_LENGTH = 128;
export const UID_PATTERN = /^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$/;
export const KANJI_ID_PATTERN = /^n([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-4][0-9]{2}|500)$/;
export const MAX_KANJI_LIST_SIZE = 500;
export const DISPLAY_NAME_MAX_LENGTH = 60;
export const AVATAR_URL_MAX_LENGTH = 20000;
export const AVATAR_URL_PATTERN =
  /^(|https:\/\/.*|data:image\/(jpeg|png|webp);base64,[A-Za-z0-9+/=]+)$/;

export const IRODORI_BOOK_SYNC_RULES: Record<
  IrodoriBookId,
  { pattern: RegExp; maxSize: number; maxJoinedLength: number }
> = {
  pemula: {
    pattern: /^irp([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-9][0-9]{2}|10[0-9]{2}|11[0-2][0-9]|113[0-6])$/,
    maxSize: 1136,
    maxJoinedLength: 9500,
  },
  dasar1: {
    pattern: /^ir([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-9][0-9]{2}|10[0-5][0-9]|106[0-7])$/,
    maxSize: 1067,
    maxJoinedLength: 8000,
  },
  dasar2: {
    pattern: /^ir2_([1-9]|[1-9][0-9]|[1-9][0-9]{2}|1[0-1][0-9]{2}|12[0-7][0-9]|128[0-8])$/,
    maxSize: 1288,
    maxJoinedLength: 12000,
  },
};

export interface SanitizedProgressLists {
  mastered: string[];
  hard: string[];
}

export interface ProgressSyncPayload {
  uid: string;
  mastered: string[];
  hard: string[];
}

export interface IrodoriProgressSyncPayload {
  uid: string;
  bookId: IrodoriBookId;
  mastered: string[];
  hard: string[];
}

export interface UserProfileWriteData {
  displayName: string;
  avatarUrl: string;
}

export function isValidFirebaseUid(uid: unknown): uid is string {
  return (
    typeof uid === 'string' &&
    uid.length >= UID_MIN_LENGTH &&
    uid.length <= UID_MAX_LENGTH &&
    UID_PATTERN.test(uid)
  );
}

export function sanitizeDisplayName(raw: unknown): string {
  if (typeof raw !== 'string') return '';
  return raw.trim().slice(0, DISPLAY_NAME_MAX_LENGTH);
}

export function isValidAvatarUrl(raw: unknown): raw is string {
  return (
    typeof raw === 'string' &&
    raw.length <= AVATAR_URL_MAX_LENGTH &&
    AVATAR_URL_PATTERN.test(raw)
  );
}

export function sanitizeAvatarUrl(raw: unknown): string {
  if (typeof raw !== 'string') return '';
  const trimmed = raw.trim();
  return isValidAvatarUrl(trimmed) ? trimmed : '';
}

export function buildUserProfileWriteData(
  rawDisplayName: unknown,
  rawAvatarUrl: unknown
): UserProfileWriteData {
  const displayName = sanitizeDisplayName(rawDisplayName);
  const trimmedAvatar = typeof rawAvatarUrl === 'string' ? rawAvatarUrl.trim() : '';
  if (trimmedAvatar && !isValidAvatarUrl(trimmedAvatar)) {
    throw new Error(
      'Foto profil harus berupa URL HTTPS atau gambar JPEG/PNG/WebP terkompresi (maksimal ~15 KB).'
    );
  }
  return {
    displayName,
    avatarUrl: trimmedAvatar,
  };
}

/**
 * Sanitizes, deduplicates, enforces Kanji ID allowlist & blueprint bounds (<= 500),
 * and ensures an item marked mastered is never duplicated inside hard.
 */
export function sanitizeProgressLists(
  rawMastered: Iterable<unknown>,
  rawHard: Iterable<unknown>,
  validIds: Set<string> = VALID_KANJI_IDS
): SanitizedProgressLists {
  const masteredSet = new Set<string>();
  for (const id of rawMastered) {
    if (typeof id === 'string' && KANJI_ID_PATTERN.test(id) && validIds.has(id)) {
      masteredSet.add(id);
      if (masteredSet.size >= MAX_KANJI_LIST_SIZE) break;
    }
  }

  const hardSet = new Set<string>();
  for (const id of rawHard) {
    if (
      typeof id === 'string' &&
      KANJI_ID_PATTERN.test(id) &&
      validIds.has(id) &&
      !masteredSet.has(id)
    ) {
      hardSet.add(id);
      if (hardSet.size >= MAX_KANJI_LIST_SIZE) break;
    }
  }

  return {
    mastered: [...masteredSet],
    hard: [...hardSet],
  };
}

/**
 * Builds a validated Firestore mutation payload conforming strictly to firebase-blueprint.json.
 */
export function buildUserProgressWriteData(
  uid: string,
  mastered: Iterable<unknown>,
  hard: Iterable<unknown>,
  validIds: Set<string> = VALID_KANJI_IDS
): ProgressSyncPayload {
  if (!isValidFirebaseUid(uid)) {
    throw new Error('UID pengguna tidak valid untuk sinkronisasi Firestore.');
  }
  const clean = sanitizeProgressLists(mastered, hard, validIds);
  return {
    uid,
    mastered: clean.mastered,
    hard: clean.hard,
  };
}

/**
 * Merges two progress states using union semantics where `mastered` always takes precedence over `hard`.
 */
export function mergeProgressSets(
  primaryMastered: Set<string>,
  primaryHard: Set<string>,
  secondaryMastered: Iterable<unknown>,
  secondaryHard: Iterable<unknown>,
  validIds: Set<string> = VALID_KANJI_IDS
): { mastered: Set<string>; hard: Set<string>; addedFromSecondary: number } {
  const cleanPrimary = sanitizeProgressLists(primaryMastered, primaryHard, validIds);
  const cleanSecondary = sanitizeProgressLists(secondaryMastered, secondaryHard, validIds);

  const mergedMastered = new Set<string>(cleanPrimary.mastered);
  let addedFromSecondary = 0;

  for (const id of cleanSecondary.mastered) {
    if (!mergedMastered.has(id)) {
      mergedMastered.add(id);
      addedFromSecondary += 1;
    }
  }

  const mergedHard = new Set<string>();
  for (const id of cleanPrimary.hard) {
    if (!mergedMastered.has(id)) {
      mergedHard.add(id);
    }
  }
  for (const id of cleanSecondary.hard) {
    if (!mergedMastered.has(id) && !mergedHard.has(id)) {
      mergedHard.add(id);
      addedFromSecondary += 1;
    }
  }

  return {
    mastered: mergedMastered,
    hard: mergedHard,
    addedFromSecondary,
  };
}

export function areSetsEqual(a: Set<string>, b: Set<string>): boolean {
  if (a.size !== b.size) return false;
  for (const item of a) {
    if (!b.has(item)) return false;
  }
  return true;
}

export function markKanjiMastered(
  mastered: Set<string>,
  hard: Set<string>,
  id: string
): { nextMastered: Set<string>; nextHard: Set<string>; isNewMastered: boolean } {
  const nextMastered = new Set(mastered);
  const nextHard = new Set(hard);
  const isNewMastered = !nextMastered.has(id);
  nextMastered.add(id);
  nextHard.delete(id);
  return { nextMastered, nextHard, isNewMastered };
}

export function markKanjiHard(
  mastered: Set<string>,
  hard: Set<string>,
  id: string
): { nextMastered: Set<string>; nextHard: Set<string> } {
  const nextMastered = new Set(mastered);
  const nextHard = new Set(hard);
  nextHard.add(id);
  nextMastered.delete(id);
  return { nextMastered, nextHard };
}

export function isValidIrodoriBookId(raw: unknown): raw is IrodoriBookId {
  return raw === 'pemula' || raw === 'dasar1' || raw === 'dasar2';
}

/**
 * Sanitizes, deduplicates, enforces per-book Irodori ID allowlist & blueprint bounds,
 * and guarantees mastered and hard sets are mutually exclusive.
 */
export function sanitizeIrodoriProgressLists(
  bookId: IrodoriBookId,
  rawMastered: Iterable<unknown>,
  rawHard: Iterable<unknown>,
  validIds: Set<string> = IRODORI_BOOKS[bookId].validIds
): SanitizedProgressLists {
  const { pattern, maxSize } = IRODORI_BOOK_SYNC_RULES[bookId];

  const masteredSet = new Set<string>();
  for (const id of rawMastered) {
    if (typeof id === 'string' && pattern.test(id) && validIds.has(id)) {
      masteredSet.add(id);
      if (masteredSet.size >= maxSize) break;
    }
  }

  const hardSet = new Set<string>();
  for (const id of rawHard) {
    if (
      typeof id === 'string' &&
      pattern.test(id) &&
      validIds.has(id) &&
      !masteredSet.has(id)
    ) {
      hardSet.add(id);
      if (hardSet.size >= maxSize) break;
    }
  }

  return {
    mastered: [...masteredSet],
    hard: [...hardSet],
  };
}

/**
 * Builds a validated Firestore mutation payload for /users/{uid}/irodoriProgress/{bookId}
 * conforming strictly to firebase-blueprint.json.
 */
export function buildIrodoriProgressWriteData(
  uid: string,
  bookId: IrodoriBookId,
  mastered: Iterable<unknown>,
  hard: Iterable<unknown>,
  validIds: Set<string> = IRODORI_BOOKS[bookId].validIds
): IrodoriProgressSyncPayload {
  if (!isValidFirebaseUid(uid)) {
    throw new Error('UID pengguna tidak valid untuk sinkronisasi Firestore.');
  }
  if (!isValidIrodoriBookId(bookId)) {
    throw new Error('Identitas buku Irodori tidak valid untuk sinkronisasi Firestore.');
  }
  const clean = sanitizeIrodoriProgressLists(bookId, mastered, hard, validIds);
  return {
    uid,
    bookId,
    mastered: clean.mastered,
    hard: clean.hard,
  };
}

/**
 * Merges two Irodori book progress states using union semantics where `mastered`
 * always takes precedence over `hard`.
 */
export function mergeIrodoriProgressSets(
  bookId: IrodoriBookId,
  primaryMastered: Set<string>,
  primaryHard: Set<string>,
  secondaryMastered: Iterable<unknown>,
  secondaryHard: Iterable<unknown>,
  validIds: Set<string> = IRODORI_BOOKS[bookId].validIds
): { mastered: Set<string>; hard: Set<string>; addedFromSecondary: number } {
  const cleanPrimary = sanitizeIrodoriProgressLists(
    bookId,
    primaryMastered,
    primaryHard,
    validIds
  );
  const cleanSecondary = sanitizeIrodoriProgressLists(
    bookId,
    secondaryMastered,
    secondaryHard,
    validIds
  );

  const mergedMastered = new Set<string>(cleanPrimary.mastered);
  let addedFromSecondary = 0;

  for (const id of cleanSecondary.mastered) {
    if (!mergedMastered.has(id)) {
      mergedMastered.add(id);
      addedFromSecondary += 1;
    }
  }

  const mergedHard = new Set<string>();
  for (const id of cleanPrimary.hard) {
    if (!mergedMastered.has(id)) {
      mergedHard.add(id);
    }
  }
  for (const id of cleanSecondary.hard) {
    if (!mergedMastered.has(id) && !mergedHard.has(id)) {
      mergedHard.add(id);
      addedFromSecondary += 1;
    }
  }

  return {
    mastered: mergedMastered,
    hard: mergedHard,
    addedFromSecondary,
  };
}

FILE: src/utils/quizEngine.test.ts
================================================
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { KANJI } from '../data/kanjiData';
import type { LevelFilter, QuizModelKey } from '../types/kanji';
import { getWordMeta } from './morphology';
import {
  ALL_5_KEYS,
  buildQuizBank,
  buildQuizSession,
  hasMeaningCollision,
  makeQuestion,
  normalizeTermText,
  splitFuriganaParts,
} from './quizEngine';

function createDeterministicRng(seed = 42): () => number {
  let state = seed >>> 0;
  return () => {
    state = (1664525 * state + 1013904223) >>> 0;
    return state / 4294967296;
  };
}

test('all 2,500 quiz questions (500 kanji x 5 models) generate 4 unique valid options with exact answer included', () => {
  const rng = createDeterministicRng(1234);
  const bank = buildQuizBank('all', 'random5', KANJI);
  assert.equal(bank.length, 2500);

  for (const entry of bank) {
    const item = KANJI[entry.kanjiIdx];
    const q = makeQuestion(item, entry.typeKey, 'all', KANJI, rng);

    assert.equal(q.options.length, 4, `${item.id} (${entry.typeKey}) should have 4 options`);
    const labels = q.options.map(o => o.label);
    const ids = q.options.map(o => o.item.id);
    assert.equal(new Set(labels).size, 4, `${item.id} (${entry.typeKey}) duplicate labels: ${labels.join(', ')}`);
    assert.equal(new Set(ids).size, 4, `${item.id} (${entry.typeKey}) duplicate item ids`);
    assert.ok(ids.includes(item.id), `${item.id} (${entry.typeKey}) missing correct answer`);

    if (entry.typeKey === 'ex2k') {
      assert.ok(q.exMeta);
      assert.ok(q.exMeta.maskedEx.includes('（　　）'));
      const correctOpt = q.options.find(o => o.item.id === item.id)!;
      assert.equal(correctOpt.label, q.exMeta.targetSurface);
    }
  }
});

test('ex2k sentence questions enforce POS/grammatical compatibility across all 500 items', () => {
  const rng = createDeterministicRng(777);
  const posMismatches: string[] = [];

  for (const item of KANJI) {
    const q = makeQuestion(item, 'ex2k', 'all', KANJI, rng);
    const ctx = q.exMeta!.blankContext;
    for (const opt of q.options) {
      const optSubPos = getWordMeta(opt.item).subPos;
      if (ctx === 'verb_conjugated' && optSubPos !== 'verb') {
        posMismatches.push(`${item.id} (${item.k}) verb slot got ${opt.item.k} (${optSubPos})`);
      }
      if (ctx === 'iadj_conjugated' && optSubPos !== 'iadj') {
        posMismatches.push(`${item.id} (${item.k}) iadj slot got ${opt.item.k} (${optSubPos})`);
      }
      if (ctx === 'naadj_modifier' && optSubPos !== 'naadj') {
        posMismatches.push(`${item.id} (${item.k}) naadj slot got ${opt.item.k} (${optSubPos})`);
      }
      if (ctx === 'suru_verb' && optSubPos !== 'suru') {
        posMismatches.push(`${item.id} (${item.k}) suru slot got ${opt.item.k} (${optSubPos})`);
      }
      if (ctx === 'noun_general' && (optSubPos === 'verb' || optSubPos === 'iadj')) {
        posMismatches.push(`${item.id} (${item.k}) noun slot got ${opt.item.k} (${optSubPos})`);
      }
    }
  }

  assert.deepEqual(posMismatches, []);
});

test('level isolation keeps distractors strictly within the active difficulty level for Easy, Medium, and Hard', () => {
  const rng = createDeterministicRng(999);
  const levels: Exclude<LevelFilter, 'all'>[] = ['easy', 'medium', 'hard'];
  const crossLevelLeaks: string[] = [];

  for (const lv of levels) {
    const session = buildQuizSession(lv, 'random5', 'all', KANJI, rng);
    for (const q of session) {
      assert.equal(q.item.dif, lv);
      for (const opt of q.options) {
        // Hard has only 2 na-adjectives (丁寧, 複雑), so naadj_modifier in Hard must borrow 2 na-adj distractors for grammatical validity
        const isHardNaAdjException = lv === 'hard' && q.type === 'ex2k' && q.exMeta?.blankContext === 'naadj_modifier';
        if (opt.item.dif !== lv && !isHardNaAdjException) {
          crossLevelLeaks.push(`[${lv}] ${q.item.id} (${q.type}) leaked ${opt.item.id} (${opt.item.dif})`);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  assert.deepEqual(crossLevelLeaks, []);
});

test('every model key in ALL_5_KEYS produces distinct labels when running buildQuizSession', () => {
  const rng = createDeterministicRng(2026);
  for (const key of ALL_5_KEYS as QuizModelKey[]) {
    const questions = buildQuizSession('easy', key, 20, KANJI, rng);
    assert.equal(questions.length, 20);
    for (const q of questions) {
      assert.equal(q.type, key);
      assert.equal(q.options.length, 4);
    }
  }
});

test('N4 quiz questions never present synonymous meanings, homophones, or homographs as competing options', () => {
  const rng = createDeterministicRng(3030);
  const violations: string[] = [];

  for (const item of KANJI) {
    for (const key of ALL_5_KEYS) {
      const q = makeQuestion(item, key, 'all', KANJI, rng);
      const distractors = q.options.filter(o => o.item.id !== item.id);
      const normKs = new Set(q.options.map(o => normalizeTermText(o.item.k)));
      const normRs = new Set(q.options.map(o => normalizeTermText(o.item.r)));
      if (normKs.size !== 4) violations.push(`${item.id}/${key}: homograph collision`);
      if (normRs.size !== 4) violations.push(`${item.id}/${key}: homophone collision`);
      if (key === 'k2m' || key === 'm2k' || key === 'ex2k') {
        for (const d of distractors) {
          if (hasMeaningCollision(item.m, d.item.m)) {
            violations.push(`${item.id}/${key} (${item.m}) collided with ${d.item.id} (${d.item.m})`);
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  assert.deepEqual(violations, []);
});

test('splitFuriganaParts aligns kanji core cleanly and skips non-kanji terms', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(splitFuriganaParts('計画', 'けいかく'), {
    prefix: '',
    base: '計画',
    ruby: 'けいかく',
    suffix: '',
  });
  assert.deepEqual(splitFuriganaParts('食べる', 'たべる'), {
    prefix: '',
    base: '食',
    ruby: 'た',
    suffix: 'べる',
  });
  assert.deepEqual(splitFuriganaParts('新しい', 'あたらしい'), {
    prefix: '',
    base: '新',
    ruby: 'あたら',
    suffix: 'しい',
  });
  assert.deepEqual(splitFuriganaParts('お持ちください', 'おもちください'), {
    prefix: 'お',
    base: '持',
    ruby: 'も',
    suffix: 'ちください',
  });
  assert.deepEqual(splitFuriganaParts('～時間', '～じかん'), {
    prefix: '～',
    base: '時間',
    ruby: 'じかん',
    suffix: '',
  });
  assert.equal(splitFuriganaParts('だいたい', 'だいたい'), null);
  assert.equal(splitFuriganaParts('エアコン', 'えあこん'), null);
});



================================================
FILE: src/utils/quizEngine.ts

import { KANJI } from '../data/kanjiData';
import type {
  IrodoriChapterNumber,
  IrodoriChapterSelection,
  KanjiEntry,
  LevelFilter,
  QuizModelKey,
  QuizOption,
  QuizQuestion,
  QuizTypeChoice,
  SentenceBlank,
} from '../types/kanji';
import {
  analyzeSentenceBlank,
  conjugateWord,
  getWordMeta,
  matchTemplateSurface,
  stripEdgeTildes,
} from './morphology';

export interface QuizModelMeta {
  key: QuizModelKey;
  short: string;
  label: string;
  prompt: string;
}

export const QTYPES: Record<QuizModelKey, QuizModelMeta> = {
  k2r: {
    key: 'k2r',
    short: '漢→あ',
    label: 'Kanji → Hiragana',
    prompt: 'Pilih cara baca (hiragana) yang benar untuk:',
  },
  k2m: {
    key: 'k2m',
    short: '漢→ID',
    label: 'Kanji → Arti',
    prompt: 'Pilih arti yang tepat untuk:',
  },
  m2k: {
    key: 'm2k',
    short: 'ID→漢',
    label: 'Arti → Kanji',
    prompt: 'Pilih kanji yang artinya:',
  },
  r2k: {
    key: 'r2k',
    short: 'あ→漢',
    label: 'Hiragana → Kanji',
    prompt: 'Pilih kanji yang dibaca:',
  },
  ex2k: {
    key: 'ex2k',
    short: '📖→漢',
    label: 'Kalimat → Kanji',
    prompt: 'Kanji mana yang melengkapi kalimat ini?',
  },
};

export const CORE_4_KEYS: QuizModelKey[] = ['k2r', 'k2m', 'm2k', 'r2k'];
export const ALL_5_KEYS: QuizModelKey[] = ['k2r', 'k2m', 'm2k', 'r2k', 'ex2k'];
export const ABC_LABELS = ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D'] as const;

export function shuffled<T>(arr: readonly T[], rng: () => number = Math.random): T[] {
  const copy = arr.slice();
  for (let i = copy.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(rng() * (i + 1));
    [copy[i], copy[j]] = [copy[j], copy[i]];
  }
  return copy;
}

export function matchesChapterSelection(
  chapter: IrodoriChapterNumber | undefined,
  chapterFilter?: IrodoriChapterSelection
): boolean {
  if (chapterFilter === undefined || chapterFilter === 'all') return true;
  if (Array.isArray(chapterFilter)) {
    return chapter !== undefined && chapterFilter.includes(chapter);
  }
  return chapter === chapterFilter;
}

function isAllChaptersSelected(chapterFilter?: IrodoriChapterSelection): boolean {
  if (chapterFilter === 'all') return true;
  if (Array.isArray(chapterFilter)) return chapterFilter.length >= 18;
  return false;
}

/**
 * Formats a multi-chapter selection into a concise Indonesian label, e.g.
 * `Semua Bab (1–18)`, `Bab 3`, `Bab 1–3, 5, 8–9`.
 */
export function formatChapterSelectionLabel(
  chapters: readonly IrodoriChapterNumber[],
  totalChapters = 18
): string {
  const unique = Array.from(new Set(chapters)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
  if (unique.length === 0) return 'Belum ada bab dipilih';
  if (unique.length >= totalChapters) return `Semua Bab (1–${totalChapters})`;
  if (unique.length === 1) return `Bab ${unique[0]}`;

  const ranges: string[] = [];
  let start = unique[0];
  let end = unique[0];

  for (let i = 1; i < unique.length; i++) {
    if (unique[i] === end + 1) {
      end = unique[i];
    } else {
      ranges.push(start === end ? `${start}` : `${start}–${end}`);
      start = unique[i];
      end = unique[i];
    }
  }
  ranges.push(start === end ? `${start}` : `${start}–${end}`);

  if (ranges.length <= 4) {
    return `Bab ${ranges.join(', ')}`;
  }
  return `${unique.length} Bab (Bab ${ranges.slice(0, 3).join(', ')}, …)`;
}

export function getPoolIndices(
  levelFilter: LevelFilter,
  dataset: readonly KanjiEntry[] = KANJI,
  chapterFilter?: IrodoriChapterSelection
): number[] {
  const out: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < dataset.length; i++) {
    if (chapterFilter !== undefined) {
      if (matchesChapterSelection(dataset[i].chapter, chapterFilter)) {
        out.push(i);
      }
    } else if (levelFilter === 'all' || dataset[i].dif === levelFilter) {
      out.push(i);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

export function getActiveTypeKeys(choice: QuizTypeChoice): QuizModelKey[] {
  if (choice === 'random') return CORE_4_KEYS;
  if (choice === 'random5') return ALL_5_KEYS;
  return QTYPES[choice] ? [choice] : CORE_4_KEYS;
}

export const HAS_KANJI_RE = /[一-龯々ヶヵ]/;
const SENTENCE_PUNCT_RE = /[、。？！?!]/;
const KATAKANA_RE = /[ァ-ヴー]/;

export interface FuriganaParts {
  prefix: string;
  base: string;
  ruby: string;
  suffix: string;
}

/**
 * Splits a Japanese surface term and its hiragana reading into shared leading/trailing
 * kana (`prefix`/`suffix`) plus the kanji-containing core (`base`) and its aligned reading (`ruby`).
 * Returns `null` when the surface has no Kanji or matches its reading verbatim.
 */
export function splitFuriganaParts(surface: string, reading: string): FuriganaParts | null {
  const s = surface.trim();
  const r = reading.trim();
  if (!s || !r || s === r || !HAS_KANJI_RE.test(s)) return null;

  let prefixLen = 0;
  while (
    prefixLen < s.length &&
    prefixLen < r.length &&
    s[prefixLen] === r[prefixLen] &&
    !HAS_KANJI_RE.test(s[prefixLen])
  ) {
    prefixLen++;
  }

  let suffixLen = 0;
  while (
    suffixLen < s.length - prefixLen &&
    suffixLen < r.length - prefixLen &&
    s[s.length - 1 - suffixLen] === r[r.length - 1 - suffixLen] &&
    !HAS_KANJI_RE.test(s[s.length - 1 - suffixLen])
  ) {
    suffixLen++;
  }

  const base = s.slice(prefixLen, s.length - suffixLen);
  const ruby = r.slice(prefixLen, r.length - suffixLen);
  if (!base || !ruby || !HAS_KANJI_RE.test(base)) {
    return { prefix: '', base: s, ruby: r, suffix: '' };
  }

  return {
    prefix: s.slice(0, prefixLen),
    base,
    ruby,
    suffix: suffixLen > 0 ? s.slice(s.length - suffixLen) : '',
  };
}

/**
 * Determines whether a vocabulary entry is appropriate for a specific quiz model.
 * - `k2r` (Kanji → Hiragana) and `r2k` (Hiragana → Kanji) require at least one Kanji,
 *   a reading distinct from the surface form, no sentence punctuation, and a non-outlier
 *   reading length (<= 13) so kana-only words and full announcements never produce
 *   pointless hiragana→hiragana or obvious length-giveaway questions.
 * - `k2m`, `m2k`, and `ex2k` remain available for all entries (including kana-only words
 *   and polite expressions).
 */
export function isEntryEligibleForQuizModel(item: KanjiEntry, typeKey: QuizModelKey): boolean {
  if (typeKey === 'k2r' || typeKey === 'r2k') {
    if (!HAS_KANJI_RE.test(item.k)) return false;
    if (SENTENCE_PUNCT_RE.test(item.k)) return false;
    if (item.r.length > 13) return false;
    const normK = normalizeTermText(item.k);
    const normR = normalizeTermText(item.r);
    if (!normK || !normR || normK === normR) return false;
  }
  return true;
}

export interface QuizBankEntry {
  kanjiIdx: number;
  typeKey: QuizModelKey;
}

export function buildQuizBank(
  levelFilter: LevelFilter,
  choice: QuizTypeChoice,
  dataset: readonly KanjiEntry[] = KANJI,
  chapterFilter?: IrodoriChapterSelection
): QuizBankEntry[] {
  const indices = getPoolIndices(levelFilter, dataset, chapterFilter);
  const keys = getActiveTypeKeys(choice);
  const bank: QuizBankEntry[] = [];
  for (const kanjiIdx of indices) {
    const item = dataset[kanjiIdx];
    for (const typeKey of keys) {
      if (isEntryEligibleForQuizModel(item, typeKey)) {
        bank.push({ kanjiIdx, typeKey });
      }
    }
  }
  return bank;
}

export function dispOf(item: KanjiEntry, key: QuizModelKey, exMeta: SentenceBlank | null): string {
  if (key === 'k2r') return item.r;
  if (key === 'k2m') return item.m;
  if (key === 'ex2k' && exMeta) {
    if (exMeta.form === 'dict' && /[～〜~▲]/.test(item.k)) {
      const matched = matchTemplateSurface(item.k, exMeta.targetSurface);
      if (matched === exMeta.targetSurface) return exMeta.targetSurface;
    }
    const surface = conjugateWord(item, exMeta.form);
    if (exMeta.form === 'dict' && /[()（）]/.test(surface) && !/[()（）]/.test(exMeta.targetSurface)) {
      const expanded = surface.replace(/[()（）]/g, '');
      const shortened = surface.replace(/[（(][^）)]*[）)]/g, '').trim();
      if (item.k === exMeta.targetSurface) return surface;
      if (shortened === exMeta.targetSurface) return shortened;
      return expanded;
    }
    if (exMeta.form === 'dict' && /[～〜~]/.test(surface)) {
      return stripEdgeTildes(surface);
    }
    return surface;
  }
  return item.k;
}

/**
 * Normalizes Japanese terms or readings for homograph/homophone collision checks.
 */
export function normalizeTermText(text: string): string {
  return text
    .replace(/[()（）～〜~▲。、！？!?…\s・,./／]/g, '')
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();
}

/**
 * Extracts normalized Indonesian meaning glosses from a meaning string so
 * overlapping synonyms (e.g. `"menggunakan; memakai"` vs `"memakai"`) are detected.
 */
export function extractMeaningGlosses(meaning: string): Set<string> {
  const withoutParens = meaning
    .replace(/\([^)]*\)/g, ' ')
    .replace(/（[^）]*）/g, ' ');
  const source = withoutParens.trim() ? withoutParens : meaning;
  const parts = source.split(/[;；,/／]/);
  const glosses = new Set<string>();

  for (const part of parts) {
    const cleaned = part
      .replace(/[～〜~…\.?!。！？'"「」『』()（）]/g, '')
      .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase();
    if (cleaned) {
      glosses.add(cleaned);
    }
  }

  return glosses;
}

/**
 * Curated Indonesian synonym / near-synonym equivalence classes audited across
 * JLPT N4 and JF Irodori (Pemula, Dasar 1, Dasar 2).
 *
 * Any two vocabulary items whose normalized glosses map to the same concept ID
 * are prevented from competing as options in the same `k2m`, `m2k`, or `ex2k`
 * question (e.g. `だいたい: biasanya; kira-kira` vs `たいてい: umumnya; kebanyakan`,
 * or `～ごろ: sekitar ～` vs `～ぐらい: sekitar ～; kurang lebih ～`).

const GLOSS_TO_CONCEPTS = new Map<string, string[]>();
for (const [conceptId, phrases] of Object.entries(MEANING_SYNONYM_GROUPS)) {
  for (const phrase of phrases) {
    const key = phrase.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().toLowerCase();
    let list = GLOSS_TO_CONCEPTS.get(key);
    if (!list) {
      list = [];
      GLOSS_TO_CONCEPTS.set(key, list);
    }
    list.push(conceptId);
  }
}

const BENIGN_GLOSS_PREFIX_RE = /^(?:pada|seperti|secara|dengan|sudah|sedang|untuk|sangat|agak)\s+/;

/**
 * Maps a meaning string to its semantic concept tags (via exact glosses and
 * benign adverbial prefix stripping, never stripping negation markers).
 */
export function extractMeaningConceptTags(meaning: string): Set<string> {
  const glosses = extractMeaningGlosses(meaning);
  const concepts = new Set<string>();

  for (const gloss of glosses) {
    const direct = GLOSS_TO_CONCEPTS.get(gloss);
    if (direct) {
      for (const c of direct) concepts.add(c);
    }
    const stripped = gloss.replace(BENIGN_GLOSS_PREFIX_RE, '').trim();
    if (stripped && stripped !== gloss) {
      const fromStripped = GLOSS_TO_CONCEPTS.get(stripped);
      if (fromStripped) {
        for (const c of fromStripped) concepts.add(c);
      }
    }
  }

  return concepts;
}

/**
 * Returns true if two Indonesian meanings share any normalized gloss, full meaning,
 * or Indonesian synonym concept group — preventing ambiguous/synonymous options
 * from appearing in the same multiple-choice question.
 */
export function hasMeaningCollision(a: string, b: string): boolean {
  const fullA = a.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().toLowerCase();
  const fullB = b.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (fullA === fullB) return true;

  const glossesA = extractMeaningGlosses(a);
  const glossesB = extractMeaningGlosses(b);
  for (const g of glossesA) {
    if (glossesB.has(g)) return true;
  }

  const conceptsA = extractMeaningConceptTags(a);
  if (conceptsA.size > 0) {
    const conceptsB = extractMeaningConceptTags(b);
    for (const c of conceptsA) {
      if (conceptsB.has(c)) return true;
    }
  }

  return false;
}

function sharedKanji(a: string, b: string): number {
  const isKanji = (ch: string) => /[一-龯]/.test(ch);
  const setA = new Set([...a].filter(isKanji));
  const setB = new Set([...b].filter(isKanji));
  let count = 0;
  setA.forEach(ch => {
    if (setB.has(ch)) count++;
  });
  return count;
}

function kanaSim(a: string, b: string): number {
  const grams = (s: string) => {
    const g = new Set<string>();
    for (let i = 0; i < s.length - 1; i++) g.add(s.slice(i, i + 2));
    return g;
  };
  const gA = grams(a);
  const gB = grams(b);
  let score = 0;
  gA.forEach(x => {
    if (gB.has(x)) score++;
  });
  if (a[0] && a[0] === b[0]) score += 0.75;
  if (Math.abs(a.length - b.length) <= 1) score += 0.25;
  return score;
}

type ScriptClass = 'kanji' | 'katakana' | 'hiragana';

function classifyScript(text: string): ScriptClass {
  if (HAS_KANJI_RE.test(text)) return 'kanji';
  if (KATAKANA_RE.test(text)) return 'katakana';
  return 'hiragana';
}

function isLengthPlausible(targetLabel: string, candLabel: string, key: QuizModelKey): boolean {
  const tLen = targetLabel.length;
  const cLen = candLabel.length;
  const diff = Math.abs(tLen - cLen);
  const minLen = Math.max(1, Math.min(tLen, cLen));
  const maxLen = Math.max(tLen, cLen);
  const ratio = maxLen / minLen;

  if (key === 'k2m') {
    if (diff <= 6) return true;
    if (tLen >= 28) {
      return cLen >= Math.floor(tLen * 0.5) && ratio <= 2.2;
    }
    return ratio <= 1.65 && diff <= 12;
  }

  // Japanese option labels (k2r, r2k, m2k, ex2k)
  if (diff <= 2) return true;
  if (tLen >= 10) {
    return cLen >= Math.max(7, Math.floor(tLen * 0.62)) && ratio <= 1.65;
  }
  return ratio <= 1.45 && diff <= 3;
}

function sharedEndingBonus(a: string, b: string): number {
  const endings = [
    '(ください)',
    'ください',
    'でしょうか',
    'ませんか',
    'ですか',
    'ました',
    '(でした)',
    'でした',
    'します',
    'ます',
    'する',
  ];
  for (const suffix of endings) {
    if (a.endsWith(suffix) && b.endsWith(suffix)) {
      return suffix.length >= 4 ? 4.5 : 2.5;
    }
  }
  return 0;
}

function formatParityScore(targetLabel: string, candLabel: string): number {
  let score = 0;
  const tHasQ = /[？?]/.test(targetLabel);
  const cHasQ = /[？?]/.test(candLabel);
  if (tHasQ && cHasQ) score += 5;
  else if (tHasQ !== cHasQ) score -= 4;

  const tHasParen = /[()（）]/.test(targetLabel);
  const cHasParen = /[()（）]/.test(candLabel);
  if (tHasParen && cHasParen) score += 4;
  else if (tHasParen !== cHasParen) score -= 3;

  const tHasTilde = /[～〜~]/.test(targetLabel);
  const cHasTilde = /[～〜~]/.test(candLabel);
  if (tHasTilde && cHasTilde) score += 3.5;
  else if (tHasTilde !== cHasTilde) score -= 2.5;

  const tHasExcl = /[！!]/.test(targetLabel);
  const cHasExcl = /[！!]/.test(candLabel);
  if (tHasExcl && cHasExcl) score += 3;
  else if (tHasExcl !== cHasExcl) score -= 2;

  return score;
}

/**
 * Checks whether a candidate distractor is grammatically compatible with the blank context
 * in `ex2k` so that conjugated verbs only compete with conjugated verbs, i-adj with i-adj, etc.
 */
function isCompatibleForBlank(candidate: KanjiEntry, exMeta: SentenceBlank): boolean {
  const { subPos } = getWordMeta(candidate);
  switch (exMeta.blankContext) {
    case 'verb_conjugated':
      return subPos === 'verb';
    case 'iadj_conjugated':
      return subPos === 'iadj';
    case 'naadj_modifier':
      return subPos === 'naadj';
    case 'suru_verb':
      return subPos === 'suru';
    case 'noun_general':
      return subPos === 'noun' || subPos === 'suru';
  }
}

interface EntryCollisionMeta {
  normK: string;
  normR: string;
  fullM: string;
  glosses: Set<string>;
  concepts: Set<string>;
}

interface DatasetCollisionIndex {
  byId: Map<string, EntryCollisionMeta>;
  sameKReadings: Map<string, Set<string>>;
  sameKMeanings: Map<string, EntryCollisionMeta[]>;
  sameRKs: Map<string, Set<string>>;
}

const datasetIndexCache = new WeakMap<readonly KanjiEntry[], DatasetCollisionIndex>();

function getDatasetCollisionIndex(dataset: readonly KanjiEntry[]): DatasetCollisionIndex {
  const cached = datasetIndexCache.get(dataset);
  if (cached) return cached;

  const byId = new Map<string, EntryCollisionMeta>();
  const sameKReadings = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
  const sameKMeanings = new Map<string, EntryCollisionMeta[]>();
  const sameRKs = new Map<string, Set<string>>();

  for (const e of dataset) {
    const meta: EntryCollisionMeta = {
      normK: normalizeTermText(e.k),
      normR: normalizeTermText(e.r),
      fullM: e.m.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().toLowerCase(),
      glosses: extractMeaningGlosses(e.m),
      concepts: extractMeaningConceptTags(e.m),
    };
    byId.set(e.id, meta);

    if (meta.normK) {
      let rSet = sameKReadings.get(meta.normK);
      if (!rSet) {
        rSet = new Set<string>();
        sameKReadings.set(meta.normK, rSet);
      }
      if (meta.normR) rSet.add(meta.normR);

      let mList = sameKMeanings.get(meta.normK);
      if (!mList) {
        mList = [];
        sameKMeanings.set(meta.normK, mList);
      }
      mList.push(meta);
    }

    if (meta.normR) {
      let kSet = sameRKs.get(meta.normR);
      if (!kSet) {
        kSet = new Set<string>();
        sameRKs.set(meta.normR, kSet);
      }
      if (meta.normK) kSet.add(meta.normK);
    }
  }

  const index: DatasetCollisionIndex = { byId, sameKReadings, sameKMeanings, sameRKs };
  datasetIndexCache.set(dataset, index);
  return index;
}

function getEntryMeta(item: KanjiEntry, index: DatasetCollisionIndex): EntryCollisionMeta {
  const found = index.byId.get(item.id);
  if (found) return found;
  return {
    normK: normalizeTermText(item.k),
    normR: normalizeTermText(item.r),
    fullM: item.m.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().toLowerCase(),
    glosses: extractMeaningGlosses(item.m),
    concepts: extractMeaningConceptTags(item.m),
  };
}

function metaHasMeaningCollision(a: EntryCollisionMeta, b: EntryCollisionMeta): boolean {
  if (a.fullM === b.fullM) return true;
  for (const g of a.glosses) {
    if (b.glosses.has(g)) return true;
  }
  for (const c of a.concepts) {
    if (b.concepts.has(c)) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

export function pickDistractors(
  item: KanjiEntry,
  key: QuizModelKey,
  count: number,
  exMeta: SentenceBlank | null,
  levelFilter: LevelFilter = 'all',
  dataset: readonly KanjiEntry[] = KANJI,
  rng: () => number = Math.random,
  chapterFilter?: IrodoriChapterSelection
): KanjiEntry[] {
  const index = getDatasetCollisionIndex(dataset);
  const targetDisp = dispOf(item, key, exMeta);
  const targetMeta = getEntryMeta(item, index);
  const targetNormK = targetMeta.normK;
  const targetNormR = targetMeta.normR;

  const sameKReadings = index.sameKReadings.get(targetNormK);
  const sameKMeanings = index.sameKMeanings.get(targetNormK);
  const sameRKs = index.sameRKs.get(targetNormR);

  const checkMeaning = key === 'k2m' || key === 'm2k' || key === 'ex2k';
  const targetScript = classifyScript(targetDisp);

  const isEligibleBase = (candidate: KanjiEntry): boolean => {
    if (candidate.id === item.id) return false;
    const candDisp = dispOf(candidate, key, exMeta);
    if (candDisp === targetDisp) return false;

    const candMeta = getEntryMeta(candidate, index);
    const candNormK = candMeta.normK;
    const candNormR = candMeta.normR;

    // Block homographs & homophones across ALL 5 models so two options never represent the same word/reading.
    if (candNormK && candNormK === targetNormK) return false;
    if (candNormR && candNormR === targetNormR) return false;

    // In r2k (Hiragana → Kanji), every distractor must also contain Kanji so kana-only options
    // never appear as instant visual giveaways.
    if (key === 'r2k' && (!HAS_KANJI_RE.test(candidate.k) || candNormK === candNormR)) {
      return false;
    }

    if (key === 'k2r' && sameKReadings?.has(candNormR)) return false;
    if (key === 'r2k' && sameRKs?.has(candNormK)) return false;

    if (checkMeaning) {
      if (metaHasMeaningCollision(candMeta, targetMeta)) return false;
      if (key === 'k2m' && sameKMeanings?.some(m => metaHasMeaningCollision(candMeta, m))) {
        return false;
      }
    }

    return true;
  };

  const matchesActiveScope = (it: KanjiEntry): boolean => {
    if (chapterFilter !== undefined) {
      return matchesChapterSelection(it.chapter, chapterFilter);
    }
    return levelFilter === 'all' || it.dif === levelFilter;
  };

  // Prioritize candidates from the active level/chapter filter so focused quizzes keep relevant distractors.
  const sameLevelAll = dataset.filter(it => matchesActiveScope(it) && isEligibleBase(it));
  const crossLevelAll =
    (chapterFilter !== undefined ? isAllChaptersSelected(chapterFilter) : levelFilter === 'all')
      ? []
      : dataset.filter(it => !matchesActiveScope(it) && isEligibleBase(it));

  const scoreCandidate = (it: KanjiEntry, isSameLevel: boolean): number => {
    const candDisp = dispOf(it, key, exMeta);
    const lenDiff = Math.abs(targetDisp.length - candDisp.length);
    const minLen = Math.max(1, Math.min(targetDisp.length, candDisp.length));
    const maxLen = Math.max(targetDisp.length, candDisp.length);
    const lenRatio = maxLen / minLen;

    let s = isSameLevel ? 100 : 0;

    if (key === 'k2r') {
      const lengthScore =
        (lenDiff <= 1 ? 4.5 : lenDiff === 2 ? 2.0 : 0) -
        lenDiff * 2.4 -
        (lenRatio > 1.5 && lenDiff >= 3 ? 12 : 0);
      s +=
        lengthScore +
        formatParityScore(targetDisp, candDisp) +
        sharedEndingBonus(targetDisp, candDisp) +
        sharedKanji(item.k, it.k) * 2.2 +
        kanaSim(item.r, it.r) * 1.1;
    } else if (key === 'r2k') {
      const lengthScore =
        (lenDiff <= 1 ? 4.5 : lenDiff === 2 ? 2.0 : 0) -
        lenDiff * 2.4 -
        (lenRatio > 1.5 && lenDiff >= 3 ? 12 : 0);
      s +=
        lengthScore +
        formatParityScore(targetDisp, candDisp) +
        sharedEndingBonus(targetDisp, candDisp) +
        kanaSim(item.r, it.r) * 2.0 +
        sharedKanji(item.k, it.k) * 1.5;
    } else if (key === 'm2k') {
      const candScript = classifyScript(candDisp);
      const scriptScore = candScript === targetScript ? 4.0 : -4.0;
      const lengthScore =
        (lenDiff <= 1 ? 4.0 : lenDiff === 2 ? 1.8 : 0) -
        lenDiff * 2.2 -
        (lenRatio > 1.5 && lenDiff >= 3 ? 12 : 0);
      s +=
        lengthScore +
        scriptScore +
        formatParityScore(targetDisp, candDisp) +
        sharedEndingBonus(targetDisp, candDisp) +
        sharedKanji(item.k, it.k) * 1.5 +
        (it.dif === item.dif ? 0.5 : 0);
    } else if (key === 'ex2k' && exMeta) {
      const itMeta = getWordMeta(it);
      const exactSubPos = itMeta.subPos === exMeta.subPos;
      const compatible = isCompatibleForBlank(it, exMeta);
      const candScript = classifyScript(candDisp);
      const scriptScore = candScript === targetScript ? 3.5 : -3.5;
      const lengthScore =
        (lenDiff <= 1 ? 3.5 : lenDiff === 2 ? 1.5 : 0) -
        lenDiff * 2.0 -
        (lenRatio > 1.5 && lenDiff >= 3 ? 10 : 0);
      s +=
        (compatible ? 50 : -50) +
        (exactSubPos ? 12 : 0) +
        lengthScore +
        scriptScore +
        formatParityScore(targetDisp, candDisp) +
        sharedKanji(item.k, it.k) * 1.8 +
        kanaSim(item.r, it.r) +
        (it.dif === item.dif ? 0.5 : 0);
    } else {
      // k2m: strongly match meaning length & punctuation/parenthesis style so long explanations
      // or parenthesized notes never stand out as obvious giveaways.
      const lengthScore =
        (lenDiff <= 4 ? 4.5 : lenDiff <= 8 ? 2.0 : 0) -
        lenDiff * 0.65 -
        (lenRatio > 1.75 && lenDiff >= 7 ? 10 : 0);
      s +=
        lengthScore +
        formatParityScore(targetDisp, candDisp) +
        (it.dif === item.dif ? 0.5 : 0) +
        kanaSim(item.r, it.r) * 0.2;
    }
    return s + rng() * 0.6;
  };

  // Build tiered pools balancing scope isolation, grammatical compatibility, and length plausibility:
  // - In chapter-scoped quizzes (Irodori), borrowing plausible same-length distractors from other
  //   chapters of the same book beats showing 1 long phrase against 3 short words from a small chapter.
  // - In JLPT N4 difficulty-filtered quizzes (Easy/Medium/Hard), keep strict level isolation first
  //   (only crossing levels if an ex2k rare subPos lacks enough candidates, e.g. Hard na-adjectives).
  const isChapterScoped = chapterFilter !== undefined;
  const isPlausible = (it: KanjiEntry) => isLengthPlausible(targetDisp, dispOf(it, key, exMeta), key);

  const tiers: KanjiEntry[][] =
    key === 'ex2k' && exMeta
      ? isChapterScoped
        ? [
            sameLevelAll.filter(it => isCompatibleForBlank(it, exMeta) && isPlausible(it)),
            crossLevelAll.filter(it => isCompatibleForBlank(it, exMeta) && isPlausible(it)),
            sameLevelAll.filter(it => isCompatibleForBlank(it, exMeta)),
            crossLevelAll.filter(it => isCompatibleForBlank(it, exMeta)),
            sameLevelAll.filter(isPlausible),
            crossLevelAll.filter(isPlausible),
            sameLevelAll,
            crossLevelAll,
          ]
        : [
            sameLevelAll.filter(it => isCompatibleForBlank(it, exMeta)),
            crossLevelAll.filter(it => isCompatibleForBlank(it, exMeta)),
            sameLevelAll,
            crossLevelAll,
          ]
      : isChapterScoped
        ? [
            sameLevelAll.filter(isPlausible),
            crossLevelAll.filter(isPlausible),
            sameLevelAll,
            crossLevelAll,
          ]
        : [sameLevelAll, crossLevelAll];

  const seenLabels = new Set<string>([targetDisp]);
  const seenIds = new Set<string>([item.id]);
  const seenNormKs = new Set<string>(targetNormK ? [targetNormK] : []);
  const seenNormRs = new Set<string>(targetNormR ? [targetNormR] : []);
  const out: KanjiEntry[] = [];
  const outMetas: EntryCollisionMeta[] = [];

  const fillFromTiers = (enforceDistractorMeaningDistinct: boolean) => {
    for (let tierIdx = 0; tierIdx < tiers.length && out.length < count; tierIdx++) {
      const tierCandidates = tiers[tierIdx]
        .filter(it => !seenIds.has(it.id))
        .map(it => ({
          it,
          s: scoreCandidate(it, matchesActiveScope(it)),
        }))
        .sort((a, b) => b.s - a.s);

      for (const { it } of tierCandidates) {
        const label = dispOf(it, key, exMeta);
        if (seenLabels.has(label)) continue;
        const candMeta = getEntryMeta(it, index);
        if (candMeta.normK && seenNormKs.has(candMeta.normK)) continue;
        if (candMeta.normR && seenNormRs.has(candMeta.normR)) continue;
        if (
          enforceDistractorMeaningDistinct &&
          checkMeaning &&
          outMetas.some(prev => metaHasMeaningCollision(prev, candMeta))
        ) {
          continue;
        }

        seenLabels.add(label);
        seenIds.add(it.id);
        if (candMeta.normK) seenNormKs.add(candMeta.normK);
        if (candMeta.normR) seenNormRs.add(candMeta.normR);
        out.push(it);
        outMetas.push(candMeta);
        if (out.length === count) break;
      }
    }
  };

  fillFromTiers(true);
  if (out.length < count) {
    fillFromTiers(false);
  }

  return out;
}

function resolvePromptHint(
  item: KanjiEntry,
  typeKey: QuizModelKey,
  dataset: readonly KanjiEntry[]
): string | undefined {
  const index = getDatasetCollisionIndex(dataset);
  const meta = getEntryMeta(item, index);

  if (typeKey === 'k2r') {
    const distinctReadings = index.sameKReadings.get(meta.normK);
    if (distinctReadings && distinctReadings.size > 1) {
      return `Arti: ${item.m}`;
    }
  } else if (typeKey === 'k2m') {
    const distinctReadings = index.sameKReadings.get(meta.normK);
    if (distinctReadings && distinctReadings.size > 1) {
      return `Baca: ${item.r}`;
    }
  } else if (typeKey === 'r2k') {
    const distinctForms = index.sameRKs.get(meta.normR);
    if (distinctForms && distinctForms.size > 1) {
      return `Arti: ${item.m}`;
    }
  }

  return undefined;
}

export function makeQuestion(
  item: KanjiEntry,
  typeKey: QuizModelKey,
  levelFilter: LevelFilter = 'all',
  dataset: readonly KanjiEntry[] = KANJI,
  rng: () => number = Math.random,
  chapterFilter?: IrodoriChapterSelection
): QuizQuestion {
  const exMeta = typeKey === 'ex2k' ? analyzeSentenceBlank(item) : null;
  const distractors = pickDistractors(
    item,
    typeKey,
    3,
    exMeta,
    levelFilter,
    dataset,
    rng,
    chapterFilter
  );
  const options: QuizOption[] = shuffled([item, ...distractors], rng).map(optItem => ({
    item: optItem,
    label: dispOf(optItem, typeKey, exMeta),
  }));
  const promptHint = resolvePromptHint(item, typeKey, dataset);

  return {
    item,
    type: typeKey,
    exMeta,
    options,
    ...(promptHint ? { promptHint } : {}),
  };
}

export function buildQuizSession(
  levelFilter: LevelFilter,
  choice: QuizTypeChoice,
  requestedCount: number | 'all',
  dataset: readonly KanjiEntry[] = KANJI,
  rng: () => number = Math.random,
  chapterFilter?: IrodoriChapterSelection
): QuizQuestion[] {
  const bank = shuffled(buildQuizBank(levelFilter, choice, dataset, chapterFilter), rng);
  if (bank.length === 0) return [];
  const count =
    requestedCount === 'all'
      ? bank.length
      : Math.max(1, Math.min(Math.floor(requestedCount) || 10, bank.length));

  return bank.slice(0, count).map(entry =>
    makeQuestion(dataset[entry.kanjiIdx], entry.typeKey, levelFilter, dataset, rng, chapterFilter)
  );
}

export function calculateQuizScore(rightCount: number, totalAnswered: number): number {
  if (totalAnswered <= 0) return 0;
  return Math.round((rightCount / totalAnswered) * 100);
}

FILE: src/utils/quizHistorySync.ts

import { IRODORI_BOOKS } from '../data/irodoriBooks';
import { IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS } from '../data/irodoriShared';
import { KANJI, LEVELS } from '../data/kanjiData';
import type {
  IrodoriChapterNumber,
  KanjiEntry,
  LevelFilter,
  QuizAnswerSnapshot,
  QuizDatasetId,
  QuizModelKey,
  QuizSessionHistoryEntry,
  QuizTypeChoice,
} from '../types/kanji';
import { analyzeSentenceBlank } from './morphology';
import { isValidFirebaseUid } from './progressSync';
import { calculateQuizScore, dispOf, formatChapterSelectionLabel } from './quizEngine';
import { MAX_QUIZ_HISTORY } from './quizStats';

// Volumetric & regex boundaries synchronized verbatim with firebase-blueprint.json & firestore.rules
export const QUIZ_DATASET_IDS: readonly QuizDatasetId[] = [
  'n4',
  'pemula',
  'dasar1',
  'dasar2',
] as const;

export const MAX_CLOUD_QUIZ_SESSIONS = MAX_QUIZ_HISTORY; // 15
export const MAX_CLOUD_QUIZ_SESSION_TOKEN_LENGTH = 12000;
export const MAX_CLOUD_QUIZ_JOINED_LENGTH = 160000;
export const MAX_CLOUD_SESSION_ID_LENGTH = 48;

const QUIZ_SESSION_TOKEN_BODY =
  '[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+\\|[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,3})?Z\\|(all|easy|medium|hard)\\|(random|random5|k2r|k2m|m2k|r2k|ex2k)\\|[0-9]{1,3}\\|[1-9][0-9]{0,2}\\|(100|[0-9]{1,2})\\|(|all|[1-9][0-9]?(\\.[1-9][0-9]?)*)\\|(|[1-9][0-9]{0,2}:[0-9]:(n|irp|ir|ir2_)[1-9][0-9]{0,3}(:(n|irp|ir|ir2_)[1-9][0-9]{0,3})?(;[1-9][0-9]{0,2}:[0-9]:(n|irp|ir|ir2_)[1-9][0-9]{0,3}(:(n|irp|ir|ir2_)[1-9][0-9]{0,3})?)*)';

export const QUIZ_SESSION_TOKEN_PATTERN = new RegExp(`^${QUIZ_SESSION_TOKEN_BODY}$`);
export const QUIZ_SESSION_COMMA_JOINED_PATTERN = new RegExp(
  `^${QUIZ_SESSION_TOKEN_BODY}(,${QUIZ_SESSION_TOKEN_BODY})*$`
);
export const QUIZ_SESSION_TILDE_JOINED_PATTERN = new RegExp(
  `^${QUIZ_SESSION_TOKEN_BODY}(~${QUIZ_SESSION_TOKEN_BODY})*$`
);

const MODEL_ORDER: readonly QuizModelKey[] = ['k2r', 'k2m', 'm2k', 'r2k', 'ex2k'] as const;
const MODEL_TO_INDEX: Record<QuizModelKey, number> = {
  k2r: 0,
  k2m: 1,
  m2k: 2,
  r2k: 3,
  ex2k: 4,
};

const VALID_LEVELS = new Set<LevelFilter>(['all', 'easy', 'medium', 'hard']);
const VALID_MODES = new Set<QuizTypeChoice>([
  'random',
  'random5',
  'k2r',
  'k2m',
  'm2k',
  'r2k',
  'ex2k',
]);

const datasetMapCache = new Map<QuizDatasetId, ReadonlyMap<string, KanjiEntry>>();

export interface QuizHistorySyncPayload {
  uid: string;
  datasetId: QuizDatasetId;
  sessions: string[];
}

export function isValidQuizDatasetId(raw: unknown): raw is QuizDatasetId {
  return raw === 'n4' || raw === 'pemula' || raw === 'dasar1' || raw === 'dasar2';
}

export function getQuizDatasetEntries(datasetId: QuizDatasetId): readonly KanjiEntry[] {
  if (datasetId === 'n4') return KANJI;
  return IRODORI_BOOKS[datasetId].data;
}

export function getQuizDatasetMap(datasetId: QuizDatasetId): ReadonlyMap<string, KanjiEntry> {
  const cached = datasetMapCache.get(datasetId);
  if (cached) return cached;
  const map = new Map<string, KanjiEntry>();
  for (const entry of getQuizDatasetEntries(datasetId)) {
    map.set(entry.id, entry);
  }
  datasetMapCache.set(datasetId, map);
  return map;
}

function sanitizeSessionId(rawId: string, fallbackSeed: number): string {
  const cleaned = rawId.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, '').slice(0, MAX_CLOUD_SESSION_ID_LENGTH);
  return cleaned.length > 0 ? cleaned : `q-${fallbackSeed}`;
}

function normalizeIsoTimestamp(raw: string): string {
  const ms = Date.parse(raw);
  if (Number.isNaN(ms)) return new Date(0).toISOString();
  return new Date(ms).toISOString();
}

/**
 * Extracts compact chapter scope code for Irodori sessions (`all` or dot-separated chapters `1.2.5`).
 * Supports both structured `entry.chapters` and legacy `entry.scopeLabel` strings like `"Bab 1–3, 5"`.
 */
function encodeScopeCode(entry: QuizSessionHistoryEntry, datasetId: QuizDatasetId): string {
  if (datasetId === 'n4') return '';

  if (entry.chapters && entry.chapters.length > 0) {
    const unique = [...new Set(entry.chapters)]
      .filter(ch => Number.isInteger(ch) && ch >= 1 && ch <= 18)
      .sort((a, b) => a - b);
    if (unique.length >= IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS.length) return 'all';
    if (unique.length > 0) return unique.join('.');
  }

  const label = entry.scopeLabel?.trim();
  if (!label) return '';
  if (label.startsWith('Semua Bab')) return 'all';

  // Parse legacy ranges such as "Bab 3" or "Bab 1–3, 5, 8–9"
  const rangeBody = label.replace(/^Bab\s+/i, '');
  if (/^\d+(?:\s*[–-]\s*\d+)?(?:\s*,\s*\d+(?:\s*[–-]\s*\d+)?)*$/.test(rangeBody)) {
    const chapters = new Set<number>();
    for (const part of rangeBody.split(',')) {
      const m = part.trim().match(/^(\d+)(?:\s*[–-]\s*(\d+))?$/);
      if (!m) continue;
      const start = Number(m[1]);
      const end = m[2] ? Number(m[2]) : start;
      for (let c = Math.max(1, start); c <= Math.min(18, end); c++) {
        chapters.add(c);
      }
    }
    const sorted = [...chapters].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    if (sorted.length >= IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS.length) return 'all';
    if (sorted.length > 0) return sorted.join('.');
  }

  return '';
}

function decodeScopeCode(
  scopeCode: string,
  level: LevelFilter,
  datasetId: QuizDatasetId
): { scopeLabel: string; chapters?: IrodoriChapterNumber[] } {
  if (datasetId === 'n4') {
    return { scopeLabel: `Level ${LEVELS[level].label}` };
  }
  if (scopeCode === 'all') {
    const chapters = [...IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS];
    return {
      scopeLabel: formatChapterSelectionLabel(chapters, IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS.length),
      chapters,
    };
  }
  if (scopeCode.length > 0) {
    const unique = new Set<IrodoriChapterNumber>();
    for (const part of scopeCode.split('.')) {
      const n = Number(part);
      if (Number.isInteger(n) && n >= 1 && n <= 18) {
        unique.add(n as IrodoriChapterNumber);
      }
    }
    const chapters = [...unique].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    if (chapters.length > 0) {
      return {
        scopeLabel: formatChapterSelectionLabel(chapters, IRODORI_CHAPTER_NUMBERS.length),
        chapters,
      };
    }
  }
  return { scopeLabel: `Level ${LEVELS[level].label}` };
}

/**
 * Fallback reverse lookup for legacy local wrong-answer snapshots that predate `chosenId`.
 */
function inferChosenIdForLegacySnapshot(
  ans: QuizAnswerSnapshot,
  datasetId: QuizDatasetId
): string | null {
  const entries = getQuizDatasetEntries(datasetId);
  for (const item of entries) {
    if (item.id === ans.itemId) continue;
    if (ans.type === 'k2r' && item.r === ans.chosenLabel && (!ans.chosenM || item.m === ans.chosenM)) {
      return item.id;
    }
    if (ans.type === 'k2m' && item.m === ans.chosenLabel) {
      return item.id;
    }
    if (
      (ans.type === 'm2k' || ans.type === 'r2k') &&
      item.k === ans.chosenLabel &&
      (!ans.chosenM || item.m === ans.chosenM)
    ) {
      return item.id;
    }
    if (ans.type === 'ex2k' && ans.chosenM && item.m === ans.chosenM) {
      return item.id;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

function packAnswerTokens(
  answers: readonly QuizAnswerSnapshot[] | undefined,
  datasetId: QuizDatasetId,
  maxBytes: number
): string {
  if (!answers || answers.length === 0 || maxBytes <= 0) return '';

  const byId = getQuizDatasetMap(datasetId);
  const encodedRows: { no: number; correct: boolean; token: string }[] = [];

  for (const ans of answers) {
    if (!Number.isInteger(ans.no) || ans.no < 1 || ans.no > 500) continue;
    if (!byId.has(ans.itemId)) continue;
    const typeIdx = MODEL_TO_INDEX[ans.type];
    if (typeIdx === undefined) continue;

    // 0..4 = wrong (k2r..ex2k), 5..9 = correct (k2r..ex2k)
    const code = typeIdx + (ans.correct ? 5 : 0);
    if (ans.correct) {
      encodedRows.push({
        no: ans.no,
        correct: true,
        token: `${ans.no}:${code}:${ans.itemId}`,
      });
    } else {
      const resolvedChosenId =
        ans.chosenId && byId.has(ans.chosenId)
          ? ans.chosenId
          : inferChosenIdForLegacySnapshot(ans, datasetId);
      const token = resolvedChosenId
        ? `${ans.no}:${code}:${ans.itemId}:${resolvedChosenId}`
        : `${ans.no}:${code}:${ans.itemId}`;
      encodedRows.push({
        no: ans.no,
        correct: false,
        token,
      });
    }
  }

  if (encodedRows.length === 0) return '';

  encodedRows.sort((a, b) => a.no - b.no);
  const fullJoined = encodedRows.map(r => r.token).join(';');
  if (fullJoined.length <= maxBytes) return fullJoined;

  // Prioritize preserving ALL wrong answers first if a huge session ever approaches the per-token byte ceiling
  const selected: typeof encodedRows = [];
  let usedBytes = 0;

  const tryAdd = (row: (typeof encodedRows)[number]) => {
    const needed = row.token.length + (selected.length > 0 ? 1 : 0);
    if (usedBytes + needed <= maxBytes) {
      selected.push(row);
      usedBytes += needed;
    }
  };

  for (const row of encodedRows) {
    if (!row.correct) tryAdd(row);
  }
  for (const row of encodedRows) {
    if (row.correct) tryAdd(row);
  }

  selected.sort((a, b) => a.no - b.no);
  return selected.map(r => r.token).join(';');
}

/**
 * Encodes one quiz session into an ultra-compact pure-ASCII ID-reference token
 * (~1 KB for a 100-question session, >90% smaller than raw text snapshots).
 */
export function encodeQuizSessionCompact(
  entry: QuizSessionHistoryEntry,
  datasetId: QuizDatasetId,
  includeAnswers = true
): string | null {
  if (!Number.isInteger(entry.total) || entry.total <= 0) return null;
  const total = Math.min(500, entry.total);
  const correct = Math.max(0, Math.min(total, Math.floor(entry.correct)));
  const score = calculateQuizScore(correct, total);
  const level: LevelFilter = VALID_LEVELS.has(entry.level) ? entry.level : 'all';
  const mode: QuizTypeChoice = VALID_MODES.has(entry.mode) ? entry.mode : 'random';
  const completedAt = normalizeIsoTimestamp(entry.completedAt);
  const id = sanitizeSessionId(entry.id, Date.parse(completedAt) || Date.now());
  const scopeCode = encodeScopeCode(entry, datasetId);

  const headerPrefix = `${id}|${completedAt}|${level}|${mode}|${correct}|${total}|${score}|${scopeCode}|`;
  const maxAnswerBytes = Math.max(0, MAX_CLOUD_QUIZ_SESSION_TOKEN_LENGTH - headerPrefix.length);
  const answersPacked = includeAnswers
    ? packAnswerTokens(entry.answers, datasetId, maxAnswerBytes)
    : '';

  const token = `${headerPrefix}${answersPacked}`;
  if (
    token.length < 25 ||
    token.length > MAX_CLOUD_QUIZ_SESSION_TOKEN_LENGTH ||
    !QUIZ_SESSION_TOKEN_PATTERN.test(token)
  ) {
    return null;
  }
  return token;
}

/**
 * Decodes a compact cloud session token and deterministically hydrates full Kanji/Irodori
 * details (including conjugated `ex2k` sentence surfaces) from static bundled dictionaries.
 */
export function decodeQuizSessionCompact(
  token: string,
  datasetId: QuizDatasetId
): QuizSessionHistoryEntry | null {
  if (
    typeof token !== 'string' ||
    token.length < 25 ||
    token.length > MAX_CLOUD_QUIZ_SESSION_TOKEN_LENGTH ||
    !QUIZ_SESSION_TOKEN_PATTERN.test(token)
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  const parts = token.split('|');
  if (parts.length !== 9) return null;

  const [id, completedAt, rawLevel, rawMode, rawCorrect, rawTotal, rawScore, scopeCode, answersPacked] =
    parts;

  const level = rawLevel as LevelFilter;
  const mode = rawMode as QuizTypeChoice;
  const total = Number(rawTotal);
  const correct = Number(rawCorrect);
  const score = Number(rawScore);

  if (
    !VALID_LEVELS.has(level) ||
    !VALID_MODES.has(mode) ||
    !Number.isInteger(total) ||
    total <= 0 ||
    total > 500 ||
    !Number.isInteger(correct) ||
    correct < 0 ||
    correct > total ||
    !Number.isInteger(score) ||
    score < 0 ||
    score > 100 ||
    Number.isNaN(Date.parse(completedAt))
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  const { scopeLabel, chapters } = decodeScopeCode(scopeCode, level, datasetId);
  const byId = getQuizDatasetMap(datasetId);
  const answers: QuizAnswerSnapshot[] = [];

  if (answersPacked.length > 0) {
    for (const rawRow of answersPacked.split(';')) {
      const cols = rawRow.split(':');
      if (cols.length < 3 || cols.length > 4) continue;
      const no = Number(cols[0]);
      const code = Number(cols[1]);
      const itemId = cols[2];
      const rawChosenId = cols[3];

      if (!Number.isInteger(no) || no < 1 || !Number.isInteger(code) || code < 0 || code > 9) {
        continue;
      }

      const type = MODEL_ORDER[code % 5];
      const isCorrect = code >= 5;
      const item = byId.get(itemId);
      if (!item || !type) continue;

      const exMeta = type === 'ex2k' ? analyzeSentenceBlank(item) : null;
      const expectedLabel =
        type === 'ex2k' ? (exMeta?.targetSurface ?? item.k) : dispOf(item, type, null);

      const chosenItem = isCorrect ? item : rawChosenId ? byId.get(rawChosenId) : undefined;
      const chosenLabel = chosenItem
        ? dispOf(chosenItem, type, exMeta)
        : isCorrect
          ? expectedLabel
          : '—';

      answers.push({
        no,
        type,
        correct: isCorrect,
        itemId: item.id,
        itemK: item.k,
        itemR: item.r,
        itemM: item.m,
        expectedLabel,
        ...(chosenItem ? { chosenId: chosenItem.id } : {}),
        chosenLabel,
        ...(!isCorrect && chosenItem?.m ? { chosenM: chosenItem.m } : {}),
        ...(type === 'ex2k' && item.ex ? { ex: item.ex } : {}),
      });
    }
    answers.sort((a, b) => a.no - b.no);
  }

  return {
    id,
    completedAt,
    level,
    scopeLabel,
    ...(chapters ? { chapters } : {}),
    mode,
    correct,
    total,
    score,
    ...(answers.length > 0 ? { answers } : {}),
  };
}

/**
 * Builds a validated Firestore payload for `/users/{uid}/quizHistory/{datasetId}`
 * strictly conforming to `firebase-blueprint.json` and `firestore.rules`.
 */
export function buildQuizHistoryWriteData(
  uid: string,
  datasetId: QuizDatasetId,
  entries: readonly QuizSessionHistoryEntry[]
): QuizHistorySyncPayload {
  if (!isValidFirebaseUid(uid)) {
    throw new Error('UID pengguna tidak valid untuk sinkronisasi riwayat kuis.');
  }
  if (!isValidQuizDatasetId(datasetId)) {
    throw new Error('Identitas dataset kuis tidak valid untuk sinkronisasi Firestore.');
  }

  const deduped: QuizSessionHistoryEntry[] = [];
  const seenIds = new Set<string>();
  const sorted = [...entries].sort((a, b) => Date.parse(b.completedAt) - Date.parse(a.completedAt));

  for (const entry of sorted) {
    if (seenIds.has(entry.id)) continue;
    seenIds.add(entry.id);
    deduped.push(entry);
    if (deduped.length >= MAX_CLOUD_QUIZ_SESSIONS) break;
  }

  const encodeAll = (dropAnswersFromIndex: number): string[] => {
    const tokens: string[] = [];
    const seenTokens = new Set<string>();
    for (let i = 0; i < deduped.length; i++) {
      const token = encodeQuizSessionCompact(deduped[i], datasetId, i < dropAnswersFromIndex);
      if (token && !seenTokens.has(token)) {
        seenTokens.add(token);
        tokens.push(token);
      }
    }
    return tokens;
  };

  let sessions = encodeAll(deduped.length);
  // Defensive boundary guarantee: if total joined size ever exceeds 160 KB, trim oldest session answers first
  for (
    let keepAnswersCount = deduped.length - 1;
    keepAnswersCount >= 0 && sessions.join(',').length > MAX_CLOUD_QUIZ_JOINED_LENGTH;
    keepAnswersCount--
  ) {
    sessions = encodeAll(keepAnswersCount);
  }

  return {
    uid,
    datasetId,
    sessions,
  };
}

export function decodeQuizHistoryFromCloud(
  rawSessions: unknown,
  datasetId: QuizDatasetId
): QuizSessionHistoryEntry[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(rawSessions)) return [];
  const seenIds = new Set<string>();
  const decoded: QuizSessionHistoryEntry[] = [];

  for (const raw of rawSessions) {
    if (typeof raw !== 'string') continue;
    const entry = decodeQuizSessionCompact(raw, datasetId);
    if (!entry || seenIds.has(entry.id)) continue;
    seenIds.add(entry.id);
    decoded.push(entry);
    if (decoded.length >= MAX_CLOUD_QUIZ_SESSIONS) break;
  }

  decoded.sort((a, b) => Date.parse(b.completedAt) - Date.parse(a.completedAt));
  return decoded;
}

/**
 * Merges cloud and local quiz histories by session ID (newest first, capped at 15),
 * preserving whichever copy holds richer answer snapshots.
 */
export function mergeQuizHistories(
  cloudEntries: readonly QuizSessionHistoryEntry[],
  localEntries: readonly QuizSessionHistoryEntry[]
): {
  merged: QuizSessionHistoryEntry[];
  needsCloudWrite: boolean;
  addedFromLocal: number;
} {
  const byId = new Map<string, QuizSessionHistoryEntry>();
  const cloudIds = new Set<string>();

  for (const entry of cloudEntries) {
    byId.set(entry.id, entry);
    cloudIds.add(entry.id);
  }

  let addedFromLocal = 0;
  let enrichedFromLocal = false;

  for (const local of localEntries) {
    const existing = byId.get(local.id);
    if (!existing) {
      byId.set(local.id, local);
      addedFromLocal += 1;
    } else if ((local.answers?.length ?? 0) > (existing.answers?.length ?? 0)) {
      byId.set(local.id, local);
      enrichedFromLocal = true;
    }
  }

  const merged = [...byId.values()]
    .sort((a, b) => Date.parse(b.completedAt) - Date.parse(a.completedAt))
    .slice(0, MAX_QUIZ_HISTORY);

  // Cloud only needs a write if at least one newly added/enriched local session survived the top-15 cap
  const mergedIncludesLocalChange =
    enrichedFromLocal || merged.some(entry => !cloudIds.has(entry.id));

  return {
    merged,
    needsCloudWrite: mergedIncludesLocalChange,
    addedFromLocal,
  };
}

export function areQuizHistoriesEqual(
  a: readonly QuizSessionHistoryEntry[],
  b: readonly QuizSessionHistoryEntry[]
): boolean {
  if (a.length !== b.length) return false;
  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
    if (
      a[i].id !== b[i].id ||
      a[i].completedAt !== b[i].completedAt ||
      a[i].correct !== b[i].correct ||
      a[i].total !== b[i].total ||
      a[i].score !== b[i].score ||
      (a[i].answers?.length ?? 0) !== (b[i].answers?.length ?? 0)
    ) {
      return false;
    }
  }
  return true;
}

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { IRODORI_DATA } from '../data/irodoriData';
import { KANJI } from '../data/kanjiData';
import type { KanjiEntry, QuizAnswerSnapshot, QuizRecord } from '../types/kanji';

import { dispOf } from './quizEngine';
import {
  QUIZ_SESSION_TOKEN_PATTERN,
  buildQuizHistoryWriteData,
  decodeQuizHistoryFromCloud,
  decodeQuizSessionCompact,
  encodeQuizSessionCompact,
  mergeQuizHistories,
} from './quizHistorySync';
import type { StorageLike } from './storage';
import {
  MAX_QUIZ_HISTORY,
  QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY,
  buildQuizAnswerSnapshots,
  clearQuizHistory,
  computeQuizPerformanceStats,
  loadQuizHistory,
  recordQuizSession,

class MemoryStorage implements StorageLike {
  private map = new Map<string, string>();
  getItem(key: string): string | null {
    return this.map.get(key) ?? null;
  }
  setItem(key: string, value: string): void {
    this.map.set(key, value);
  }
  removeItem(key: string): void {
    this.map.delete(key);
  }
}

class TightQuotaStorage extends MemoryStorage {
  constructor(private readonly maxBytes: number) {
    super();
  }
  override setItem(key: string, value: string): void {
    if (value.length > this.maxBytes) {
      throw new Error('QuotaExceededError');
    }
    super.setItem(key, value);
  }
}

test('loadQuizHistory handles empty, malformed JSON, and invalid entries safely', () => {
  const storage = new MemoryStorage();
  assert.deepEqual(loadQuizHistory(storage), []);

  storage.setItem(QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY, '{broken json');
  assert.deepEqual(loadQuizHistory(storage), []);

  storage.setItem(
    QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY,
    JSON.stringify([
      {
        id: 'ok-1',
        completedAt: '2026-11-18T06:00:00.000Z',
        level: 'easy',
        mode: 'random',
        correct: 8,
        total: 10,
        score: 80,
      },
      { id: 'bad-total', completedAt: '2026-11-18T06:00:00.000Z', level: 'easy', mode: 'random', correct: 5, total: 0, score: 0 },
      { id: 'bad-level', completedAt: '2026-11-18T06:00:00.000Z', level: 'expert', mode: 'random', correct: 1, total: 2, score: 50 },
    ])
  );
  const loaded = loadQuizHistory(storage);
  assert.equal(loaded.length, 1);
  assert.equal(loaded[0].id, 'ok-1');
  assert.equal(loaded[0].answers, undefined);
});

test('recordQuizSession computes weighted accuracy, trend delta, caps history, and clears cleanly', () => {
  const storage = new MemoryStorage();
  let history = loadQuizHistory(storage);

  history = recordQuizSession(
    history,
    { level: 'all', mode: 'k2r', correct: 6, total: 10, completedAt: '2026-11-18T05:00:00.000Z', id: 's1' },
    storage
  );
  history = recordQuizSession(
    history,
    { level: 'medium', mode: 'ex2k', correct: 18, total: 20, completedAt: '2026-11-18T06:00:00.000Z', id: 's2' },
    storage
  );

  const stats = computeQuizPerformanceStats(history);
  assert.equal(stats.totalSessions, 2);
  assert.equal(stats.totalCorrect, 24);
  assert.equal(stats.totalAnswered, 30);
  assert.equal(stats.overallAccuracyPct, 80); // 24 / 30 = 80%
  assert.equal(stats.bestScore, 90);
  assert.equal(stats.latestScore, 90);
  assert.equal(stats.trendDelta, 30); // 90 - 60 = +30

  // Verify cap at MAX_QUIZ_HISTORY
  for (let i = 0; i < MAX_QUIZ_HISTORY + 5; i++) {
    history = recordQuizSession(
      history,
      { level: 'easy', mode: 'random', correct: 9, total: 10, id: `extra-${i}` },
      storage
    );
  }
  assert.equal(history.length, MAX_QUIZ_HISTORY);
  assert.equal(loadQuizHistory(storage).length, MAX_QUIZ_HISTORY);

  const cleared = clearQuizHistory(storage);
  assert.deepEqual(cleared, []);
  assert.deepEqual(loadQuizHistory(storage), []);
});

test('buildQuizAnswerSnapshots persists ordered answers and handles tight localStorage quota gracefully', () => {
  const itemA: KanjiEntry = {
    id: 'n1',
    k: '計画',
    r: 'けいかく',
    m: 'rencana',
    ex: '夏休みの計画を立てました。',
    exi: 'Menyusun rencana liburan musim panas.',
    lv: 'N4',
    dif: 'easy',
  };
  const itemB: KanjiEntry = {
    id: 'n2',
    k: '経験',
    r: 'けいけん',
    m: 'pengalaman',
    ex: 'いい経験になりました。',
    exi: 'Menjadi pengalaman yang baik.',
    lv: 'N4',
    dif: 'easy',
  };

  const rightList: QuizRecord[] = [
    { no: 2, item: itemB, type: 'k2r', exMeta: null, chosen: itemB, chosenLabel: 'けいけん' },
  ];
  const wrongList: QuizRecord[] = [
    { no: 1, item: itemA, type: 'k2m', exMeta: null, chosen: itemB, chosenLabel: 'pengalaman' },
  ];

  const snapshots = buildQuizAnswerSnapshots(rightList, wrongList);
  assert.equal(snapshots.length, 2);
  assert.equal(snapshots[0].no, 1);
  assert.equal(snapshots[0].correct, false);
  assert.equal(snapshots[0].expectedLabel, 'rencana');
  assert.equal(snapshots[0].chosenLabel, 'pengalaman');
  assert.equal(snapshots[1].no, 2);
  assert.equal(snapshots[1].correct, true);
  assert.equal(snapshots[1].expectedLabel, 'けいけん');

  const storage = new MemoryStorage();
  const saved = recordQuizSession(
    [],
    {
      level: 'easy',
      mode: 'random',
      correct: 1,
      total: 2,
      id: 'sess-with-answers',
      completedAt: '2026-11-18T12:00:00.000Z',
      answers: snapshots,
    },
    storage
  );
  assert.equal(saved[0].answers?.length, 2);

  const reloaded = loadQuizHistory(storage);
  assert.equal(reloaded.length, 1);
  assert.equal(reloaded[0].answers?.length, 2);
  assert.equal(reloaded[0].answers?.[0].itemK, '計画');
  assert.equal(reloaded[0].answers?.[1].itemK, '経験');

  // Tight quota storage trims oldest session answers first so newest session still persists cleanly
  const tight = new TightQuotaStorage(780);
  let hist = recordQuizSession(
    [],
    {
      level: 'easy',
      mode: 'random',
      correct: 1,
      total: 2,
      id: 'older-sess',
      completedAt: '2026-11-18T11:00:00.000Z',
      answers: snapshots,
    },
    tight
  );
  hist = recordQuizSession(
    hist,
    {
      level: 'easy',
      mode: 'random',
      correct: 1,
      total: 2,
      id: 'newer-sess',
      completedAt: '2026-11-18T12:00:00.000Z',
      answers: snapshots,
    },
    tight
  );
  const reloadedTight = loadQuizHistory(tight);
  assert.equal(reloadedTight.length, 2);
  assert.equal(reloadedTight[0].id, 'newer-sess');
  assert.equal(reloadedTight[0].answers?.length, 2);
  assert.equal(reloadedTight[1].id, 'older-sess');
  assert.equal(reloadedTight[1].answers, undefined);
});

test('compact quiz history cloud codec round-trips all 5 models (including conjugated ex2k) with <1.6KB for 100 questions', () => {
  // Build a real 100-question N4 quiz session spanning all 5 models
  const models = ['k2r', 'k2m', 'm2k', 'r2k', 'ex2k'] as const;
  const rightList: QuizRecord[] = [];
  const wrongList: QuizRecord[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) {
    const item = KANJI[i];
    const distractor = KANJI[i + 1];
    const type = models[i % models.length];
    const exMeta = type === 'ex2k' ? analyzeSentenceBlank(item) : null;
    const isCorrect = i % 5 !== 0; // 80 right, 20 wrong
    const chosen = isCorrect ? item : distractor;
    const chosenLabel = dispOf(chosen, type, exMeta);
    const rec: QuizRecord = {
      no: i + 1,
      item,
      type,
      exMeta,
      chosen,
      chosenLabel,
    };
    if (isCorrect) rightList.push(rec);
    else wrongList.push(rec);
  }

  const snapshots = buildQuizAnswerSnapshots(rightList, wrongList);
  assert.equal(snapshots.length, 100);
  assert.equal(snapshots[0].chosenId, KANJI[1].id);

  const session = recordQuizSession(
    [],
    {
      id: 'q-1763500000000-n4abc',
      completedAt: '2026-11-18T12:34:56.000Z',
      level: 'all',
      scopeLabel: 'Level Semua',
      mode: 'random5',
      correct: 80,
      total: 100,
      answers: snapshots,
    },
    null
  )[0];

  const encoded = encodeQuizSessionCompact(session, 'n4');
  assert.ok(encoded, '100-question session must encode cleanly');
  assert.ok(QUIZ_SESSION_TOKEN_PATTERN.test(encoded), 'encoded token must match Firestore Rules regex');
  // Ultra-compact efficiency guarantee: 100 full Q&A snapshots in < 1.6 KB (vs ~22 KB raw JSON)
  assert.ok(
    encoded.length < 1600,
    `Expected compact 100-question token < 1600 bytes, got ${encoded.length}`
  );

  const decoded = decodeQuizSessionCompact(encoded, 'n4');
  assert.ok(decoded);
  assert.equal(decoded.id, session.id);
  assert.equal(decoded.completedAt, session.completedAt);
  assert.equal(decoded.correct, 80);
  assert.equal(decoded.total, 100);
  assert.equal(decoded.score, 80);
  assert.equal(decoded.scopeLabel, 'Level Semua');
  assert.equal(decoded.answers?.length, 100);

  // Verify exact hydration fidelity across every single question (including conjugated ex2k)
  for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) {
    const orig = snapshots[i];
    const dec: QuizAnswerSnapshot = decoded.answers![i];
    assert.equal(dec.no, orig.no);
    assert.equal(dec.type, orig.type);
    assert.equal(dec.correct, orig.correct);
    assert.equal(dec.itemId, orig.itemId);
    assert.equal(dec.itemK, orig.itemK);
    assert.equal(dec.itemR, orig.itemR);
    assert.equal(dec.itemM, orig.itemM);
    assert.equal(dec.expectedLabel, orig.expectedLabel);
    assert.equal(dec.chosenLabel, orig.chosenLabel);
    assert.equal(dec.chosenM, orig.chosenM);
    assert.equal(dec.ex, orig.ex);
  }
});

test('compact codec supports Irodori chapter ranges, legacy snapshots without chosenId, and cloud/local merge', () => {
  const irItemA = IRODORI_DATA[0];
  const irItemB = IRODORI_DATA[1];
  const irodoriSession = recordQuizSession(
    [],
    {
      id: 'q-ir-1',
      completedAt: '2026-11-18T14:00:00.000Z',
      level: 'all',
      scopeLabel: 'Bab 1–3, 5',
      chapters: [1, 2, 3, 5],
      mode: 'k2m',
      correct: 1,
      total: 2,
      answers: [
        // Legacy snapshot without chosenId (should still reverse-resolve chosenId cleanly)
        {
          no: 1,
          type: 'k2m',
          correct: false,
          itemId: irItemA.id,
          itemK: irItemA.k,
          itemR: irItemA.r,
          itemM: irItemA.m,
          expectedLabel: irItemA.m,
          chosenLabel: irItemB.m,
          chosenM: irItemB.m,
        },
        {
          no: 2,
          type: 'k2m',
          correct: true,
          itemId: irItemB.id,
          itemK: irItemB.k,
          itemR: irItemB.r,
          itemM: irItemB.m,
          expectedLabel: irItemB.m,
          chosenLabel: irItemB.m,
        },
      ],
    },
    null
  )[0];

  const payload = buildQuizHistoryWriteData('userA_123', 'dasar1', [irodoriSession]);
  assert.equal(payload.uid, 'userA_123');
  assert.equal(payload.datasetId, 'dasar1');
  assert.equal(payload.sessions.length, 1);

  const decodedList = decodeQuizHistoryFromCloud(payload.sessions, 'dasar1');
  assert.equal(decodedList.length, 1);
  assert.equal(decodedList[0].scopeLabel, 'Bab 1–3, 5');
  assert.deepEqual(decodedList[0].chapters, [1, 2, 3, 5]);
  assert.equal(decodedList[0].answers?.[0].correct, false);
  assert.equal(decodedList[0].answers?.[0].chosenLabel, irItemB.m);
  assert.equal(decodedList[0].answers?.[1].correct, true);

  // Merge cloud + local: dedup by id, keep richer answers, newest-first, detect needsCloudWrite
  const cloudOld = { ...irodoriSession, id: 's-old', completedAt: '2026-11-18T10:00:00.000Z' };
  const cloudWithoutAnswers = {
    ...irodoriSession,
    id: 's-shared',
    completedAt: '2026-11-18T12:00:00.000Z',
    answers: undefined,
  };
  const localWithAnswers = {
    ...irodoriSession,
    id: 's-shared',
    completedAt: '2026-11-18T12:00:00.000Z',
  };
  const localNew = { ...irodoriSession, id: 's-new', completedAt: '2026-11-18T15:00:00.000Z' };

  const merged = mergeQuizHistories([cloudWithoutAnswers, cloudOld], [localNew, localWithAnswers]);
  assert.equal(merged.needsCloudWrite, true);
  assert.equal(merged.addedFromLocal, 1);
  assert.deepEqual(
    merged.merged.map(s => s.id),
    ['s-new', 's-shared', 's-old']
  );
  assert.equal(merged.merged[1].answers?.length, 2);

  // Already in sync -> needsCloudWrite must be false (0 wasted Firestore writes)
  const alreadySynced = mergeQuizHistories(merged.merged, merged.merged);
  assert.equal(alreadySynced.needsCloudWrite, false);
  assert.equal(alreadySynced.addedFromLocal, 0);
});



================================================
FILE: src/utils/quizStats.ts
