import type {
  IrodoriChapterNumber,
  LevelFilter,
  QuizAnswerSnapshot,
  QuizDatasetId,
  QuizModelKey,
  QuizRecord,
  QuizSessionHistoryEntry,
  QuizTypeChoice,
} from '../types/kanji';
import { calculateQuizScore } from './quizEngine';
import type { StorageLike } from './storage';

export const QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY = 'n4kanji_quiz_history_v1';
export const IRODORI_QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY = 'irodori_quiz_history_v1';
export const IRODORI_PEMULA_QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY = 'irodori_pemula_quiz_history_v1';
export const IRODORI_DASAR2_QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY = 'irodori_dasar2_quiz_history_v1';
export const MAX_QUIZ_HISTORY = 15;
export const MAX_STORED_ANSWERS_PER_SESSION = 500;

export const QUIZ_HISTORY_KEYS_BY_DATASET: Record<QuizDatasetId, string> = {
  n4: QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY,
  pemula: IRODORI_PEMULA_QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY,
  dasar1: IRODORI_QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY,
  dasar2: IRODORI_DASAR2_QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY,
};

const VALID_LEVELS = new Set<LevelFilter>(['all', 'easy', 'medium', 'hard']);
const VALID_MODEL_KEYS = new Set<QuizModelKey>(['k2r', 'k2m', 'm2k', 'r2k', 'ex2k']);
const VALID_MODES = new Set<QuizTypeChoice>([
  'random',
  'random5',
  'k2r',
  'k2m',
  'm2k',
  'r2k',
  'ex2k',
]);

function getDefaultStorage(): StorageLike | null {
  try {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.localStorage) {
      return window.localStorage;
    }
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
  return null;
}

function sanitizeChapterNumbers(raw: unknown): IrodoriChapterNumber[] | undefined {
  if (!Array.isArray(raw)) return undefined;
  const set = new Set<IrodoriChapterNumber>();
  for (const ch of raw) {
    if (typeof ch === 'number' && Number.isInteger(ch) && ch >= 1 && ch <= 18) {
      set.add(ch as IrodoriChapterNumber);
    }
  }
  if (set.size === 0) return undefined;
  return [...set].sort((a, b) => a - b);
}

function isValidAnswerSnapshot(raw: unknown): raw is QuizAnswerSnapshot {
  if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'object' || Array.isArray(raw)) return false;
  const obj = raw as Record<string, unknown>;
  return (
    typeof obj.no === 'number' &&
    Number.isInteger(obj.no) &&
    obj.no >= 1 &&
    typeof obj.type === 'string' &&
    VALID_MODEL_KEYS.has(obj.type as QuizModelKey) &&
    typeof obj.correct === 'boolean' &&
    typeof obj.itemId === 'string' &&
    obj.itemId.length > 0 &&
    typeof obj.itemK === 'string' &&
    typeof obj.itemR === 'string' &&
    typeof obj.itemM === 'string' &&
    typeof obj.expectedLabel === 'string' &&
    typeof obj.chosenLabel === 'string'
  );
}

function sanitizeAnswerSnapshots(raw: unknown): QuizAnswerSnapshot[] | undefined {
  if (!Array.isArray(raw)) return undefined;
  const valid = raw.filter(isValidAnswerSnapshot).map(ans => ({
    no: ans.no,
    type: ans.type,
    correct: ans.correct,
    itemId: ans.itemId,
    itemK: ans.itemK,
    itemR: ans.itemR,
    itemM: ans.itemM,
    expectedLabel: ans.expectedLabel,
    ...(typeof ans.chosenId === 'string' && ans.chosenId.length > 0
      ? { chosenId: ans.chosenId }
      : {}),
    chosenLabel: ans.chosenLabel,
    ...(typeof ans.chosenM === 'string' ? { chosenM: ans.chosenM } : {}),
    ...(typeof ans.ex === 'string' ? { ex: ans.ex } : {}),
  }));
  if (valid.length <= MAX_STORED_ANSWERS_PER_SESSION) {
    return valid.sort((a, b) => a.no - b.no);
  }
  // Keep all wrong answers first so learners never lose mistakes even on huge sessions
  const wrong = valid.filter(a => !a.correct).slice(0, MAX_STORED_ANSWERS_PER_SESSION);
  const remainingSlots = Math.max(0, MAX_STORED_ANSWERS_PER_SESSION - wrong.length);
  const right = valid.filter(a => a.correct).slice(0, remainingSlots);
  return [...wrong, ...right].sort((a, b) => a.no - b.no);
}

/**
 * Converts completed quiz records (`rightList` + `wrongList`) into compact,
 * serializable answer snapshots ordered by question number.
 */
export function buildQuizAnswerSnapshots(
  rightList: readonly QuizRecord[],
  wrongList: readonly QuizRecord[]
): QuizAnswerSnapshot[] {
  const toSnapshot = (rec: QuizRecord, correct: boolean): QuizAnswerSnapshot => {
    const expectedLabel =
      rec.type === 'k2r'
        ? rec.item.r
        : rec.type === 'k2m'
          ? rec.item.m
          : rec.type === 'ex2k'
            ? (rec.exMeta?.targetSurface ?? rec.item.k)
            : rec.item.k;
    return {
      no: rec.no,
      type: rec.type,
      correct,
      itemId: rec.item.id,
      itemK: rec.item.k,
      itemR: rec.item.r,
      itemM: rec.item.m,
      expectedLabel,
      chosenId: rec.chosen.id,
      chosenLabel: rec.chosenLabel,
      ...(rec.chosen.m && !correct ? { chosenM: rec.chosen.m } : {}),
      ...(rec.type === 'ex2k' && rec.item.ex ? { ex: rec.item.ex } : {}),
    };
  };

  const combined = [
    ...rightList.map(rec => toSnapshot(rec, true)),
    ...wrongList.map(rec => toSnapshot(rec, false)),
  ];
  return sanitizeAnswerSnapshots(combined) ?? [];
}

function isValidHistoryEntry(raw: unknown): raw is QuizSessionHistoryEntry {
  if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'object' || Array.isArray(raw)) return false;
  const obj = raw as Record<string, unknown>;
  return (
    typeof obj.id === 'string' &&
    obj.id.length > 0 &&
    typeof obj.completedAt === 'string' &&
    !Number.isNaN(Date.parse(obj.completedAt)) &&
    typeof obj.level === 'string' &&
    VALID_LEVELS.has(obj.level as LevelFilter) &&
    typeof obj.mode === 'string' &&
    VALID_MODES.has(obj.mode as QuizTypeChoice) &&
    typeof obj.correct === 'number' &&
    Number.isInteger(obj.correct) &&
    obj.correct >= 0 &&
    typeof obj.total === 'number' &&
    Number.isInteger(obj.total) &&
    obj.total > 0 &&
    obj.correct <= obj.total &&
    typeof obj.score === 'number' &&
    Number.isFinite(obj.score) &&
    obj.score >= 0 &&
    obj.score <= 100
  );
}

export function loadQuizHistory(
  storage: StorageLike | null = getDefaultStorage(),
  storageKey: string = QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY
): QuizSessionHistoryEntry[] {
  if (!storage) return [];
  try {
    const raw = storage.getItem(storageKey);
    if (!raw) return [];
    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) return [];
    return parsed
      .filter(isValidHistoryEntry)
      .slice(0, MAX_QUIZ_HISTORY)
      .map(entry => {
        const sanitizedAnswers = sanitizeAnswerSnapshots(entry.answers);
        const sanitizedChapters = sanitizeChapterNumbers(entry.chapters);
        return {
          ...entry,
          ...(sanitizedChapters ? { chapters: sanitizedChapters } : {}),
          ...(sanitizedAnswers ? { answers: sanitizedAnswers } : {}),
        };
      });
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

function persistHistoryWithQuotaFallback(
  storage: StorageLike,
  storageKey: string,
  entries: QuizSessionHistoryEntry[]
): void {
  try {
    storage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(entries));
    return;
  } catch {
    // Quota tight: prune detailed answers from oldest sessions first while preserving scores
  }

  const trimmed = entries.map(e => ({ ...e }));
  for (let i = trimmed.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    if (trimmed[i].answers) {
      delete trimmed[i].answers;
      try {
        storage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(trimmed));
        return;
      } catch {
        // Keep trimming older snapshots until it fits
      }
    }
  }
}

export function saveQuizHistory(
  entries: QuizSessionHistoryEntry[],
  storage: StorageLike | null = getDefaultStorage(),
  storageKey: string = QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY
): QuizSessionHistoryEntry[] {
  const capped = entries.slice(0, MAX_QUIZ_HISTORY);
  if (storage) {
    if (capped.length === 0) {
      try {
        storage.removeItem(storageKey);
      } catch {
        // Ignore storage lock errors
      }
    } else {
      persistHistoryWithQuotaFallback(storage, storageKey, capped);
    }
  }
  return capped;
}

export function recordQuizSession(
  currentHistory: QuizSessionHistoryEntry[],
  input: {
    level: LevelFilter;
    scopeLabel?: string;
    chapters?: IrodoriChapterNumber[];
    mode: QuizTypeChoice;
    correct: number;
    total: number;
    completedAt?: string;
    id?: string;
    answers?: QuizAnswerSnapshot[];
  },
  storage: StorageLike | null = getDefaultStorage(),
  storageKey: string = QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY
): QuizSessionHistoryEntry[] {
  if (!Number.isInteger(input.total) || input.total <= 0) {
    return currentHistory;
  }
  const safeCorrect = Math.max(0, Math.min(input.total, Math.floor(input.correct)));
  const sanitizedAnswers = input.answers ? sanitizeAnswerSnapshots(input.answers) : undefined;
  const sanitizedChapters = sanitizeChapterNumbers(input.chapters);
  const entry: QuizSessionHistoryEntry = {
    id: input.id ?? `q-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
    completedAt: input.completedAt ?? new Date().toISOString(),
    level: VALID_LEVELS.has(input.level) ? input.level : 'all',
    ...(input.scopeLabel ? { scopeLabel: input.scopeLabel } : {}),
    ...(sanitizedChapters ? { chapters: sanitizedChapters } : {}),
    mode: VALID_MODES.has(input.mode) ? input.mode : 'random',
    correct: safeCorrect,
    total: input.total,
    score: calculateQuizScore(safeCorrect, input.total),
    ...(sanitizedAnswers && sanitizedAnswers.length > 0 ? { answers: sanitizedAnswers } : {}),
  };

  const next = [entry, ...currentHistory].slice(0, MAX_QUIZ_HISTORY);
  if (storage) {
    persistHistoryWithQuotaFallback(storage, storageKey, next);
  }
  return next;
}

export function clearQuizHistory(
  storage: StorageLike | null = getDefaultStorage(),
  storageKey: string = QUIZ_HISTORY_KEY
): QuizSessionHistoryEntry[] {
  if (storage) {
    try {
      storage.removeItem(storageKey);
    } catch {
      // Ignore storage lock errors
    }
  }
  return [];
}

export interface QuizPerformanceStats {
  totalSessions: number;
  totalCorrect: number;
  totalAnswered: number;
  overallAccuracyPct: number;
  bestScore: number;
  latestScore: number | null;
  trendDelta: number | null;
}

export function computeQuizPerformanceStats(
  history: QuizSessionHistoryEntry[]
): QuizPerformanceStats {
  if (history.length === 0) {
    return {
      totalSessions: 0,
      totalCorrect: 0,
      totalAnswered: 0,
      overallAccuracyPct: 0,
      bestScore: 0,
      latestScore: null,
      trendDelta: null,
    };
  }

  let totalCorrect = 0;
  let totalAnswered = 0;
  let bestScore = 0;

  for (const item of history) {
    totalCorrect += item.correct;
    totalAnswered += item.total;
    if (item.score > bestScore) {
      bestScore = item.score;
    }
  }

  const overallAccuracyPct =
    totalAnswered > 0 ? Math.round((totalCorrect / totalAnswered) * 100) : 0;
  const latestScore = history[0].score;
  const trendDelta = history.length >= 2 ? history[0].score - history[1].score : null;

  return {
    totalSessions: history.length,
    totalCorrect,
    totalAnswered,
    overallAccuracyPct,
    bestScore,
    latestScore,
    trendDelta,
  };
}

FILE: src/utils/storage.test.ts

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {
  BACKUP_APP_ID,
  BACKUP_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  HARD_KEY,
  LS_KEY,
  commitProgressTransaction,
  safeLoadSet,
  validateAndParseBackup,
  type StorageLike,
} from './storage';

class MemoryStorage implements StorageLike {
  private data = new Map<string, string>();
  public failOnSet = false;
  public corruptVerify = false;

  getItem(key: string): string | null {
    return this.data.get(key) ?? null;
  }

  setItem(key: string, value: string): void {
    if (this.failOnSet) {
      throw new Error('QuotaExceededError');
    }
    this.data.set(key, this.corruptVerify ? '[]' : value);
  }

  removeItem(key: string): void {
    this.data.delete(key);
  }
}

test('validateAndParseBackup rejects arbitrary JSON objects and malformed backups', () => {
  assert.throws(() => validateAndParseBackup('not json'), /JSON/);
  assert.throws(() => validateAndParseBackup('[]'), /objek backup/);
  assert.throws(() => validateAndParseBackup('{}'), /valid/);
  assert.throws(() => validateAndParseBackup('{"foo": 123, "bar": "baz"}'), /valid/);
  assert.throws(() => validateAndParseBackup('{"mastered": "n3"}'), /valid/);
  assert.throws(() => validateAndParseBackup('{"mastered": ["n3"], "extra": true}'), /valid/);
  assert.throws(() => validateAndParseBackup('{"mastered": ["n3"], "hard": [], "extra": true}'), /tidak dikenali/);
  assert.throws(() => validateAndParseBackup('{"mastered": ["fake1"], "hard": []}'), /tidak mengandung ID/);
  assert.throws(
    () => validateAndParseBackup(JSON.stringify({ app: 'other-app', version: 1, mastered: [], hard: [] })),
    /Identitas aplikasi/
  );
  assert.throws(
    () => validateAndParseBackup(JSON.stringify({ app: BACKUP_APP_ID, version: 99, mastered: [], hard: [] })),
    /Versi skema/
  );
  assert.throws(
    () => validateAndParseBackup(JSON.stringify({ app: BACKUP_APP_ID, version: 1 })),
    /harus memiliki array/
  );
});

test('validateAndParseBackup accepts valid v1 backup, flags empty backups and unrecognized IDs', () => {
  const emptyPayload = JSON.stringify({
    app: BACKUP_APP_ID,
    version: BACKUP_SCHEMA_VERSION,
    exportedAt: '2026-11-18T00:00:00.000Z',
    mastered: [],
    hard: [],
  });
  const emptyParsed = validateAndParseBackup(emptyPayload);
  assert.equal(emptyParsed.isEmptyBackup, true);
  assert.deepEqual(emptyParsed.rejectedIds, []);

  const mixedPayload = JSON.stringify({
    app: BACKUP_APP_ID,
    version: BACKUP_SCHEMA_VERSION,
    exportedAt: '2026-11-18T00:00:00.000Z',
    mastered: ['n3', 'unknown_999'],
    hard: ['n4', 'bad_id'],
  });
  const mixedParsed = validateAndParseBackup(mixedPayload);
  assert.equal(mixedParsed.isEmptyBackup, false);
  assert.deepEqual(mixedParsed.impMastered, ['n3']);
  assert.deepEqual(mixedParsed.impHard, ['n4']);
  assert.deepEqual(mixedParsed.rejectedIds, ['unknown_999', 'bad_id']);
});

test('commitProgressTransaction checks write success and rolls back cleanly on storage failure', () => {
  const storage = new MemoryStorage();
  const initialM = new Set(['n3', 'n4']);
  const initialH = new Set(['n6']);
  const firstRes = commitProgressTransaction(initialM, initialH, storage);
  assert.equal(firstRes.ok, true);
  assert.deepEqual([...safeLoadSet(LS_KEY, undefined, storage)], ['n3', 'n4']);
  assert.deepEqual([...safeLoadSet(HARD_KEY, undefined, storage)], ['n6']);

  // Simulate QuotaExceededError
  storage.failOnSet = true;
  const failRes = commitProgressTransaction(new Set(['n11']), new Set(['n13']), storage);
  assert.equal(failRes.ok, false);
  assert.ok(failRes.error);

  // Previous data in storage must remain completely intact
  storage.failOnSet = false;
  assert.deepEqual([...safeLoadSet(LS_KEY, undefined, storage)], ['n3', 'n4']);
  assert.deepEqual([...safeLoadSet(HARD_KEY, undefined, storage)], ['n6']);

  // Simulate silent write corruption
  storage.corruptVerify = true;
  const corruptRes = commitProgressTransaction(new Set(['n11']), new Set(), storage);
  assert.equal(corruptRes.ok, false);
});

FILE: src/utils/storage.ts
================================================
import { VALID_KANJI_IDS } from '../data/kanjiData';
import type { BackupParseResult, StorageMutationResult } from '../types/kanji';

export const LS_KEY = 'n4kanji_mastered_v1';
export const HARD_KEY = 'n4kanji_hard_v1';
export const IRODORI_LS_KEY = 'irodori_mastered_v1';
export const IRODORI_HARD_KEY = 'irodori_hard_v1';
export const IRODORI_PEMULA_LS_KEY = 'irodori_pemula_mastered_v1';
export const IRODORI_PEMULA_HARD_KEY = 'irodori_pemula_hard_v1';
export const IRODORI_DASAR2_LS_KEY = 'irodori_dasar2_mastered_v1';
export const IRODORI_DASAR2_HARD_KEY = 'irodori_dasar2_hard_v1';
export const OWNER_UID_KEY = 'n4kanji_owner_uid_v1';
export const BACKUP_APP_ID = 'n4-kanji-kotoba';
export const BACKUP_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1;

export interface StorageLike {
  getItem(key: string): string | null;
  setItem(key: string, value: string): void;
  removeItem(key: string): void;
}

function getDefaultStorage(): StorageLike | null {
  try {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.localStorage) {
      return window.localStorage;
    }
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
  return null;
}

export function safeLoadSet(
  key: string,
  validIds: Set<string> = VALID_KANJI_IDS,
  storage: StorageLike | null = getDefaultStorage()
): Set<string> {
  if (!storage) return new Set();
  try {
    const raw = storage.getItem(key);
    if (!raw) return new Set();
    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) return new Set();
    return new Set(parsed.filter((id): id is string => typeof id === 'string' && validIds.has(id)));
  } catch {
    return new Set();
  }
}

export function getStoredOwnerUid(
  storage: StorageLike | null = getDefaultStorage()
): string | null {
  if (!storage) return null;
  try {
    const val = storage.getItem(OWNER_UID_KEY);
    return val && val.trim() ? val.trim() : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function setStoredOwnerUid(
  uid: string | null,
  storage: StorageLike | null = getDefaultStorage()
): void {
  if (!storage) return;
  try {
    if (uid) {
      storage.setItem(OWNER_UID_KEY, uid);
    } else {
      storage.removeItem(OWNER_UID_KEY);
    }
  } catch {
    // Ignore storage lock errors for non-critical metadata
  }
}

export function clearStoredProgress(
  storage: StorageLike | null = getDefaultStorage()
): void {
  if (!storage) return;
  try {
    storage.removeItem(LS_KEY);
    storage.removeItem(HARD_KEY);
    storage.removeItem(IRODORI_PEMULA_LS_KEY);
    storage.removeItem(IRODORI_PEMULA_HARD_KEY);
    storage.removeItem(IRODORI_LS_KEY);
    storage.removeItem(IRODORI_HARD_KEY);
    storage.removeItem(IRODORI_DASAR2_LS_KEY);
    storage.removeItem(IRODORI_DASAR2_HARD_KEY);
    storage.removeItem('n4kanji_quiz_history_v1');
    storage.removeItem('irodori_pemula_quiz_history_v1');
    storage.removeItem('irodori_quiz_history_v1');
    storage.removeItem('irodori_dasar2_quiz_history_v1');
    storage.removeItem(OWNER_UID_KEY);
  } catch {
    // Ignore storage lock errors
  }
}

/**
 * Transactionally persists both mastered and hard sets to localStorage for any scoped key pair.
 */
export function commitScopedProgressTransaction(
  masteredKey: string,
  hardKey: string,
  nextMastered: Set<string>,
  nextHard: Set<string>,
  storage: StorageLike | null = getDefaultStorage()
): StorageMutationResult {
  if (!storage) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: 'Penyimpanan browser (localStorage) tidak tersedia atau diblokir.',
    };
  }

  let prevRawM: string | null = null;
  let prevRawH: string | null = null;

  try {
    prevRawM = storage.getItem(masteredKey);
    prevRawH = storage.getItem(hardKey);
  } catch {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: 'Penyimpanan browser (localStorage) tidak dapat diakses.',
    };
  }

  try {
    const mJson = JSON.stringify([...nextMastered]);
    const hJson = JSON.stringify([...nextHard]);
    storage.setItem(masteredKey, mJson);
    storage.setItem(hardKey, hJson);

    const verifyM = storage.getItem(masteredKey);
    const verifyH = storage.getItem(hardKey);
    if (verifyM !== mJson || verifyH !== hJson) {
      throw new Error('Verification mismatch after write');
    }

    return { ok: true };
  } catch {
    try {
      if (prevRawM === null) storage.removeItem(masteredKey);
      else storage.setItem(masteredKey, prevRawM);
      if (prevRawH === null) storage.removeItem(hardKey);
      else storage.setItem(hardKey, prevRawH);
    } catch {
      // Ignore rollback errors if storage is completely locked
    }
    return {
      ok: false,
      error:
        'Gagal menyimpan ke penyimpanan browser (memori penuh atau diblokir). Progres sebelumnya tetap aman.',
    };
  }
}

/**
 * Transactionally persists both mastered and hard sets to localStorage.
 * If either write fails, rolls back to the previous stored values and returns ok: false
 * so the UI never reports a false success.
 */
export function commitProgressTransaction(
  nextMastered: Set<string>,
  nextHard: Set<string>,
  storage: StorageLike | null = getDefaultStorage()
): StorageMutationResult {
  return commitScopedProgressTransaction(LS_KEY, HARD_KEY, nextMastered, nextHard, storage);
}

function parseLegacyListField(rawField: unknown, fieldLabel: string): unknown[] {
  if (Array.isArray(rawField)) return rawField;
  if (typeof rawField === 'string') {
    let parsed: unknown;
    try {
      parsed = JSON.parse(rawField);
    } catch {
      throw new Error(`Format data backup lama pada "${fieldLabel}" rusak.`);
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) {
      throw new Error(`Data backup lama pada "${fieldLabel}" harus berupa array.`);
    }
    return parsed;
  }
  throw new Error(`Struktur data backup lama pada "${fieldLabel}" tidak valid.`);
}

const ALLOWED_V1_KEYS = new Set(['app', 'version', 'exportedAt', 'mastered', 'hard']);
const ALLOWED_LEGACY_PLAIN_KEYS = new Set(['mastered', 'hard', 'exportedAt']);

export function validateAndParseBackup(
  rawText: string,
  validIds: Set<string> = VALID_KANJI_IDS
): BackupParseResult {
  let data: unknown;
  try {
    data = JSON.parse(rawText);
  } catch {
    throw new Error('File bukan format JSON yang valid atau rusak.');
  }

  if (!data || typeof data !== 'object' || Array.isArray(data)) {
    throw new Error('Struktur JSON tidak valid (harus berupa objek backup).');
  }

  const obj = data as Record<string, unknown>;
  const keys = Object.keys(obj);

  const hasApp = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, 'app');
  const hasVersion = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, 'version');
  const hasModernMastered = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, 'mastered');
  const hasModernHard = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, 'hard');
  const hasLegacyMastered = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, LS_KEY);
  const hasLegacyHard = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, HARD_KEY);

  let rawMastered: unknown[] = [];
  let rawHard: unknown[] = [];
  let formatType: BackupParseResult['formatType'] = 'v1';

  if (hasApp || hasVersion) {
    if (obj.app !== BACKUP_APP_ID) {
      throw new Error(`Identitas aplikasi tidak cocok (app: "${String(obj.app ?? '')}").`);
    }
    if (obj.version !== BACKUP_SCHEMA_VERSION) {
      throw new Error(`Versi skema backup tidak didukung (version: ${String(obj.version ?? '')}).`);
    }
    if (!hasModernMastered || !hasModernHard) {
      throw new Error('File backup v1 harus memiliki array "mastered" dan "hard".');
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(obj.mastered)) {
      throw new Error('Struktur data "mastered" rusak (harus berupa array).');
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(obj.hard)) {
      throw new Error('Struktur data "hard" rusak (harus berupa array).');
    }
    if (obj.exportedAt !== undefined && typeof obj.exportedAt !== 'string') {
      throw new Error('Format field "exportedAt" tidak valid.');
    }
    for (const k of keys) {
      if (!ALLOWED_V1_KEYS.has(k)) {
        throw new Error(`Field tidak dikenali dalam file backup: "${k}".`);
      }
    }
    rawMastered = obj.mastered;
    rawHard = obj.hard;
    formatType = 'v1';
  } else if (hasLegacyMastered || hasLegacyHard) {
    rawMastered = hasLegacyMastered ? parseLegacyListField(obj[LS_KEY], LS_KEY) : [];
    rawHard = hasLegacyHard ? parseLegacyListField(obj[HARD_KEY], HARD_KEY) : [];
    formatType = 'legacy-storage';
  } else if (hasModernMastered && hasModernHard) {
    // Strict legacy-plain format: must have BOTH mastered & hard as arrays and no unrelated keys
    for (const k of keys) {
      if (!ALLOWED_LEGACY_PLAIN_KEYS.has(k)) {
        throw new Error('File JSON tidak dikenali sebagai backup N4 Kanji.');
      }
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(obj.mastered) || !Array.isArray(obj.hard)) {
      throw new Error('Struktur data "mastered" atau "hard" harus berupa array.');
    }
    rawMastered = obj.mastered;
    rawHard = obj.hard;
    formatType = 'legacy-plain';
  } else {
    throw new Error('File tidak memiliki struktur data backup kanji N4 yang valid.');
  }

  const impMasteredSet = new Set<string>();
  const impHardSet = new Set<string>();
  const rejectedSet = new Set<string>();

  const inspectList = (arr: unknown[], targetSet: Set<string>) => {
    for (const entry of arr) {
      if (typeof entry === 'string' && validIds.has(entry)) {
        targetSet.add(entry);
      } else {
        rejectedSet.add(typeof entry === 'string' ? entry : JSON.stringify(entry));
      }
    }
  };

  inspectList(rawMastered, impMasteredSet);
  inspectList(rawHard, impHardSet);

  const impMastered = [...impMasteredSet];
  const impHard = [...impHardSet];
  const rejectedIds = [...rejectedSet];

  // If a non-v1 legacy-plain file had entries but zero valid Kanji IDs, reject it as arbitrary JSON
  if (formatType === 'legacy-plain' && rejectedIds.length > 0 && impMastered.length === 0 && impHard.length === 0) {
    throw new Error('File tidak mengandung ID kanji N4 yang valid.');
  }

  return {
    formatType,
    exportedAt: typeof obj.exportedAt === 'string' ? obj.exportedAt : '',
    impMastered,
    impHard,
    rejectedIds,
    isEmptyBackup: impMastered.length === 0 && impHard.length === 0,
  };
}

export function buildBackupPayload(mastered: Set<string>, hardSet: Set<string>) {
  return {
    app: BACKUP_APP_ID,
    version: BACKUP_SCHEMA_VERSION,
    exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    mastered: [...mastered],
    hard: [...hardSet],
  };
}

FILE: src/utils/tts.ts
================================================
let cachedVoices: SpeechSynthesisVoice[] = [];
let fallbackAudio: HTMLAudioElement | null = null;
let activeUtterance: SpeechSynthesisUtterance | null = null;

function loadVoices(): void {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'speechSynthesis' in window) {
    cachedVoices = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices() || [];
  }
}

if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'speechSynthesis' in window) {
  loadVoices();
  if (window.speechSynthesis.onvoiceschanged !== undefined) {
    window.speechSynthesis.onvoiceschanged = loadVoices;
  }
}

function playOnlineTTSFallback(text: string): void {
  try {
    if (fallbackAudio) {
      fallbackAudio.pause();
      fallbackAudio = null;
    }
    fallbackAudio = new Audio(
      `https://translate.googleapis.com/translate_tts?client=gtx&ie=UTF-8&tl=ja&dt=t&q=${encodeURIComponent(text)}`
    );
    fallbackAudio.playbackRate = 0.95;
    fallbackAudio.play().catch(() => {});
  } catch {
    // Ignore audio fallback errors in headless or restricted environments
  }
}

export function speakJP(text: string): void {
  const cleanText = text.replace(/[()（）]/g, '').trim();
  if (!cleanText || typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) {
    playOnlineTTSFallback(cleanText);
    return;
  }
  loadVoices();
  const jpVoice = cachedVoices.find(
    v => v.lang && (v.lang.toLowerCase().includes('ja') || v.lang.toLowerCase().includes('jp'))
  );
  window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
  if (window.speechSynthesis.paused) {
    window.speechSynthesis.resume();
  }
  const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(cleanText);
  utterance.lang = 'ja-JP';
  utterance.rate = 0.9;
  if (jpVoice) utterance.voice = jpVoice;
  activeUtterance = utterance;
  let started = false;
  utterance.onstart = () => {
    started = true;
  };
  utterance.onerror = () => {
    if (!started) playOnlineTTSFallback(cleanText);
  };
  setTimeout(() => {
    if (activeUtterance !== utterance) return;
    window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
    if (!jpVoice) {
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (!started && !window.speechSynthesis.speaking) {
          playOnlineTTSFallback(cleanText);
        }
      }, 600);
    }
  }, 50);
}

name: Deploy ke GitHub Pages

on:
  push:
    branches: ['main']
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read
  pages: write
  id-token: write

concurrency:
  group: pages
  cancel-in-progress: true

jobs:
  build:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Checkout repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Setup Node.js
        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: '24'

      - name: Setup Java 21 (for Firestore Emulator)
        uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: 'temurin'
          java-version: '21'

      - name: Cache Firebase Emulator binaries
        uses: actions/cache@v4
        with:
          path: ~/.cache/firebase/emulators
          key: ${{ runner.os }}-firebase-emulators-v1

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: npm install --legacy-peer-deps --no-audit --no-fund

      - name: Type-check TypeScript
        run: npm run lint

      - name: Lint Firestore Security Rules
        run: npm run lint:rules

      - name: Run unit & security test suite
        run: npm test

      - name: Run Firestore Rules Emulator integration tests
        run: npm run test:rules:emulator

      - name: Build production bundle
        env:
          VITE_BASE_PATH: /kanji-n4-plus-plus/
        run: npm run build

      - name: Setup Pages
        uses: actions/configure-pages@v5

      - name: Upload Pages artifact
        uses: actions/upload-pages-artifact@v3
        with:
          path: ./dist

  deploy:
    environment:
      name: github-pages
      url: ${{ steps.deployment.outputs.page_url }}
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    needs: build
    steps:
      - name: Deploy to GitHub Pages
        id: deployment
        uses: actions/deploy-pages@v4
